Public officials or officers responsible for administrative or judicial police, who, not having the power to cause an unlawful detention to cease, have neglected or refused to record that which has been brought to their knowledge, and to report it to the competent authority, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months.

###### Art. 157

The directors, commanders, guards and concierges of houses of custody, arrest, (...) or punishment, who have received a prisoner without a legal order or warrant or without judgment; In force: 01-07-1999>
Those who have detained him or have refused to produce him to the police officer or to the bearer of his orders, without justifying the prohibition of the public prosecutor or the judge;
Those who have refused to exhibit their registers to the police officer;
Shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to two years and a fine of twenty-six francs to two hundred francs.

###### Art. 158

Shall be punished by a fine of two hundred [euros] to two thousand [euros], and may be sentenced to disqualification from the right to hold public functions, employments or offices, all judges, all officers of the public prosecution or of the

judicial police, all other public officers who, without the prescribed authorisations, have provoked, given, signed either a judgment against a minister, a senator or a representative, or an order or warrant tending to prosecute them or to have them impeached, or who, without the same authorisations, have given or signed the order or warrant to seize or arrest either a minister, or a senator or a representative, except, as regards the latter two, the case of flagrante delicto. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 159

Shall be punished by the same penalty the officers of the public prosecution, the judges or the public officers who have detained or caused to be detained a person outside the places determined by the government or by the public administration.

### TITRE III. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC TRUST

#### CHAPITRE I. - COUNTERFEITING

###### Art. 160. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 1°> Whoever has counterfeited gold or silver coins having legal tender in Belgium or abroad shall be punished (by imprisonment) of ten years to fifteen years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 41, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 161. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Whoever shall have altered the same coins shall be punished with (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 42, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 162

<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 2, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> Whoever shall have counterfeited coins of other metal having legal tender in Belgium or abroad or who shall have counterfeited coins denominated in euro shall be punished with imprisonment of five years to ten years.
The offender may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 163

<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 3, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> The alteration of the same coins shall be punished with imprisonment of five years to ten years.

###### Art. 164. (Repealed)

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 3°>

###### Art. 165. (Repealed)

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 3°>

###### Art. 166. (Repealed)

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 3°>

###### Art. 167. (Repealed)

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 3°>

###### Art. 168

Those who, in concert with them, shall have participated either in the issuance or attempted issuance of the said counterfeited or altered coins, or in their introduction into Belgian territory or the attempt of such introduction, shall be punished as counterfeiters or as their accomplices, according to the distinctions established in the preceding articles.

###### Art. 169

Whoever, without being guilty of the participation stated in the preceding article, shall have obtained, with knowledge, counterfeited or altered coins and shall have put them into circulation, or attempted to put them into circulation, shall be punished with imprisonment of one month [1 to five years]1.
[1 Whoever, with the purpose of putting them into circulation, shall have imported, exported, transported, received or obtained counterfeited or altered coins, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to five years.]1
(The attempt of the offence provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to six months.) <L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 4°>

(1)<L 2017-10-18/09, art. 3, 125; In force: 13-11-2017>

###### Art. 170

Whoever, having received as genuine counterfeited or altered coins, shall have put them back into circulation, after having verified or had verified their defects, shall be punished with a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.
(The attempt of the offence provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros].) <L 2001-04-04/39, art. 4, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 170bis. [1 Articles 160, 161, 162, 163, 168 and 169 apply indistinctly to coins which have already been issued and put into circulation as currency having legal tender and to coins which, although intended to be put into circulation as currency having legal tender, have not yet been issued.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2017-10-18/09, art. 4, 125; In force: 13-11-2017>

SPECIAL PROVISIONS.

###### Art. 171. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Those who shall be guilty of fraud in the selection of samples intended, in execution of the monetary law, for the verification of the fineness and weight of gold and silver coins, shall be sentenced (to imprisonment) of fifteen years to twenty years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 43, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 172. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Those who shall have committed this fraud in the selection of samples of coins of other metal shall be punished with (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 44, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

#### CHAPTER II

ON THE COUNTERFEITING OR FALSIFICATION OF PUBLIC SECURITIES, SHARES, BONDS, INTEREST COUPONS AND BANKNOTES AUTHORIZED BY LAW.

###### Art. 173

<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 5, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified bonds issued by the Public Treasury, interest coupons relating to these bonds, vouchers, cheques or transfers issued by the treasury, bearer notes issued by the Public Treasury or bearer banknotes having legal tender or whose issuance is authorized by a law or pursuant to a law or denominated in euro, shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years.
Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified bearer notes having legal tender or whose issuance is authorized by a law of a foreign country or pursuant to a provision having force of law therein, shall be punished with the same penalties.

###### Art. 174. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified either bearer bonds of the public debt of a foreign country, or interest coupons relating to these securities (...), shall be punished (with imprisonment) of ten years to fifteen years. <L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 6°> <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 45, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 175. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 46, 040; In force: 13-03-2003> Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified either shares, bonds or other securities, lawfully issued by provinces, municipalities, administrations or public establishments, under whatever denomination, by companies or private individuals, or interest or dividend coupons relating to these different securities, shall be punished with ten years to fifteen years of imprisonment, if the issuance took place in Belgium and with five years to ten years of the same penalty, if the issuance took place abroad.

###### Art. 176

Those who, in concert with them, shall have participated either in the issuance or attempted issuance of these counterfeited or falsified shares, bonds, coupons or notes, or in their introduction into Belgium, or the attempt of such introduction, shall be punished as counterfeiters or as their accomplices, according to the distinctions established in the preceding articles.

###### Art. 177

Whoever, without having been guilty of the participation stated in the preceding article, shall have obtained, with knowledge, these counterfeited or falsified shares, bonds, coupons, notes and shall have issued them or attempted to issue them, shall be punished with imprisonment of one year to five years.
[1 Whoever, with the purpose of putting them into circulation, shall have imported, exported, transported, received or obtained counterfeited or falsified notes shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years.]1
(The attempt of the offence provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to one year.) <L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 7°>

(1)<L 2017-10-18/09, art. 2, 125; In force: 13-11-2017>

Art. 177bis. [1 Articles 173, 176 and 177 apply indistinctly to notes which have already been issued and put into circulation as notes having legal tender and to notes which, although intended to be put into circulation as notes having legal tender, have not yet been issued.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2017-10-18/09, art. 6, 125; In force: 13-11-2017>

###### Art. 178

Any person who, having received as genuine counterfeit or falsified shares, bonds, coupons or notes, shall have put them back into circulation after having verified or had verified their defects, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
(The attempt of the offence provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to six months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] or by one of these penalties only.) <L 2001-04-04/39, art. 6, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

CHAPITRE IIbis. <Inserted by L 2001-12-10/31, art. 19; In force: 01-01-2002> - PROTECTION OF MONETARY SIGNS HAVING LEGAL TENDER.

Art. 178bis. <Inserted by L 2001-12-10/31, art. 20; In force: 01-01-2002> Any person who shall have issued a monetary sign intended to circulate among the public as a means of payment without having been authorised to do so by the competent authority, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of 50 to 10,000 EUR, or by one of these penalties only. ".

Art. 178ter. <Inserted by L 2001-12-10/31, art. 21; In force: 01-01-2002> Any person who shall have, knowingly, used a monetary sign having legal tender in Belgium or abroad as a medium for a message, advertising or otherwise, or who, knowingly, shall have made its use as a means of payment more difficult by deteriorating, soiling, overloading it or by rendering it unfit in any manner whatsoever, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of 26 to 1,000 EUR, or by one of these penalties only.

#### CHAPITRE III

COUNTERFEITING OR FALSIFICATION OF SEALS, STAMPS, HALLMARKS, MARKS, ETC.

###### Art. 179. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Shall be punished (by imprisonment) of ten years to fifteen years, those who shall have counterfeited the seal of the State, or made use of the counterfeit seal. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 47, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 180

<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 7, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> Shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years:
Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified either national stamps, or the hallmarks used to mark gold or silver materials.
Those who shall have made use of these counterfeit or falsified stamps or hallmarks. Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified the hallmarks, dies, squares or other objects or means intended for the manufacture of coins.
Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified the hallmarks, matrices, blocks, plates or other objects or means used for the manufacture, either of stamps, or of shares, bonds, interest or dividend coupons, or of bearer notes issued by the Public Treasury or of banknotes having legal tender or the issue of which has been authorised by a law or pursuant to a law or denominated in euro.

###### Art. 181

Shall be punished by the same penalty those who shall have knowingly exposed for sale papers or gold or silver materials marked with a counterfeit or falsified stamp or hallmark.

###### Art. 182

If the marks affixed by the assay office have been fraudulently applied to other objects, or if these marks or the impression of a stamp have been counterfeited without the use of a counterfeit hallmark or stamp, the guilty parties shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years.

###### Art. 183

Any person who, having obtained with knowledge paper marked with a counterfeit or falsified stamp, shall have made use of it, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months.

###### Art. 184

Shall be punished by three months to three years' imprisonment and may be sentenced to disqualification in accordance with Article 33:
Any person who shall have counterfeited coupons for the transport of persons or things, or who shall have made use of the counterfeit coupon;
Any person who shall have counterfeited the seal, stamp or mark either of any authority whatsoever, or of a private establishment, bank, industry or commerce, or of a private individual, or who shall have made use of the counterfeit seals, stamps or marks.
The attempt of these offences shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year.

###### Art. 185

Shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to three years, any person who, having unduly obtained the genuine seals, stamps, hallmarks and marks having one of the purposes expressed in Articles 179 and 180, shall have made an application or use thereof prejudicial to the rights and interests of the State, of any authority whatsoever or even of a private individual.
The attempt of this offence shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to one year.

Art. 185bis.<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 8, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year:
Those who, with fraudulent intent, [1 shall have received, obtained or possessed]1 either the counterfeit or falsified hallmarks, dies, squares or other objects or means, referred to in the penultimate paragraph of Article 180, or the genuine hallmarks, dies, squares or other objects or means intended for the manufacture of coins.
Those who, with fraudulent intent, shall have received or obtained either the counterfeit or falsified hallmarks, matrices, blocks, plates or other objects or means, intended by their nature for the counterfeiting or falsification of bearer notes issued by the Public Treasury or of banknotes having legal tender or the issue of which has been authorised by a law or pursuant to a law or denominated in euro, or the genuine hallmarks, matrices, blocks, plates or other objects or means intended for the manufacture of these notes.
[1 Those who, with fraudulent intent, shall have manufactured, received, possessed or obtained security devices used to protect coins or notes against counterfeiting.]1

(1)<L 2017-10-18/09, art. 7, 125; In force: 13-11-2017>

###### Art. 186

<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 9, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified the seals, stamps, hallmarks or marks having one of the purposes indicated in Articles 179 and 180 and belonging to foreign countries, or who shall have made use of these counterfeit or falsified seals, stamps, hallmarks or marks, shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years.
Shall be punished by the same penalty:
Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified the hallmarks, dies, squares or other objects or means intended for the manufacture of foreign coins.

Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified the hallmarks, matrices, blocks, plates or other objects or means used for the manufacture of bearer notes issued by a foreign State or of banknotes having legal tender therein or the issue of which is authorised by a law of a foreign country or by a provision having force of law therein.
Those who shall have counterfeited the seal, stamp or mark of any foreign authority whatsoever or who shall have made use of the counterfeit seals, stamps or marks, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and may be sentenced to disqualification in accordance with Article 33.
The attempt of this offence shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year.

###### Art. 187

(Whoever, having unlawfully obtained the genuine seals, stamps, punches or marks having one of the purposes indicated in articles 179 and 180 and belonging to foreign countries, shall have made use or application thereof prejudicial to the rights and interests of those countries, of any authority whatsoever or even of a private individual, shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to two years.) <L 22-06-1896, art. 2>
The attempt of this offence shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to six months.

Art. 187bis. <L 2001-04-04/39, art. 10, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> Shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to one year:
Whoever, with fraudulent intent, shall have received or obtained either the punches, dies, squares or other counterfeited or falsified objects or means referred to in article 186, paragraph 3, or the genuine punches, dies, squares or other objects or means intended for the manufacture of foreign coins.
Whoever, with fraudulent intent, shall have received or obtained either the punches, matrices, plates, blocks or other counterfeited or falsified objects or means referred to in article 186, paragraph 4, or the genuine punches, matrices, plates, blocks or other objects or means intended for the manufacture of bearer notes issued by a foreign State or of banknotes having legal tender therein or the issue of which is authorised by a law of a foreign country or by a provision having force of law therein.

###### Art. 188

Shall be punished with imprisonment from two months to three years and may be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33, those who shall have counterfeited postage stamps or other national or

foreign adhesive stamps, or who shall have exposed for sale or put into circulation counterfeited stamps.
The attempt to counterfeit shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to one year.

###### Art. 189

Those who, having obtained counterfeited postage stamps or other adhesive stamps, shall have made use thereof, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to one month.

###### Art. 190

Shall be punished with a fine from twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
Those who shall have caused to disappear, either from a postage stamp or other adhesive stamp, or from a coupon for the transport of persons or things, the mark indicating that they have already been used;
Those who shall have made use of a stamp or a coupon from which this mark has been caused to disappear.

Art. 190bis.<Inserted by L 1991-03-21/30, art. 150, 006; In force: 01-10-1992> The provisions of articles 188 to 190 apply not only to adhesive postage stamps, but also to those which are printed on documents issued by [1 bpost]1, as well as to postage values represented by machine impressions or by symbols approved by [1 bpost]1.

(1)<L 2010-12-13/07, art. 4, 081; In force: 17-01-2011>

###### Art. 191

Whoever shall have, either affixed, or caused to be affixed by addition, removal or by any alteration whatsoever, on manufactured objects, the name of a manufacturer other than the one who is the author thereof, or the trade name of a factory other than that of manufacture, shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to six months.
The same penalty shall be pronounced against any merchant, commission agent or dealer whatsoever who shall have knowingly exposed for sale or put into circulation objects marked with false or altered names.

[Particular provision] <L 2014-05-05/09, art. 12, 106; In force: 18-07-2014>

###### Art. 192

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 11°> Persons guilty of the offences
mentioned in articles 160 to 168, 169, paragraph 2, 171 to 176, 177, paragraph 2, in the last two paragraphs of article 180, in articles 185bis, 186, paragraphs 2 to 4, 187bis, 497, paragraph 2, and in article 497bis, paragraph 1, shall be exempt from penalties if, before any issue of counterfeited or altered coins or of counterfeited or falsified papers, and before any prosecution, they have given notice thereof and revealed the perpetrators to the authority.

Art. 192bis. <Inserted by L 2001-04-04/39, art. 11; In force: 03-07-2001> The acts qualified as offences concerning the euro, described in Chapters I, II and III of this title, are punished with the penalties provided for in the same provisions when they are committed with regard to coins or banknotes of Belgium or of a Member State of the European Union no longer having legal tender or the issue of which is no longer authorised following the introduction or adoption of fiduciary euro.

Art. 192ter. <Inserted by L 2005-01-10/38, art. 2, In force: 20-02-2005> § 1er. Whoever, having been sentenced to a term of imprisonment of more than five years, by a court of a Member State of the European Union for acts referred to in articles 160 to 170, 173, 176 to 178, 180, and 185 to 187bis, shall have committed again one of these acts, may be sentenced to imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years, if this act is a crime carrying imprisonment from five years to ten years. If this act is a crime carrying imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years, he may be sentenced to imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years. He shall be sentenced to imprisonment of at least seventeen years, if this act is a crime carrying imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years.

###### § 2

Whoever, having been sentenced to a term of imprisonment of more than five years, by a court of a Member State of the European Union for acts referred to in articles 160 to 170, 173, 176 to 178, 180, and 185 to 187bis, shall have committed again one of these acts, may be sentenced to a penalty double the maximum provided by law for this act, if this act is an offence

###### § 3

Whoever, having been sentenced to a term of imprisonment of at least one year, by a court of a Member State of the European Union for acts referred to in articles 160 to 170, 173, 176 to 178, 180, and 185 to 187bis, shall have committed again one of these acts, may be sentenced to a penalty double the maximum provided by law for this act, if this act is an offence.

#### CHAPITRE IV

FORGERY COMMITTED IN WRITINGS, IN COMPUTER DATA AND IN TELEGRAPHIC DISPATCHES. <L 2000-11-28/34, art. 2 , 028; In force: 13-02-2001>

###### Art. 193

Forgery committed in writings (,in computer data) or in telegraphic dispatches, with fraudulent intent or with intent to injure, shall be punished in accordance with the following articles. <L 2000-11-28/34, art. 3, 028; In force
: 13-02-2001>

##### SECTION I

FORGERY IN AUTHENTIC AND PUBLIC WRITINGS, IN COMMERCIAL OR BANKING WRITINGS AND IN PRIVATE WRITINGS.

###### Art. 194. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Any public official or officer who, in the exercise of his functions, shall have committed a forgery,
Either by false signatures,
Or by alteration of deeds, writings or signatures, Or by substitution of persons,
Or by writings made or inserted in registers or other public deeds, after their completion or closing,
Shall be punished with imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 48, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 195. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Shall be punished with imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years, any public official or officer who, in drawing up deeds of his ministry, shall have distorted the substance or circumstances thereof, <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 48, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>
Either by writing agreements other than those which would have been drawn up or dictated by the parties,
Or by recording as true facts which were not true.

###### Art. 196. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years) other persons who have committed a forgery in authentic and public writings, and all persons who have committed a forgery in commercial writings, bank writings or private writings, <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 49, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

Either by false signatures,
Or by counterfeiting or alteration of writings or signatures,
Or by fabrication of agreements, dispositions, obligations or discharges or by their subsequent insertion in the acts,
Or by addition or alteration of clauses, declarations or facts that these acts were intended to receive or record.

###### Art. 197

In all cases expressed in this section, whoever has made use of the false act or the false document shall be punished as if he were the author of the forgery.

##### SECTION II

FORGERIES COMMITTED IN PASSPORTS, WEAPONS PERMITS, BOOKLETS, TRAVEL WARRANTS AND CERTIFICATES.

###### Art. 198

Whoever has counterfeited or falsified a passport, (a document referred to by the law on weapons) or a booklet, or has made use of a counterfeited or falsified passport, (document referred to by the law on weapons) or booklet, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year. <L 2006-06-08/30, art. 39, 057; In force
: 09-06-2006>

###### Art. 199

Whoever has taken in a passport, (a document referred to by the law on weapons) or a booklet, a false name, or has participated as a witness in having these documents issued under the false name, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months. <L 2006-06-08/30, art. 39, 057; In force: 09-06-2006>

Art. 199bis. <L 14-08-1974, art. 15> Shall be punished by imprisonment of 8 days to 6 months and a fine of 26 [euros] to 500 [euros], or one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
1° whoever, with fraudulent intent, uses, transfers to a third party or accepts from a third party, a passport, a travel document, an identity card or a document in lieu thereof, as well as the forms used for their issuance, or who does not comply with the prohibitions and restrictions imposed therein;
2° whoever does not comply, within the fixed time limit, with a decision to withdraw a passport or document in lieu thereof, issued by the competent authority.

###### Art. 200. Shall be punished by one month to two years' imprisonment, whoever has

fabricated, counterfeited or falsified a travel warrant or has made use of a fabricated, counterfeited or falsified travel warrant.

###### Art. 201

Any person who has had a travel warrant issued by the public officer under a false name or by assuming a false capacity, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two years.

###### Art. 202

The public officer who has issued a passport, (a document referred to by the law on weapons), a booklet, a travel warrant to a person he did not know, without having had his name and capacity attested by two citizens known to him, shall be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L
2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2006-06-08/30, art. 39, 057; In
force: 09-06-2006>
If the public officer was aware of the false name or capacity, when he issued these documents, he shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years.
He shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years, if he was moved by gifts or promises.
In these last two cases, he may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 203

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year, any person who, to redeem himself or exempt another from a service legally due, or from any other obligation imposed by law, has fabricated a certificate of illness or infirmity, either under the name of a doctor, surgeon or other health officer, or under any name by falsely adding one of these capacities.

###### Art. 204

Any doctor, surgeon or other health officer who, to favour someone, has falsely certified illnesses or infirmities capable of exempting from a service legally due or from any other obligation imposed by law, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two years.
If he was moved by gifts or promises, he shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years; he may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 205

Whoever has fabricated, under the name of a public official or officer, a certificate attesting to good conduct, indigence or any other

circumstance capable of attracting the benevolence of the public authority or of private individuals towards the person designated therein, or of procuring positions, credit or assistance for him, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year.
If the certificate was fabricated under the name of a private individual, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months.

###### Art. 206

Those who have fabricated, under the name of a public official or officer, certificates of any nature capable of compromising public or private interests, shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years, and may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.
If the certificate was fabricated under the name of a private individual, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to one year.

###### Art. 207

Whoever has falsified a certificate, and whoever has made use of a falsified, false or fabricated certificate in the circumstances enumerated in articles 203, 204, 205 and 206, shall be punished by the penalties provided by these articles and according to the distinctions they establish.

###### Art. 208. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Any public official or officer who, in the exercise of his functions, has issued a false certificate, falsified a certificate, or made use of a false or falsified certificate, shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 49, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 209

Those who have participated as witnesses in having a false certificate issued by a public authority shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two years.
If they allowed themselves to be corrupted by gifts or promises, they shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years, and they may be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 210

<L 17-12-1963, art. 3> Whoever, charged by law or by virtue thereof with keeping a register or files concerning the accommodation of travellers, has knowingly registered these persons under false names or has falsified this register or these files in any other manner, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months.

SECTION IIbis. - Computer forgery. <inserted by L 2000-11-28/34, art. 4; In force: 13-02-2001>

Art. 210bis. <inserted by L 2000-11-28/34, art. 4; In force: 13-02-2001> § 1er. Whoever commits a forgery, by introducing into a computer system, by modifying or deleting data, which are stored, processed or transmitted by a computer system, or by modifying by any technological means the possible use of data in a computer system, and thereby modifies the legal scope of such data, is punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### § 2

Whoever makes use of the data thus obtained, while knowing that they are false, is punished as if he were the author of the forgery.

###### § 3

The attempt to commit the offence referred to in § 1er and is punished by imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to fifty thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 4

The penalties provided for in §§ 1 to 3 are doubled if an offence against one of these provisions is committed within five years following the pronouncement of a conviction for one of these offences or for one of the offences provided for in articles 259bis, 314bis, 504quater or in title IXbis.

##### SECTION III. - FORGERY COMMITTED IN TELEGRAPHIC DISPATCHES

###### Art. 211

Officials, employees and agents of a telegraphic service, who have committed forgery in the exercise of their functions, by fabricating or falsifying telegraphic dispatches, shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years.

###### Art. 212

Whoever has made use of the false dispatch shall be punished as if he were the author of the forgery.

[Provisions common to the five preceding chapters]<L 2014-05-05/09, art. 13, 106; In force: 18-07-2014>

###### Art. 213. The application of the penalties imposed against those who have made use of

counterfeit, fabricated or falsified coins, effects, coupons, notes, seals, stamps, punches, marks, telegraphic dispatches and writings, shall only take place insofar as these persons have made use of the false thing, with fraudulent intent or with intent to harm.

###### Art. 214

In the cases provided for [1 in chapters I to IV of this title]1 and for which no fine is specifically imposed, a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two thousand [euros] shall be pronounced. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

(1)<L 2014-05-05/09, art. 14, 106; In force: 18-07-2014>

#### CHAPITRE V. - FALSE TESTIMONY AND FALSE OATH

###### Art. 215. (See NOTE under TITLE)

False testimony in criminal matters, whether against the accused or in his favour, shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years).
<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 49, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 216

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 50, 040; In force: 13-03-2003> If the accused has been sentenced, either to detention of more than ten years, or to fixed-term imprisonment of more than ten years, the false witness who has testified against him shall undergo the penalty of imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years.
He shall undergo that of imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years, if the accused has been sentenced to life imprisonment.

###### Art. 217

The penalties imposed by the two preceding articles shall be reduced by one degree, in accordance with article 80, when persons called to court to give simple information have been guilty of false statements, whether against the accused or in his favour.

###### Art. 218

The person guilty of false testimony in correctional matters, whether against the defendant or in his favour, shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years.

###### Art. 219

The person guilty of false testimony in police matters, whether against the defendant or in his favour, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to one year.

###### Art. 220

False testimony in civil matters shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to three years.

###### Art. 221

The interpreter and the expert guilty of false statements, whether in criminal matters against the accused or in his favour, or in correctional or police matters, against the defendant or in his favour, or in civil matters, shall be punished as false witnesses, in accordance with articles 215, 216, 218, 219 and 220. The expert in criminal matters who has been heard without taking an oath shall be punished in accordance with article 217.

Art. 221bis. <L 10-10-1967, art. 132> Whoever, being charged with making the literal recording of an inquiry in civil matters, has knowingly omitted a question, statement, interpellation or answer, knowingly modifies its content by addition, deletion or alteration of words or sentences, distorted, removed or caused to disappear, in whole or in part the notes or devices used to collect the recorded words, makes use of these notes or devices, reproduces or discloses their content for purposes unrelated to the inquiry, or knowingly transcribes inaccurately the recorded words, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
He shall be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] if he has neglected to take useful precautions to avoid either the disappearance or distortion of the notes or devices used to collect the recorded words, or the use of these notes or devices, the reproduction or disclosure of their content for purposes unrelated to the inquiry. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 222

<L 10-10-1967, art. 133> In the cases provided for by articles 217, 218, 219, 220, 221 and 221bis, paragraph 1, the guilty party may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 223

<L 10-10-1967, art. 134> The person guilty of subornation of witnesses, experts, interpreters or persons referred to in article 221bis shall be liable to the same penalties as the false witness, according to the distinctions established by articles

215 to 222.

Art. 223bis. <L 10-10-1967, art. 135> Whoever, outside the case referred to in article 221bis, has distorted, removed or caused to disappear, in whole or in part, the notes or devices used to collect the words recorded during an inquiry in civil matters, or made use of these notes or devices, reproduced or disclosed their content for purposes unrelated to the inquiry, shall be punished by the penalties provided for in articles 220 and 222.

###### Art. 224

(The person guilty of false testimony, false statements or one of the acts referred to in articles 221bis and 223bis, who has received money, any reward or promises, shall be sentenced in addition, to a fine of fifty [euros] to three thousand [euros].) <L 10-10-1967, art. 136> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The same penalty shall be applied to the suborner, without prejudice to other penalties.

###### Art. 225

The preceding provisions relating to false statements are not applicable to children under the age of sixteen, nor to persons who are heard without taking an oath, by reason of the kinship or affinity that unites them to the accused or defendants, when these statements have been made in favour of the accused or defendants.

###### Art. 226

Whoever has been tendered or referred an oath in civil matters, and who has made a false oath, shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years, and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to ten thousand [euros]; he may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33. <L 2000-06-26/42, art.
2, In force: 01-01-2002>
(Is punished by the same penalties whoever has made a false oath during an opposition to seals or an inventory.) <L 10-10-1967, art. 167>

#### CHAPITRE VI. - USURPATION OF FUNCTIONS, TITLES OR NAMES

###### Art. 227

Whoever has interfered in public functions, civil or military, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years.

Art. 227bis. <L 07-05-1947, art. unique> § 1. Shall be punished by a fine of two

hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros], whoever, without right, has publicly attributed to himself the title or rank belonging, as holder or substitute, to persons participating in the exercise of a public power or exercising a public function, civil or military.) <L 01-02-1977, art. unique> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 2

Shall be punished by a fine of one hundred to five hundred [euros], reserve officers, pensioned officers, officers and reserve officers holding an honorary rank, who shall have publicly borne the title of officer or that of their rank without following it, as the case may be, with the mention "reserve", "pensioned", "honorary", "reserve, honorary". <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 227ter. <L 10-10-1967, art. 138> Whoever shall have publicly attributed to himself, either the title of lawyer, without being registered on the roll of the Bar or on a list of trainees, or the title of honorary lawyer, without being provided with the authorisation referred to in article 436 of the Judicial Code, shall be punished by a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

Art. 227quater. [1 Shall be punished by a fine of two hundred euros to twenty thousand euros:
1° whoever acts professionally as a mediator within the meaning of the Judicial Code, without appearing on the list of accredited mediators referred to in article 1727 and without being exempted from accreditation with the exception of whoever acts professionally as a mediator within the meaning of the Judicial Code in disputes between enterprises.
2° whoever, without being authorised to do so, publicly attributes to himself the professional title of accredited mediator and whoever bears a title or adds to the title he bears a mention that may give rise to confusion with the professional title of accredited mediator.
Shall be punished by the same penalty whoever provides his collaboration to a third party or lends him his name with the aim of removing him from the penalty that sanctions the unlawful bearing of the title of accredited mediator or the unlawful exercise of the profession of accredited mediator.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2018-06-18/03, art. 238, 130; In force: 12-07-2018>

###### Art. 228

Any person who shall have publicly worn a costume, a uniform, a decoration, a ribbon or other insignia of an order that does not belong to him, shall be punished by a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 229

The Belgian who shall have publicly worn the decoration, the ribbon or other insignia of a foreign order before having obtained authorisation from the King, shall be punished by a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 230

Shall be punished by a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros], whoever shall have publicly attributed to himself titles of nobility that do not belong to him. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 231

Whoever shall have publicly taken a name that does not belong to him shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months, and by a fine of twenty-five [euros] to three hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 232

Any official, any public officer who, in his acts, shall attribute to the persons named therein names or titles of nobility that do not belong to them, shall be punished, in case of connivance, by a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

### TITLE IV

(CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC ORDER, COMMITTED BY PERSONS EXERCISING A PUBLIC FUNCTION OR BY MINISTERS OF RELIGION IN THE EXERCISE OF THEIR MINISTRY.) <L 1999-02-10/39, art. 2,
023; In force: 02-04-1999>

#### CHAPTER I. - COALITION OF OFFICIALS

###### Art. 233

When measures contrary to the laws or to royal decrees shall have been concerted, either in a meeting of individuals or bodies holding some part of the public authority, or by deputation or correspondence between them, the guilty parties shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to six months.

###### Art. 234.(See NOTE under TITLE)

If, by one of the means expressed in the preceding article, measures have been concerted against the execution of a law or a royal decree, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to five years.
The guilty parties may, in addition, be sentenced to deprivation of the rights mentioned in the first three numbers of [1 article 31, paragraph 1]1.
If the concert took place between the civil authorities and the military bodies or their leaders, those who shall have provoked it shall be punished by detention of ten years to fifteen years; the others, by detention of five years to ten years.

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 11, 073; In force: 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 235. (See NOTE under TITLE)

In the case where the civil authorities shall have formed with the military bodies or their leaders a conspiracy threatening the security of the State, the instigators shall be punished by (detention of fifteen years to twenty years); the others, by detention of ten years to fifteen years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 51, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 236

Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and by a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], officials who, as a result of concert, shall have given their resignations with the aim of preventing or suspending, either the administration of justice, or the accomplishment of a lawful service. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
They may be sentenced, in addition, to deprivation of the right to hold public functions, employments or offices.

#### CHAPTER II. - ENCROACHMENT BY ADMINISTRATIVE AND JUDICIAL AUTHORITIES

###### Art. 237

Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years, by a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], and may be sentenced to deprivation, for five years to ten years, of the rights mentioned in the first three numbers of [1 article 31, paragraph 1]1: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
(The members and lay members of courts and tribunals, the officers of the judicial police) who shall have interfered in the exercise of the legislative power, either

by regulations containing legislative provisions, or by stopping or suspending the execution of one or more laws, or by deliberating on the question of whether these laws shall be executed;
(The members and lay members of courts and tribunals, the officers of the judicial police), who shall have exceeded their power by interfering in matters attributed to the administrative authorities, either by making regulations on these matters, or by forbidding the execution of orders emanating from the administration. <L 10-10-1967, art. 139>

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 12, 073; In force: 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 238. (The judges and the social or commercial lay judges)

who, when the administrative authority is at issue before them, shall nevertheless have proceeded to the judgment of the case, despite the conflict lawfully raised by that authority and before the decision of the Court of Cassation, shall each be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 10-10-1967, art. 139, § 2> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The officers of the public prosecution who shall have made submissions or given opinions for the said judgment shall be punished by the same penalty.

###### Art. 239

The governors, district commissioners, mayors and members of administrative bodies who shall have interfered in the exercise of the legislative power, as stated in paragraph 2 of article 237, or who shall have undertaken to make decrees tending to give orders or prohibitions of any kind to courts or tribunals, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and by a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.
They may, in addition, be sentenced to deprivation, for five years to ten years, of the rights mentioned in the first three numbers of [1 article 31, paragraph 1]1.

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 13, 073; In force: 15-04-2009>

#### CHAPTER III

(ON EMBEZZLEMENT, EXTORTION AND CONFLICT OF INTEREST COMMITTED BY PERSONS EXERCISING A PUBLIC FUNCTION). <L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; In force: 02-04-1999>

###### Art. 240

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> Any person exercising a public function who has embezzled public or private funds, effects in lieu thereof, documents, titles, deeds, movable effects which were in their hands either by virtue of, or by reason of, their function shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 241

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> Any person exercising a public function who has maliciously or fraudulently destroyed or suppressed deeds or titles of which they were the depositary in that capacity, which had been communicated to them or to which they had access by reason of their function shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 242

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> When documents or deeds of judicial proceedings, or other papers, registers, computer or magnetic media, deeds or effects contained in archives, court registries or public depositories, or entrusted to a public depositary in that capacity, have been removed or destroyed, the depositary guilty of negligence shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to six months and a fine of 100 [euros] to 10,000 [euros] or one of these penalties. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 243

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> Any person exercising a public function who has been guilty of extortion by ordering the collection of, demanding or receiving what they knew was not due or exceeded what was due for duties, taxes, contributions, monies, revenues or interest, for salaries or remuneration, shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros] or one of these penalties, and may be sentenced, in addition, to disqualification from the right to hold public functions, positions or offices, in accordance with article 33. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros], if the extortion was committed by means of violence or threats. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 244. (Repealed)

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; In force: 02-04-1999>

(...). <L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; In force: 02-04-1999>

###### Art. 245

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> Any person exercising a public function who, either directly or through the interposition of persons or by simulated acts, has taken or received any interest whatsoever in acts, adjudications, enterprises or administrations of which they had, at the time of the act, in whole or in part, the administration or supervision, or who, having the mission to order payment or to settle an affair, has taken any interest therein, shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros] or one of these penalties, and may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification from the right to hold public functions, positions or offices, in accordance with article 33. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The preceding provision shall not be applicable to one who could not, by reason of the circumstances, favor their private interests by their position, and who has acted openly.

#### CHAPTER IV. - (ON CORRUPTION OF PERSONS EXERCISING A PUBLIC FUNCTION)

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999>

###### Art. 246

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> § 1. Passive corruption is constituted by the fact that a person exercising a public function [1 solicits, accepts or receives]1, directly or through the interposition of persons, an offer, a promise or an advantage of any nature, for themselves or for a third party, to adopt one of the behaviors referred to in article 24

###### 7. §

2. Active corruption is constituted by the fact of proposing, directly or through the interposition of persons, to a person exercising a public function an offer, a promise or an advantage of any nature, for themselves or for a third party, so that they adopt one of the behaviors referred to in article 24

###### 7. §

3. Assimilated to a person exercising a public function within the meaning of this article is any person who has stood as a candidate for such a function, who makes it believed that they will exercise such a function, or who, by using false qualities,

makes it believed that they exercise such a function.

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 21, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 247

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> § 1. When the corruption has as its object the performance by the person exercising a public function of an act of their function, lawful but not subject to remuneration, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to one year and a fine of 100 [euros] to 10,000 [euros] or one of these penalties <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.
When, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in article 246, § 1, is followed by a proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 25,000 [euros] or one of these penalties <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### § 2

When the corruption has as its object the performance by the person exercising a public function of an unlawful act in the course of exercising their function or the abstention from performing an act which fell within the scope of their duties, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 25,000 [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> When, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in article
246, § 1, is followed by a proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.
In the case where the corrupted person has performed the unlawful act or has abstained from performing an act which fell within the scope of their duties, they shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 75,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### § 3

When the corruption has as its object the performance by the person exercising a public function of a crime or an offense in the course of exercising their function, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-
01-2002>.
When, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in article

246, § 1, is followed by a proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### § 4

Where the corruption concerns the use by the person exercising a public function of the real or supposed influence which that person has by virtue of their function, in order to obtain an act from a public authority or administration or the abstention from such an act, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine from 100 [euros] to 10,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.
Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in Article 246, § 1, is followed by a proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from 100 [euros] to 25,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.
If the corrupted person has actually used the influence which that person had by virtue of their function, they shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 248

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> Where the acts provided for in Articles 246 and 247, §§ 1 to 3, concern a police officer, a person holding the capacity of judicial police officer or a member of the public prosecution service, the maximum penalty is increased to double the maximum penalty provided for by Article 247 for the acts.

###### Art. 249

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> § 1. Where the corruption provided for in Article 246 concerns an arbitrator and relates to an act falling within their jurisdictional function, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine from 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in Article 246, § 1, is followed by a proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from 500 [euros] to 10,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### § 2

Where the corruption provided for in Article 246 concerns a lay judge or a juror and relates to an act falling within their jurisdictional function, the penalty shall be imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.
Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in Article 246, § 1, is followed by a proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### § 3

Where the corruption provided for in Article 246 concerns a judge and relates to an act falling within their jurisdictional function, the penalty shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.
Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in Article 246, § 1, is followed by a proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years and a fine from 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros]
<L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 250

[1 Where the corruption provided for by Articles 246 to 249 concerns a person exercising a public function in a foreign State or in an international public law organisation, the minimum of the fines is tripled and the maximum of the fines is quintupled.]1

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 22, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 251. (Repealed)

<L 2007-05-11/42, art. 6, 066; In force: 08-06/2007>

###### Art. 252

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> Without prejudice to the application of Articles 31 and 32, persons punished pursuant to the provisions of this chapter may also be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with Article 33.

###### Art. 253. (Repealed)

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999>

#### CHAPITRE V. - ABUSE OF AUTHORITY

###### Art. 254

Any public official, agent or representative of the government, of whatever status or rank, who has requisitioned or ordered, or caused to be requisitioned or ordered, the action or deployment of public force against the execution of a law or a royal decree, or against the collection of a legally established tax, or against the execution either of an order or warrant of justice, or of any other order emanating from the authority, shall be punished by imprisonment from one year to five years. The offender may be sentenced, in addition, to disqualification from the rights mentioned in the first three numbers of [1 Article 31, paragraph 1]1.

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 14, 073; In force: 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 255. (See NOTE under TITLE)

If such requisition or order has been carried out, the offender shall be sentenced to detention from five years to ten years.

###### Art. 256

If the orders or requisitions have been the direct cause of other crimes punishable by penalties stronger than those expressed in Articles 254 and 255, those stronger penalties shall be applied to the officials, agents or representatives guilty of having given the said orders or made the said requisitions. (Nevertheless, the penalty of life imprisonment shall be replaced, in this case, by that of imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years.) <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 52, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 257

Where a public official or officer, an administrator, agent or representative of the government or the police, an executor of warrants of justice or judgments, a commander in chief or subordinate commander of the public force, has, without legitimate reason, used or caused to be used violence against persons, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions, the minimum of the penalty imposed for these acts shall be increased in accordance with Article 266.

###### Art. 258

Any judge, any administrator or member of an administrative body, who, under any pretext whatsoever, even the silence or obscurity of the law, has denied to render the justice which they owe to the parties, shall be punished by a fine from two hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], and may be sentenced to disqualification from the right to hold public functions, employments or offices. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 259

Any commander, officer or non-commissioned officer of the public force who, after having been legally requisitioned by the civil authority, has refused to deploy the force placed under his command, shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to three months.

CHAPTER Vbis. - [1 Interception, taking cognisance and recording of communications not accessible to the public and data from a computer system]1

(1)	<L 2016-12-25/37, art. 29, 122; En vigueur : 27-01-2017>

Art. 259bis.<Inserted by L 1994-06-30/49, art. 1; En vigueur : 03-02-1995> § 1. Shall be punished [4 by imprisonment of six months to three years]4 and a fine of five hundred [euros] to twenty thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only, any public officer or official, depositary or agent of the public force who, in the course of exercising his functions, outside the cases provided for by law or without complying with the formalities it prescribes: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
1° [2 either, intentionally, by means of any device, intercepts or causes to be intercepted, takes cognisance or causes cognisance to be taken, records or causes to be recorded communications not accessible to the public, in which he does not take part, without the consent of all participants in such communications;]2
2° either, with the intention of committing one of the offences mentioned above, installs or causes to be installed any device;
3° [2 either, knowingly, possesses, reveals or discloses to another person the content of communications not accessible to the public or data from a computer system illegally intercepted or recorded, or of which he has taken cognisance illegally, or knowingly uses in any manner information obtained in this way.]

###### §

2. Shall be punished [4 by imprisonment of six months to five years]4 and a fine of five hundred [euros] to thirty thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only, any public officer or official, depositary or agent of the public force who, in the course of exercising his functions, outside the cases provided for by law or without complying with the formalities it prescribes, with fraudulent intent or with

intent to harm, uses a recording, legally made, [2 of communications not accessible to the public or data from a computer system]2. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
[§ 2bis. Shall be punished [4 by imprisonment of six months to three years]4 and a fine of five hundred euros to twenty thousand euros or by one of these penalties only, any public officer or official, depositary or agent of the public force who, in the course of exercising his functions, outside the cases provided for by law or without complying with the formalities it prescribes, unlawfully, possesses, produces, sells, obtains for use, imports, distributes or makes available in another form a device, including computer data, primarily designed or adapted to enable the commission of the offence provided for in § 1.]
<L 2006-05-15/46, art. 2, 1°, 059; En vigueur : 22-09-2006>

###### § 3

The attempt to commit one of the offences referred to in [§§ 1, 2 or 2bis] is punished as the offence itself. <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 2, 2°, 059; En vigueur : 22-09-2006>

###### § 4

The penalties [provided for in §§ 1 to 3] are doubled if an offence against one of these provisions is committed within five years from the pronouncement of a judgment or decision, having become res judicata, imposing a conviction by reason of one of these offences or one of the offences referred to [in Article 314bis, §§ 1 to 3]. <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 2, 3°, 059; En vigueur : 22-09-2006>

###### § 5. [3 ...]3

(1)	<L 2010-02-04/26, art. 38, 079; En vigueur : 01-09-2010>
(2)	<L 2016-12-25/37, art. 30, 122; En vigueur : 27-01-2017>
(3)	<L 2017-03-30/11, art. 83, 123; En vigueur : 08-05-2017>
(4)	<L 2017-07-06/24, art. 212, 124; En vigueur : 03-08-2017>

PROVISION COMMON TO THE PRECEDING CHAPTERS.

###### Art. 260

Where a public official or officer, a depositary or agent of the public force, has ordered or done any act contrary to a law or a royal decree, if he proves that he acted by order of his superiors, for matters within their competence and in respect of which hierarchical obedience was due to them, he shall be exempt from penalty, which shall, in that case, be applied only to the superiors who gave the order.

#### CHAPTER VI. - EXERCISE OF PUBLIC AUTHORITY ILLEGALLY ANTICIPATED OR PROLONGED

###### Art. 261

Any public official who has entered upon the exercise of his functions without having taken the oath prescribed by law shall be sentenced to a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 262

Any public official who has been revoked, dismissed, suspended or legally interdicted, who, after having had official knowledge thereof, has continued the exercise of his functions, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros].
Shall be punished by the same penalties any elective or temporary public official who has continued to exercise his functions after their legal cessation. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

#### CHAPTER VII. - CERTAIN OFFENCES RELATING TO THE KEEPING OF CIVIL STATUS RECORDS

###### Art. 263

<L 31-03-1987, art. 88> Shall be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], the civil status officer who has contravened one of the provisions [1 of Title 2 of Book I]1 of the Civil Code. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(1)	<L 2018-06-18/03, art. 99, 130; En vigueur : 31-03-2019>

###### Art. 264

<L 31-03-1987, art. 89> Shall be punished by a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], the civil status officer or the agent specially delegated by him who have contravened one of the provisions of [1 Article 29, § 1,]1 of the Civil Code.
<L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(1)	<L 2018-06-18/03, art. 100, 130; En vigueur : 31-03-2019>

###### Art. 265

<L 31-03-1987, art. 90> Shall be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], the civil status officer who has proceeded with the celebration of a marriage without having ascertained the required consents. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En

vigueur : 01-01-2002> SPECIAL PROVISION.

###### Art. 266. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Outside the case where the law specifically regulates the penalties incurred for crimes or offences committed by public officials or officers, those among them who have made themselves guilty of other crimes or other offences which they were charged with preventing, establishing, prosecuting or suppressing, shall be sentenced to the penalties attached to such crimes or offences, the minimum of which shall be doubled, if it concerns imprisonment, and increased by two years, if it concerns (imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years or a lesser term). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 53, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

#### CHAPTER VIII

OFFENCES COMMITTED BY MINISTERS OF RELIGION IN THE EXERCISE OF THEIR MINISTRY.

###### Art. 267

(Shall be punished by a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], any minister of a religion who proceeds with the nuptial blessing before the celebration of the civil marriage.) <L 03-08-1909, art. unique> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur
: 01-01-2002>
(This provision shall not be applicable where one of the persons who received the nuptial blessing was in danger of death, and any delay in this ceremony could have had the effect of rendering it impossible.) <L 03-08-1909, art. unique>
In case of a new offence of the same kind, he may, in addition, be sentenced to imprisonment of eight days to three months.

###### Art. 268

Ministers of religion who, in the exercise of their ministry, through speeches delivered in a public assembly, have directly attacked the government, a law, a royal decree or any other act of public authority, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

### TITLE V

CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC ORDER COMMITTED BY PRIVATE INDIVIDUALS.

#### CHAPTER I. - REBELLION

###### Art. 269

Any attack, any resistance with violence or threats against ministerial officers, rural or forestry guards, custodians or agents of the public force, persons responsible for the collection of taxes and contributions, bearers of writs of execution, customs officers, sequestrators, officers or agents of the administrative or judicial police, acting for the execution of laws, orders or ordinances of public authority, warrants or judgments, shall be qualified as rebellion.

###### Art. 270. (Repealed)

<L 13-10-1930, art. 31>

###### Art. 271

Rebellion committed by a single person, armed, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years; if it took place without arms, by imprisonment of eight days to six months.

###### Art. 272. (See NOTE under TITLE)

If the rebellion was committed by several persons, and as a result of prior concert, the rebels bearing arms shall be sentenced to (imprisonment of five years to ten years), and the others to imprisonment of one year to five years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 54, 040; In force
: 13-03-2003>
If the rebellion was not the result of prior concert, the armed offenders shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years, and the others by imprisonment of three months to two years.

###### Art. 273

In case of rebellion with a band or gathering, article 134 of this Code shall be applicable to rebels without functions or employment in the band, who withdrew at the first warning from the public authority, or even afterwards, if they were seized outside the place of rebellion, without new resistance and without arms.

###### Art. 274

In all cases where imprisonment is imposed for an act of rebellion, the offenders may be sentenced, in addition, to a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

The leaders of the rebellion and those who incited it may, in addition, be sentenced (...) to disqualification, in accordance with article 33. <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

#### CHAPTER II

INSULTS AND VIOLENCE AGAINST MINISTERS, MEMBERS OF THE LEGISLATIVE CHAMBERS, CUSTODIANS OF AUTHORITY OR OF THE PUBLIC FORCE.

###### Art. 275

(Any person who has insulted by acts, words, gestures or threats, a member of the Legislative Chambers in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his mandate, a Minister (, a member of the
[1 Constitutional Court]1) or a (magistrate of the administrative order or a member of the judicial order) or an officer of the public force on active service, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions, shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to six months and a fine of fifty to three hundred [euros].) <L 27-07-1934, art. 1> <L 10-10-1967, art. 139, § 5> <L 02-02-1984, art. 6> <L 2000-06-26/42, art.
2, In force: 01-01-2002>
If the insult took place at the sitting of one of the Chambers or at the hearing of a court or tribunal, the imprisonment shall be two months to two years, and the fine two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
Insults addressed to a member of the Chambers may not, except in the case of flagrant offence, be prosecuted except on the complaint of the insulted person or on the denunciation of the Chamber of which he is a member.

(1)	<L 2010-02-21/03, art. 6, 078; In force: 08-03-2010>

###### Art. 276

Insult by words, acts, gestures or threats, directed, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions, against a ministerial officer, an agent custodian of authority or of the public force, or against any other person having a public character, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one month and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 277

Insults committed against constituted bodies shall be punished in the same manner as insults committed against members of those bodies,

according to the distinctions established in the two preceding articles.

###### Art. 278

(Any person who has struck a member of the Legislative Chambers in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his mandate, a Minister, (a member of the [1Constitutional Court]1,) a magistrate or an officer of the public force on active service, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions, shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of fifty to five hundred [euros].) <L 27-07-1934, art. 2> <L 02-02-1984, art. 7> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
If the blows were struck at the sitting of one of the Chambers or at the hearing of a court or tribunal, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

(1)	<L 2010-02-21/03, art. 7, 078; In force: 08-03-2010>

###### Art. 279

If the blows struck caused bloodshed, wounds or illness, the offender shall be sentenced to imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to fifteen hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 279bis. <inserted by L 2006-12-20/41, art. 2; In force: 22-02-2007> Where blows struck without intention to cause death have nevertheless caused it, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of seven years to ten years.
He shall be punished by imprisonment of twelve years to fifteen years if he committed these acts of violence with premeditation.

###### Art. 280

[1 If the crime or offence was committed against a ministerial officer, an agent custodian of authority or of the public force, or against any other person having a public character, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions, the penalties shall be as follows:
1° in the cases referred to in article 398, paragraph 1, the penalties shall be imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of fifty euros to three hundred euros;
2° in the cases referred to in article 398, paragraph 2, the penalties shall be imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of fifty euros to

three hundred euros;
3° in the cases referred to in article 399, paragraph 1, the penalties shall be imprisonment of four months to four years and a fine of one hundred euros to five hundred euros;
4° in the cases referred to in article 399, paragraph 2, the penalties shall be imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one hundred euros to five hundred euros;
5° in the cases referred to in article 400, paragraph 1, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years;
6° in the cases referred to in article 400, paragraph 2, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;
7° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 1, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;
8° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 2, the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years.]1

(1)	<L 2010-03-08/08, art. 2, 080; In force: 09-04-2010>

###### Art. 281

<Repealed by L 2010-03-08/08, art. 3, 080; In force: 09-04-2010>

Art. 281bis.
<Repealed by L 2010-03-08/08, art. 4, 080; In force: 09-04-2010>

Art. 281ter.
<Repealed by L 2010-03-08/08, art. 5, 080; In force: 09-04-2010>

###### Art. 282

The penalties provided for in articles 275, 278 and 279 shall be applicable in the case where insult or assault has been committed against jurors by reason of their functions, or against witnesses by reason of their depositions.

#### CHAPTER III. - BREAKING OF SEALS

###### Art. 283

When seals affixed by order of the public authority have been broken, the custodians shall be punished, for simple negligence, by imprisonment of eight days to six months.

###### Art. 284

Those who have intentionally broken seals shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to two years, and if it is the custodian himself or the public official who ordered or carried out the affixing, he shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to three years.
The attempt of this offence shall be punished, in the first case of this article, by imprisonment of three months to one year, and, in the second case, by imprisonment of six months to two years.

###### Art. 285

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 55, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003> If the broken seals were affixed on papers or effects of an individual charged, accused or indicted for a crime carrying life imprisonment or life detention, imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years or detention of twenty years to thirty years, or of an individual sentenced to one of these penalties, the negligent custodian shall be punished by three months to one year of imprisonment.

###### Art. 286
