Whoever has intentionally broken seals affixed on papers or effects of the nature stated in the preceding article, shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to three years, and if it is the custodian himself or the public official who ordered the affixing, the guilty party shall be punished by imprisonment of two years to five years.
The attempt of this offence shall be punished, in the first case provided for by this article, by six months to two years of imprisonment, and, in the second case, by one year to three years of the same penalty.

###### Art. 287

If the breaking of seals is committed with violence against persons, the guilty party shall be punished by imprisonment of two years to five years.
The attempt of this breaking of seals shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years.

###### Art. 288

In the cases of articles 284, 286 and 287, the guilty party may, in addition, be sentenced to a fine of fifty [euros] to two thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

#### CHAPTER IV. - OBSTRUCTION OF THE EXECUTION OF PUBLIC WORKS

###### Art. 289

Whoever, by acts of violence, has opposed the execution of works ordered or authorized by the competent authority, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months.

###### Art. 290

Those who, by unlawful assembly and violence, acts of violence or threats, have opposed the execution of such works, shall be sentenced to imprisonment of three months to two years.
The leaders or instigators shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years.

###### Art. 291

In the cases provided for in the two preceding articles, the guilty parties may, in addition, be sentenced to a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

#### CHAPTER V. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES OF SUPPLIERS

###### Art. 292. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

Persons charged with supplies, contracts or management on behalf of the army or navy, who have voluntarily caused the service with which they are charged to fail, shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years) and a fine of two hundred [euros] to three thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 56, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>
The same penalties shall be applied to the agents of suppliers, if such agents have voluntarily caused the service to fail.

###### Art. 293. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

Public officials or agents appointed or salaried by the government, who have incited or assisted the guilty parties to cause the service to fail, shall be sentenced to imprisonment (of seven years to ten years), and to a fine of three hundred [euros] to three thousand [euros]. <L
2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 57, 040; En
vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 294

When the cessation of service is the result of negligence on the part of suppliers, their agents, public officials or agents, appointees or salaried employees of the government, the guilty parties shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to

one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 295

Even though the service has not failed, if deliveries or works have been voluntarily delayed, the guilty parties shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur :01-01-2002>
They shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], if the delay is the result of negligence.
<L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 296

In the various cases provided for by articles 294 and 295, § 2, prosecution may only be brought upon the denunciation of the minister concerned.

###### Art. 297

If there has been fraud as to the nature, quality or quantity of works or labour or things supplied, the guilty parties shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to ten thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
They may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 298

Public officials or agents appointed or salaried by the government, who have participated in such fraud, shall be punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to ten thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
They shall, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

#### CHAPTER VI

PUBLICATION OR DISTRIBUTION OF WRITINGS WITHOUT INDICATION OF THE NAME AND ADDRESS OF THE AUTHOR OR PRINTER.

###### Art. 299

Any person who has knowingly contributed to the publication or distribution of any printed matter in which the true indication of the name and address of the author or printer is not found, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art.
2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
However, imprisonment may not be pronounced when the printed matter, published

without the required indications, forms part of a publication whose origin is known by its prior appearance.

###### Art. 300

The following shall be exempted from the penalty provided for by the preceding article: Those who have disclosed the printer;
Criers, bill-posters, sellers or distributors who have disclosed the person from whom they obtained the printed writing.

#### CHAPTER VII

OFFENCES AGAINST LAWS AND REGULATIONS ON LOTTERIES, GAMING HOUSES AND PAWNSHOPS.

###### Art. 301

All operations offered to the public and intended to procure a gain by means of chance are deemed to be lotteries.

###### Art. 302

The authors, entrepreneurs, administrators, appointees or agents of lotteries not legally authorized, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of fifty [euros] to three thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
The movable objects put in the lottery, and those which are used or intended for its service, shall be confiscated.
When immovable property has been put in the lottery, confiscation shall not be pronounced; it shall be replaced by a fine of one hundred [euros] to ten thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 303

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one month and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
Those who have placed, hawked or distributed tickets for lotteries not legally authorized;
Those who, by notices, announcements, posters or by any other means of publication, have made known the existence of these lotteries or facilitated the issue of their tickets.
In all cases, the tickets, as well as the notices, announcements or posters, shall be seized and destroyed.

###### Art. 304

Shall be exempt from the penalties provided by the preceding article, town criers and

bill-posters who have made known the person from whom they hold the tickets or writings mentioned above.

###### Art. 305. (Repealed)

<L 1999-05-07/77, art. 73, 027; In force: 30-12-2000>

###### Art. 306

Those who, without legal authorization, have kept pawnbroking houses or pledge houses, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 307

Those who, having an authorization, have not kept a register in conformity with the regulations, containing consecutively, without any blank or interlineation, the sums or objects lent, the names, domiciles and professions of the borrowers, the nature, quality, value of the objects placed in pledge, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one month and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 308

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force
: 01-01-2002>
Individuals who have habitually taken goods to the offices of the pawnshop for others and for remuneration;
Those who have habitually purchased pawnshop receipts; Those who have transferred or purchased receipts from these establishments, evidencing loans on new merchandise.

#### CHAPTER VIII

OFFENCES RELATING TO INDUSTRY, COMMERCE AND PUBLIC AUCTIONS.

###### Art. 309

Whoever has maliciously or fraudulently communicated secrets of the factory in which he has been or is still employed, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of fifty [euros] to two thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 310. (Repealed)

<L 24-05-1921, art. unique>

###### Art. 311

Persons who, by any fraudulent means whatsoever, have caused the rise or fall of the price of foodstuffs or merchandise or of public papers and securities, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of three hundred [euros] to ten thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art.
2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 312

Any commander of military divisions, provinces or fortified towns and cities, any governor or district commissioner who has, within the extent of the places where he has the right to exercise his authority, engaged in similar manoeuvres or who has participated therein, either openly, or by simulated acts or by interposition of persons, shall incur, independently of the penalties pronounced by the preceding article, the disqualification from the rights set out in the first three numbers of [1 Article 31, paragraph 1]1.

(1)	<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 15, 073; In force: 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 313

Those who, by riotous assembly and by violence or threats, have disturbed public order in grain markets or grain halls, with the intent to provoke pillage or merely to force sellers to part with their foodstuffs at a price lower than that which would result from free competition, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years.
The leaders or instigators shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 314

<L 1993-12-24/37, art. 66, 011; In force: 01-05-1997> Persons who, in auctions for the ownership, usufruct or lease of movable or immovable property, an enterprise, a supply, an operation or any service, have obstructed or disturbed the freedom of bids or tenders, by violence or threats, by gifts or promises or by any other fraudulent means, shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to six months and a fine of one hundred [euros] to three thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

CHAPTER VIIIbis. - [1 Offences relating to the secrecy of communications not accessible to the public and data of a computer system]1

(1)	<L 2016-12-25/37, art. 31, 122; In force: 27-01-2017>

Art. 314bis.<Inserted by L 1994-06-30/49, art. 2; In force: 03-02-1995> § 1. Shall be punished [2 by imprisonment of six months to two years]2 and a fine of two hundred [euros] to ten thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only, whoever: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
1° [1 either, intentionally, by means of any apparatus whatsoever, intercepts or causes to be intercepted, takes cognizance or causes cognizance to be taken, records or causes to be recorded communications not accessible to the public, in which he does not take part, without the consent of all the participants in these communications;]1
2° either, with the intention of committing one of the offences mentioned above, installs or causes to be installed any apparatus whatsoever.

###### § 2

[1 Shall be punished [2 by imprisonment of six months to three years]2 and a fine of five hundred euros to twenty thousand euros or by one of these penalties only, whoever knowingly possesses, reveals or discloses to another person the content of communications not accessible to the public or data of a computer system illegally intercepted or recorded, or of which he has taken cognizance illegally, or knowingly uses in any manner whatsoever information obtained in this way.
Shall be punished by the same penalties whoever, with fraudulent intent or with intent to harm, uses a recording, legally made, of communications not accessible to the public or data of a computer system.]1
(§ 2bis. Shall be punished [2 by imprisonment of six months to two years]2 and a fine of two hundred euros to ten thousand euros or by one of these penalties only, whoever, unlawfully, possesses, produces, sells, obtains for use, imports, distributes or makes available in another form a device, including computer data, primarily designed or adapted to permit the commission of the offence provided for in § 1.) <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 3, 1°, 059; In force: 22-09-2006>

###### § 3

The attempt to commit one of the offences referred to in (§§ 1, 2 or 2bis) is punished as the offence itself. <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 3, 2°, 059; In force: 22-09-2006>

###### § 4

The penalties (provided for in §§ 1 to 3) are doubled if an offence against one of

these provisions is committed within five years from the pronouncement of a judgment or decision, having acquired the force of res judicata, imposing a conviction by reason of one of these offences or one of the offences referred to (in Article 259bis, §§ 1 to 3). <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 3, 3°, 059; In force: 22-09-2006>

(1)	<L 2016-12-25/37, art. 32, 122; In force: 27-01-2017>
(2)	<L 2017-07-06/24, art. 213, 124; In force: 03-08-2017>

#### CHAPTER IX. - CONCERNING CERTAIN OTHER OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC ORDER

##### SECTION I. - OFFENCES AGAINST THE LAWS ON BURIALS

###### Art. 315

Shall be punished by eight days to two months' imprisonment, or by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
Those who, without prior authorisation from the public officer, shall have proceeded or caused to proceed with a burial.
Those who shall have contravened, in any manner whatsoever, the regulations relating to places of burial and precipitate burials.

##### SECTION II. - (OBSTRUCTIONS TO THE EXERCISE OF THE JUDICIAL FUNCTION)

<L 10-10-1967, art. 140>

###### Art. 316

<L 10-10-1967, art. 140> Is punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] whoever abstains from responding to inquiries ordered by the authority with a view to establishing the lists of jurors or who, in order to be exempted from fulfilling the function of juror, makes an inaccurate declaration. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 316bis. <L 10-10-1967, art. 140> Is punished by a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
1° the non-exempted juror who does not appear at the assize court on the day and at the time indicated for the opening of the proceedings, upon the summons that has been served on him or upon the notice that he has received;
2° the juror who, after having complied with the summons or notice, withdraws without the authorisation of the president before the expiration of his functions.

###### Art. 317. (Repealed)

<L 03-03-1933, art. 28>

###### Art. 318. (Repealed)

<L 03-03-1933, art. 28>

##### SECTION III. - OFFENCES RELATING TO EPIZOOTICS

###### Art. 319. (Repealed)

<L 24-03-1987, art. 32, § 1>

###### Art. 320. (Repealed)

<L 24-03-1987, art. 32, § 1>

###### Art. 321. (Repealed)

<L 24-03-1987, art. 32, § 1>

### TITLE VI. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC SAFETY

#### CHAPTER I

(ASSOCIATION FORMED FOR THE PURPOSE OF COMMITTING ATTACKS AGAINST PERSONS OR PROPERTY and CRIMINAL ORGANISATION). <L 1999-01-10/49, art. 2, 022; In force: 08-03-1999>

###### Art. 322

Any association formed for the purpose of committing attacks against persons or property is a crime or an offence, which exists by the mere fact of the organisation of the gang.

###### Art. 323

(If the association has had as its purpose the perpetration of crimes carrying the penalty of imprisonment for life or imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years or a higher term, the instigators of this association, the leaders of this gang and those who shall have exercised any command therein, shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years.) <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 58, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>
They shall be punished by imprisonment of two years to five years, if the association was formed to commit other crimes, and by imprisonment of six months to three years, if the association was formed to commit offences.

###### Art. 324

All other individuals forming part of the association and those who shall have knowingly and voluntarily supplied to the gang or its divisions weapons, ammunition, instruments of crime, lodgings, retreat or place of meeting, shall be

punished:
In the first case provided for by the preceding article, by imprisonment of six months to five years;
In the second case, by imprisonment of two months to three years; And in the third, by imprisonment of one month to two years.

Art. 324bis. (inserted by <L 1999-01-10/49, art. 3, In force: 08-03-1999>) Constitutes a criminal organisation the structured association of more than two persons, established over time, with a view to committing in a concerted manner, crimes and offences punishable by imprisonment of three years or a more severe penalty, in order to obtain, directly or indirectly, patrimonial advantages, (...). <L 2005-08-10/61, art. 4, 053; In force: 12-09-2005>
An organisation whose real object is exclusively of a political, trade union, philanthropic, philosophical or religious nature or which exclusively pursues any other legitimate aim cannot, as such, be considered as a criminal organisation within the meaning of paragraph 1.

Art. 324ter. (inserted by <L 1999-01-10/49, art. 3, In force: 08-03-1999>)

###### § 1

er. (Where the criminal organisation uses intimidation, threat, violence, fraudulent manoeuvres or corruption or resorts to commercial or other structures to conceal or facilitate the commission of offences, any person who, knowingly and voluntarily, forms part thereof, is punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and by a fine of one hundred euros to five thousand euros or by one of these penalties only, even if he does not intend to commit an offence within the framework of this organisation nor to associate himself therewith in one of the ways provided for by articles 66 to 69.) <L 2005-08-10/61, art. 5, 053; In force: 12-09-2005>

###### § 2

Any person who participates in the preparation or realisation of any lawful activity of this criminal organisation, while knowing that his participation contributes to the objectives thereof, as provided for in article 324bis, is punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and by a fine of one hundred [euros] to five thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 3

Any person who participates in any decision-making within the framework of the activities of the criminal organisation, while knowing that his participation contributes to the objectives thereof, as provided for in article 324bis, is

punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years and by a fine of five hundred [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 4

Any leader of the criminal organisation is punished by imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years and by a fine of one thousand [euros] to two hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 325

The convicted offenders, by virtue of articles 323(, 324 and 324ter), to the penalty of imprisonment, may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33 (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. 32> <L 1999-01-10/49, art. 4,
022; In force: 08-03-1999>

###### Art. 326

Shall be exempted from the penalties pronounced by this chapter, those of the offenders who, before any attempt at crimes or offences forming the object of the association and before any proceedings commenced shall have revealed to the authority the existence of these gangs and the names of their commanders in chief or subordinate.
(Paragraph 2 repealed) <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

#### CHAPTER II

(THREATS OF ATTACK AGAINST PERSONS OR AGAINST PROPERTY, AND FALSE INFORMATION RELATING TO SERIOUS ATTACKS). <L 04-07-1972, art. 5>

###### Art. 327

<L 04-07-1972, art. 1> Whoever shall have, either verbally, or by anonymous or signed writing, with order or under condition, threatened an attack against persons or property, punishable by a criminal penalty, shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and by a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The threat by anonymous or signed writing of an attack against persons or property punishable by a criminal penalty, not accompanied by order or condition, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years and by a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 328

<L 04-07-1972, art. 2> Anyone who, either verbally or by anonymous or signed writing, (or by any act whatsoever,) knowingly gives

false information concerning the existence of a danger of an attack against persons or property punishable by a criminal penalty, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2003-04-04/59, art. 2, 042; In force: 15-05-2003>

Art. 328bis. <Inserted by L 2003-04-04/59, art. 3; In force: 15-05-2003> Anyone who has disseminated, by any means whatsoever, substances which, presenting no danger in themselves, give the impression of being dangerous, and which he knows or should know may inspire serious fears of an attack against persons or property, punishable by imprisonment of at least two years, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of fifty euros to three hundred euros.

###### Art. 329

<L 04-07-1972, art. 3> The threat by gestures or emblems of an attack against persons or property, punishable by a criminal penalty, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 330

<L 04-07-1972, art. 4> The threat, made either verbally or by anonymous or signed writing, with order or under condition, of an attack against persons or property, punishable by imprisonment of at least three months, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force
: 01-01-2002>

Art. 330bis. [1 In the cases referred to in articles 327 to 330, the minimum of the penalties provided by these articles shall be doubled when the person to whom the threats of attack are addressed or to whom false information relating to an attack is given is a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts.]1

(1)	<Inserted by L 2011-11-26/19, art. 3, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 331. In the cases provided for by article 327, the offender may, in addition, be

sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33 (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

Art. 331bis.(See NOTE under TITLE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 1> Is punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years): <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 59, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>
1° [1 anyone who, with the aim of causing death or serious injury to others or substantial damage to property or the environment, threatens to use radioactive materials or devices or threatens to commit an act directed against a nuclear installation or to disrupt the operation of such an installation;]1 2° anyone who threatens to commit a theft of nuclear materials in order to compel a natural or legal person, an international organization or a State to do or refrain from doing an act.
(3° anyone who threatens to use biological or chemical weapons or products to commit an attack against persons, property, legal persons, international organizations or a State.) <L 2003-04-04/59, art. 4, 042; In force: 15-05-2003>

(1)	<L 2013-05-23/09, art. 2, 093; In force: 16-06-2013>

#### CHAPTER III. - ESCAPE OF DETAINEES

###### Art. 332

In case of escape of detainees, the persons responsible for their conduct or custody shall be punished as follows.

###### Art. 333

<L 29-08-1945, art. 1> If the escapee was being prosecuted or convicted for an offence, if he was a prisoner of war, or if he was detained at the disposal of the Minister of Justice, these persons responsible shall be punished, in case of negligence, by imprisonment of eight days to three months, and, in case of connivance, by imprisonment of six months to two years.
The same penalties shall be applicable in the case of escape of any individual interned by application of the social defence law of 9 April 1930.

###### Art. 334

If the escapee was being prosecuted or convicted for a crime, or if he was arrested under the law on extraditions [1 under the law on extraditions]1 , these persons responsible shall undergo imprisonment of fifteen days to one

year, in case of negligence, and imprisonment of one year to five years, in case of connivance.

(1)	<L 2015-07-20/19, art. 2, 113; In force: 05-09-2015>

###### Art. 335

Those who, not being charged with the custody or conduct of the detainee, have procured or facilitated his escape, shall be punished, in the case of article 333, by imprisonment of fifteen days to one year, and, in the case of article 334, by imprisonment of three months to two years.
(Paragraph 2 repealed) <L 1993-06-29/31, art. 1, 010; In force: 05-09-1993>

###### Art. 336

If the escape took place or was attempted with violence, threats or prison breaking, the penalties against those who have favoured it by providing instruments suitable for carrying it out shall be:
In the circumstances set out in article 333, imprisonment of two years to five years against the persons responsible, and of three months to two years against other persons;
In the circumstances set out in article 334, (imprisonment of five years to ten years) against the persons responsible, and imprisonment of six months to three years against other persons. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 60, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 337. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 61, 040; In force: 13-03-2003> If the escape took place or was attempted with violence, threats or prison breaking, the penalties against those who have favoured it by transmission of weapons shall be:
In the circumstances set out in article 333, imprisonment of five years to ten years against the persons responsible, and imprisonment of two years to five years against other persons.
In the circumstances set out in article 334, imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years against the persons responsible, imprisonment of five years to ten years against other persons.

#### CHAPTER IV. - BREACH OF BANISHMENT AND CERTAIN CONCEALMENTS

###### Art. 338. (Repealed)

<L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 339

Those who have concealed or caused to be concealed persons whom they knew to be prosecuted or convicted for a crime, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2,In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 340

Anyone who has concealed or caused to be concealed, hidden or caused to be hidden the corpse of a person who has been murdered or died as a result of blows or wounds, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years, and a fine of fifty [euros] to six hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 341

Excepted from the two preceding provisions are the ascendants or descendants, spouses even if divorced, brothers or sisters, and relatives in the same degrees of the concealed criminals, of the perpetrators or accomplices of the homicide, blows or wounds.

#### CHAPTER V

OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC SAFETY COMMITTED BY VAGRANTS OR BEGGARS.

###### Art. 342. (Repealed)

<L 1993-01-12/34, art. 28, 007; In force: 01-03-1993>

###### Art. 343. (Repealed)

<L 1993-01-12/34, art. 28, 007; In force: 01-03-1993>

<L 1993-01-12/34, art. 28, 007; In force: 01-03-1993>

###### Art. 345. (Repealed)

<L 1993-01-12/34, art. 28, 007; In force: 01-03-1993>

###### Art. 346. (Repealed)

<L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 347. (Repealed)

<L 1993-01-12/34, art. 28, 007; En vigueur : 01-03-1993>

TITRE VIbis. - (CRIMES RELATING TO HOSTAGE-TAKING). <L 02-07-1975, art. 1>

Art. 347bis.<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 4, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1er. The arrest, detention or abduction of persons in order to secure the performance of an order or condition, such as

preparing or facilitating the commission of a crime or offence, favouring flight, escape, obtaining release or ensuring impunity for the perpetrators or accomplices of a crime or offence, shall constitute hostage-taking.

###### § 2. Hostage-taking shall be punished by imprisonment of twenty to thirty years

[1 The penalty shall be life imprisonment if the person taken hostage is a minor or a person whose vulnerable situation by reason of age, pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental disability was apparent or known to the perpetrator.]

###### §

3. Except in the cases referred to in § 4, the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years if, within five days of the arrest, detention or abduction, the person taken hostage has been voluntarily released without the order or condition having been performed.

###### § 4. The penalty shall be life imprisonment in the following cases

1° if the arrest, detention or abduction of the person taken hostage has caused either an apparently incurable illness, or [2 incapacity for personal work of more than four months]2, or the complete loss of use of an organ, or serious mutilation, or death;
2° (if the person taken hostage has been subjected to the acts referred to in Article 417ter, first paragraph.) <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 2, 036; En vigueur : 24-08-2002>

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 4, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>
(2)	<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

### TITRE VII

CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST THE ORDER OF FAMILIES AND AGAINST PUBLIC MORALITY.

#### CHAPITRE I. - ABORTION

###### Art. 348. (See NOTE under TITRE)

<L 1990-04-03/30, art. 1, 002; En vigueur : 15-04-1990> Any person who, whether a doctor or not, by any means whatsoever, has intentionally caused a woman to miscarry without her consent, shall be punished by (imprisonment of five to ten years). If the means employed have failed to take effect, Article 52 shall apply. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 62, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 349

Where the miscarriage has been caused by violence intentionally inflicted, but without intent to produce it, the offender shall be punished by

imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2,En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
If the violence was committed with premeditation or with knowledge of the woman's condition, the imprisonment shall be six months to three years, and the fine fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

#### CHAPITRE III

CRIMES AND OFFENCES TENDING TO PREVENT OR DESTROY PROOF OF THE CIVIL STATUS OF A CHILD.

###### Art. 361

<L 30-03-1984, art. 4> Any person who, being required to declare the birth of a child by virtue of [1 Article 43, § 1er,]1 of the Civil Code, has not made the declaration [1 as provided in that Article]1, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of 26 [euros] to 200 [euros], or by one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
1° any person who, being required to declare the birth of a child by virtue of [1 Article 43, § 1er,]1 of the Civil Code, has not made the declaration [1 as provided in that Article]1;
2° any person who, being required to inform the registrar of a birth by virtue of [1 Article 42,]1 of the Civil Code, has not given notice in accordance with those provisions.

(1)	<L 2018-06-18/03, art. 101, 130; En vigueur : 31-03-2019>

###### Art. 362

[1 Any person who, having found a newborn child, has not immediately informed the public emergency services thereof, as prescribed by Article 45 of the Civil Code, shall be punished by the penalties referred to in the preceding Article.]1 This provision shall not apply to any person who has agreed to take charge of the child and who has made a declaration to that effect before the municipal authority of the place where the child was found.

(1)	<L 2018-06-18/03, art. 102, 130; En vigueur : 31-03-2019>

###### Art. 363

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 5, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Any person who substitutes one child for another or who attributes to a woman a child of whom she has not given birth shall be punished by imprisonment of five to ten years.
Any person who destroys proof of the civil status of a child or prevents its establishment shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years.
The same penalty shall apply to those who have given the instruction to commit the acts mentioned in the preceding paragraphs, if that instruction has been carried out.

#### CHAPITRE V. - [1 Voyeurism, indecent assault and rape]1

(1)	<L 2016-02-01/09, art. 7, 115; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 371

/1. [1 Shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to five years anyone who has:
1° observed or caused to be observed a person or made or caused to be made a visual or audio recording of them,
-	directly or by technical or other means,
-	without the consent of that person or without their knowledge,
-	while that person was nude or engaged in explicit sexual activity, and
-	while that person was in circumstances where they could reasonably consider that their privacy would not be violated;
2° shown, made accessible or disseminated the visual or audio recording of a person who is nude or engaged in explicit sexual activity, without their consent or without their knowledge, even if that person consented to its creation.
If these acts have been committed on the person or with the assistance of the person of a

minor over sixteen years of age, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years.
The penalty shall be imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years, if the minor was under sixteen years of age.
Voyeurism exists as soon as there is commencement of execution.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2016-02-01/09, art. 8, 115; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 372.(See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 15-05-1912, art. 48> Any indecent assault committed without violence or threats on the person or with the assistance of the person of a child of either sex, under sixteen years of age, shall be punished by imprisonment (from five years to ten years). <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 6, 029; In force
: 27-03-2001>
(Shall be punished by imprisonment from ten to fifteen years the indecent assault committed, without violence or threats, by any ascendant or adopter on the person or with the assistance of the person of a minor, even over sixteen years of age [1 ...]1. (The same penalty shall be applied if the offender is either the brother or sister of the minor victim or any person who occupies a similar position within the family, or any person habitually or occasionally cohabiting with them and who has authority over them.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 6, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

(1)<L 2016-05-31/02, art. 3, 118; In force: 18-06-2016>

Art. 372bis. (Repealed) <L 18-06-1985, art. 1>

###### Art. 373.(See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 15-05-1912, art. 49> [1 Shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to five years, the indecent assault committed on persons or with the assistance of persons of either sex, with violence, coercion, threat, surprise or deceit, or which was made possible by reason of an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency of the victim.]1
If the assault was committed on the person [1 or with the assistance of the person]1 of a minor over sixteen years of age, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment (from five years to ten years). <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 7, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
The penalty shall be (imprisonment) from ten to fifteen years, if the minor was under sixteen years of age. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 7, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

(1)<L 2016-02-01/09, art. 9, 115; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 374. The assault exists as soon as there is commencement of execution

###### Art. 375.(See NOTE under TITLE)

(Any act of sexual penetration, of whatever nature and by whatever means, committed on a person who does not consent thereto, constitutes the crime of rape.
There is no consent in particular when the act was imposed by violence, coercion [1 , threat, surprise]1 or deceit, or was made possible by reason of an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency of the victim.) <L 04-07-1989, art. 1, 1°>
(Whoever has committed the crime of rape shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 8, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
(If the crime was committed on the person of a minor over sixteen years of age, the offender shall be punished by the penalty of imprisonment from ten to fifteen years.)
<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 8, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
(If the crime was committed on the person of a child over fourteen years of age and under sixteen years of age, the offender shall be punished by the penalty of imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 8, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
(Is deemed rape with violence any act of sexual penetration, of whatever nature and by whatever means, committed on the person of a child who has not reached the age of fourteen years. In this case, the penalty shall be imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 8, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
(It shall be imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years if the child was under ten years of age.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 8, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

(1)	<L 2016-02-01/09, art. 10, 115; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 376.(See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 04-07-1989, art. 2> If the rape or indecent assault has caused the death of the person on whom it was committed, the offender shall be punished (by imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years). <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 9, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
(If the rape or indecent assault was preceded or accompanied by the acts referred to

in article 417ter, paragraph one, or by sequestration, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years.) <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 3, 036; In force
: 24-08-2002>
If the rape or indecent assault was committed either on a person [1 whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts]1, or under threat of a weapon or an object resembling one, the offender shall be punished (by imprisonment) from ten to fifteen years. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 9, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 5, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

Art. 377ter. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 11, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> In the cases provided for by this chapter, the offenders shall be sentenced to deprivation of the rights set out in [1 Article 31, paragraph 1]1.

The application of this prohibition shall be in accordance with Article 389.]2

publication and dissemination by book, press, cinematography, radio, television or by any other means, of texts, drawings, photographs, images of any kind or sound messages likely to reveal the identity of the victim of an offence referred to in this chapter are prohibited, unless the victim has given written consent or the public prosecutor or the examining magistrate has given consent for the purposes of

information or investigation.
Offences against this article are punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of three hundred [euros] to three thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

#### CHAPTER VI. - (CORRUPTION OF YOUTH AND PROSTITUTION)

<L 26-05-1914, art. 4>

###### Art. 379. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 1995-04-13/32, art. 2, 014; In force: 05-05-1995> Whoever shall have committed an offence against morals by inciting, favouring or facilitating, in order to satisfy the passions of others, the debauchery, corruption or prostitution of a minor of either sex, shall be punished by imprisonment (of five to ten years) and a fine of five hundred [euros] to twenty-five thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 13, 029; In force
: 27-03-2001>
The offender shall be punished (by imprisonment) of ten to fifteen years and a fine of five hundred [euros] to fifty thousand [euros] if the minor has not reached the age of sixteen years. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 13, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
(The penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years and a fine of one thousand [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros], if the minor has not reached the age of fourteen years.) <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 13, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 380.(See NOTE under TITLE)

(Formerly art. 380bis.)

###### § 1

Shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred [euros] to twenty-five thousand [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
1° anyone who, to satisfy the passions of others, has recruited, enticed, diverted or detained, for the purpose of debauchery or prostitution, even with their consent, an adult person (...);
2° anyone who has kept a house of debauchery or prostitution;
3° anyone who has sold, rented or made available for the purposes of prostitution rooms or any other premises with the aim of realizing an abnormal profit;
4° anyone who has, in any manner whatsoever, exploited the debauchery or prostitution of others.

###### § 2

The attempt to commit the offences referred to in § 1 shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 3

Shall be punished (by imprisonment) of ten years to fifteen years and a fine of five hundred [euros] to fifty thousand [euros], the offences referred to in § 1, insofar as their perpetrator: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
1° makes use, directly or indirectly, of fraudulent manoeuvres, violence, threats or any form of coercion whatsoever;
2° or abuses the [1 situation of vulnerability in which a person finds themselves due to their illegal or precarious administrative situation, their age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency]

###### 1. §

4. Shall be punished (by imprisonment) of ten years to fifteen years and a fine of one thousand [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
1° anyone who, to satisfy the passions of others, has recruited, enticed, diverted or detained, either directly or through an intermediary, a minor (...), even with their consent, for the purpose of debauchery or prostitution; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In
force: 27-03-2001>
2° anyone who has kept, either directly or through an intermediary, a house of debauchery or prostitution where minors engage in prostitution or debauchery;
3° anyone who has sold, rented or made available to a minor, for the purposes of debauchery or prostitution, rooms or any other premises with the aim of realizing an abnormal profit;
4° anyone who has exploited, in any manner whatsoever, the debauchery or prostitution of a minor (...). <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
(5° anyone who has obtained by the delivery, offer or promise of a material or financial advantage, the debauchery or prostitution of a minor.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

###### § 5

(The offences referred to in § 4 shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years and a fine of one thousand [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] if they are committed against a minor under sixteen years of age.) <L 2000-06-26/42, art.

2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-
2001>
(§ 6. Anyone who has attended [3 , live, including by means of information and communication technologies,]3 the debauchery or prostitution of a minor shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to two thousand [euros].) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force
: 27-03-2001> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
[

###### §

7. The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]2

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 8, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>
(2)	<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 2, 097; In force: 02-08-2013>
(3)	<L 2016-05-31/02, art. 4, 118; In force: 18-06-2016>

Art. 380bis. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 15, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
(Formerly art. 380quater) Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], anyone who, in a public place has by words, gestures or signs incited a person to debauchery. The penalty shall be doubled if the offence has been committed against a minor. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 380ter. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 16, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
(Formerly art. 380quinquies)

###### § 1

Shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to two thousand [euros], anyone who, by whatever means, makes or has made, publishes, distributes or disseminates advertising, directly or indirectly, even by concealing its nature under artifices of language, for an offer of services of a sexual nature, when such advertising is specifically addressed to minors or when it refers to services offered either by minors, or by persons alleged to be such. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The penalty shall be imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of three hundred [euros] to three thousand [euros] when the advertising referred to in article 1 has as its object or effect, direct or indirect, to facilitate the prostitution or debauchery of a minor or their exploitation for sexual purposes. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 2

Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros], anyone who, by whatever means, makes or has made,

publishes, distributes or disseminates advertising, directly or indirectly, even by concealing its nature under artifices of language, for an offer of services of a sexual nature, when such services are provided by a means of telecommunication. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 3

In cases not covered by §§ 1 and 2, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros], anyone who has, by any means of advertising whatsoever, even by concealing the nature of their offer or request under artifices of language, made known that they engage in prostitution, that they facilitate the prostitution of others or that they wish to enter into relations with a person engaging in debauchery. <L
2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
Shall be punished by the same penalties, anyone who, by any means of advertising whatsoever, incites, by the allusion made therein, to the exploitation of minors or adults for sexual purposes, or uses such advertising in connection with an offer of services.

Art. 380quater. (repealed) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 15, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

Art. 380quinquies. (repealed) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 16, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 381

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 17, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> The offences referred to in articles 379 and 380, §§ 3 and 4, shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years and a fine of one thousand [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] and the offences referred to in article 380, § 5, shall be punished by imprisonment of seventeen years to twenty years and a fine of one thousand [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros], if they constitute acts of participation in the principal or accessory activity of an association, whether or not the offender has the status of leader. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 381bis. (repealed) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 17, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 382

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 18, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> § 1. In the cases referred to in articles 379 and 380, the offenders shall, in addition, be sentenced to the deprivation of the rights set out in [1 article 31, paragraph 1]1.

###### § 2

The courts may prohibit persons convicted of an offence provided for in Article 380, §§ 1 to 3, for a term of one year to three years, from operating, either by themselves or through an intermediary, a drinking establishment, an employment agency, an entertainment business, a visual media rental or sales agency, a hotel, a furnished rental agency, a travel agency, a marriage brokerage business, an adoption institution, an establishment entrusted with the care of minors, a business providing transport for pupils and youth groups, a leisure or holiday establishment, or any establishment offering physical or psychological care, or from being employed therein in any capacity whatsoever.
In the event of a second conviction for an offence provided for in Article 380, §§ 1 to 3, the prohibition may be pronounced for a term of one year to twenty years. In the event of conviction for an offence provided for in Articles 379 and 380, §§ 4 and 5, the prohibition may be pronounced for a term of one to twenty years.

###### § 3

Without regard to the capacity of the natural or legal person as operator, owner, lessee or manager, the court may order the closure of the establishment in which the offences were committed, for a period of one month to three years.
Where the convicted person is neither owner, nor operator, nor lessee, nor manager of the establishment, closure may only be ordered if the seriousness of the concrete circumstances so requires, and this for a period of no more than two years, after summons at the request of the public prosecutor, the owner, the operator, the lessee or the manager of the establishment.
The summons before the court is transcribed [2 at the competent office of the General Administration of Patrimonial Documentation]2 for the location of the property by the bailiff who executed the writ.
The summons must contain the cadastral designation of the property concerned and identify the owner in the form and under the penalty provided for in Article 12 of the Act of 10 October 1913 amending the Mortgage Act and the Act on Forced Expropriation and reorganising the mortgage registry.
Any decision rendered in the case is noted in the margin of the transcription of the summons according to the procedure provided for by Article 84 of the Mortgage Act. The clerk sends [2 to the competent office of the General Administration of Patrimonial Documentation]2 the extracts and the declaration that no

appeal has been lodged.

###### § 4. Article 389 is applicable to this provision

(1)	<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 17, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>
(2)	<L 2018-07-11/07, art. 68, 133; En vigueur : 30-07-2018>

Art. 382bis.<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 20, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Without prejudice to the application of Article 382, any conviction for acts referred to [3 in Articles 371/1]3 to 377, [2 377quater,]2 379 to 380ter, 381 and 383 to 387, committed against a minor or involving his or her participation, may include, for a period of one year to twenty years, the prohibition of the right:
1° to participate, in any capacity whatsoever, in teaching given in a public or private establishment that receives minors;
2° to be part, as a volunteer member, statutory or contractual staff member, or as a member of the administrative and management bodies, of any legal person or de facto association whose activity principally concerns minors;
3° to be assigned to an activity that places the convicted person in a relationship of trust or authority vis-à-vis minors, as a volunteer member, statutory or contractual staff member or as a member of the administrative and management bodies, of any legal person or de facto association.
[1 4° to live, reside or remain in the designated determined zone designated by the competent judge. The imposition of this measure must be specially motivated and take into account the seriousness of the facts and the capacity for reintegration of the convicted person.]1
Article 389 is applicable to this provision.

Art. 382ter.[1 The special confiscation referred to in Article 42, 1°, is applied even if the ownership of the things to which it relates does not belong to the convicted person, without this confiscation being able, however, to prejudice the rights of third parties over the property liable to confiscation. It must also be applied, in the same circumstances, to the movable property, the part of

it, the immovable property, the room or any other space.
It may also be applied to the counter-value of such movables or immovables alienated between the commission of the offence and the final judicial decision.
In the event of seizure of immovable property, the formalities of Article 35bis of the Code of Criminal Procedure are followed.]1

This measure is taken either of the court's own motion or at the request of the civil party or the public prosecutor in a judicial decision specially motivated by reason of the seriousness of the facts, the capacity for reintegration or the risk of recidivism.]1

This excerpt sits under: PENAL CODE > BOOK 2. - OFFENCES AND THEIR PUNISHMENT IN PARTICULAR > TITLE VII > CHAPTER VI. - (CORRUPTION OF YOUTH AND PROSTITUTION)

#### CHAPTER VII. - PUBLIC OFFENCES AGAINST MORALITY

###### Art. 383

Whoever has exposed, sold or distributed songs, pamphlets or other writings, printed or not, figures or images contrary to morality, shall be sentenced to imprisonment from eight days to six months and to a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
(Whoever has sung, read, recited, caused to be heard or uttered obscenities in public meetings or places referred to in § 2 of article
444 shall be punished with the same penalties.) <L 29-01-1905, art. 1>
(Shall be punished with the same penalties:
Whoever has, with a view to trade or distribution, manufactured, possessed, imported or caused to be imported, transported or caused to be transported, delivered to a transport or distribution agent, advertised by any means of publicity whatsoever, songs, pamphlets, writings, figures or images contrary to morality;) <L 14-06-1926, art. 1>
Whoever has exposed, sold or distributed emblems or objects contrary to morality, has, with a view to trade or distribution, manufactured or possessed them, imported or caused to be imported, transported or caused to be transported, delivered to a transport or distribution agent, advertised by any means of publicity whatsoever.) <L 14-06-1926, art. 1>
[1 ...]1
[1 ...]1
(Paragraphs 8 to 10 repealed) <L 09-07-1973, art. unique>

(1)	<L 2018-10-15/03, art. 7, 136; In force: 08-11-2018>

Art. 383bis.§ 1. [2 Without prejudice to the application of articles 379 and 380, whoever has without right exposed, offered, sold, rented, transmitted, supplied, distributed, disseminated, or made available, or delivered child pornography material or has produced, imported or caused to be imported it, shall be punished with imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from five hundred euros to ten thousand euros.]

###### §

2. [2 Whoever has knowingly and without right acquired, possessed child pornography material or has, knowingly, accessed it by means of information and communication technologies, shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from one hundred euros to one thousand euros.]

###### §

3. The offence referred to in § 1, shall be punished (with imprisonment) from ten years to

fifteen years and a fine from five hundred [euros] to fifty thousand [euros], if it constitutes an act of participation in the principal or accessory activity of an association, whether or not the offender has the capacity of director. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 21, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force
: 01-01-2002>

###### § 4

[2 For the application of this article, "child pornography material" means:
1° any material visually representing, by any means whatsoever, a minor engaging in sexually explicit conduct, real or simulated, or representing the sexual organs of a minor for primarily sexual purposes;
2° any material visually representing, by any means whatsoever, a person who appears to be a minor engaging in sexually explicit conduct, real or simulated, or representing the sexual organs of that person, for primarily sexual purposes;
3° realistic images representing a minor who does not exist, engaging in sexually explicit conduct, or representing the sexual organs of that minor for primarily sexual purposes.]

###### §

5. [2 Articles 382, 382ter, 382quater, 382quinquies and 389 apply to the offences referred to in paragraphs 1 to 3.]2

(1)	<L 2011-11-30/28, art. 7, 083; In force: 30-01-2012>
(2)	<L 2016-05-31/02, art. 6, 118; In force: 18-06-2016>

Art. 383bis/1. [1 An organisation approved by the King may by right receive reports relating to images likely to be referred to in article 383bis, analyse their content and origin, and transmit them to the police services and judicial authorities.
For this purpose, this organisation carries out the mission entrusted to it, according to the procedures laid down by the King and relating more particularly to:
1° the obligation to be a member of an international association of Internet hotlines fighting against child pornography;
2° the transmission of the aforementioned reports to the police services and judicial authorities;
3° the transmission of the aforementioned reports relating to images hosted abroad, to the aforementioned international association;

4° the screening of persons responsible for receiving reports, analysing their content and origin and transmitting them, and of persons responsible for supervising these tasks within the organisation, in application of the law of 11 December 1998 relating to classification and security clearances, certificates and opinions;
5° the annual transmission of an activity report to the Minister of Justice; 6° the prohibition on constituting a database from the images reported to it.
The King determines the procedure for granting and withdrawing approval.]1

(1)	<Inserted by L 2016-05-31/02, art. 7, 118; In force: 18-06-2016>

###### Art. 384

<L 14-06-1926, art. 2> (In the cases referred to in article 383), the author of the writing, figure, image or object, shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 22, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force
: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 385

Whoever has publicly outraged morality by actions which offend decency, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to one year and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
(If the outrage has been committed in the presence of a minor under sixteen years of age, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one month to three years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros].) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 23, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 386

<L 28-07-1962, art. 2> If the offences provided for in article 383 have been committed against minors, the imprisonment shall be from six months to two years and the fine from one thousand [euros] to five thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
In the same case and without prejudice to the application of paragraph 2 of article 385, the penalties provided for in the first paragraph of that article may be doubled.

Art. 386bis. (deleted by change of numbering) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 24, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

Art. 386ter. (deleted by change of numbering) L 2000-11-28/35, art. 24, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 387

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 24, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>
(Formerly art. 386bis) Whoever sells or distributes to minors or exposes on the public highway or along it indecent images, figures or objects of a nature to disturb their imagination shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from one thousand [euros] to five thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 388

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 25, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> In the cases provided for in this chapter, the guilty parties may furthermore be sentenced to deprivation of the rights set out in [1 Article 31, paragraph 1]1.
In case of conviction by application of Articles 386, paragraph 1, or 387 and if the offence was committed in the operation of a bookshop, second-hand bookshop or business dealing in photographic products or equipment necessary for the production of any type of visual medium, or an entertainment business, the closure of the establishment may be ordered for a period of one month to three months.
In case of a second conviction for one of the acts referred to in paragraph 2, committed within three years from the first conviction, closure may be ordered for a period of three months to six months.
In case of a third conviction for the same acts, committed within five years from the second conviction, permanent closure may be ordered. In the latter case, the courts and tribunals may furthermore prohibit the convicted persons from operating, either by themselves or through an intermediary, a bookshop, a second-hand bookshop, a business dealing in photographic products or equipment necessary for the production of any type of visual medium, an entertainment business or one or more of these businesses or enterprises or from being employed therein in any capacity whatsoever.
Where the convicted person is neither owner, nor operator, nor lessee, nor manager of the establishment, closure may only be ordered if the seriousness of the concrete circumstances so requires. In that case, Article 382, § 3, paragraphs 2 to 5, is

applicable.
Article 389 is applicable to this provision.

(1)	<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 18, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 389

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 26, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1. The
duration of the prohibition pronounced by application of Articles 378, 382, § 1, 382bis and 388, paragraph 1, shall run from the day of conviction with suspension or from the day on which the convicted person shall have served or his sentence of imprisonment not accompanied by suspension shall have become time-barred and, in case of early release, from the day of his release provided that it is not revoked.
However, the prohibition pronounced by application of Article 382, § 2, shall take effect from the day on which the conviction in adversarial proceedings or by default becomes final.

###### § 2

Any infringement of the provision of the judgment or decision pronouncing a prohibition by application of the articles referred to in § 1 shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to six months and a fine of one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur
: 01-01-2002>

###### § 3

The closure pronounced by application of Articles 382, § 3, and 388 shall take effect from the day on which the conviction in adversarial proceedings or by default becomes final.

###### § 4

Any infringement of the provision of the judgment or decision ordering the closure of an establishment by application of the articles referred to in § 3 shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one thousand [euros] to five thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

#### CHAPITRE VIII. - (BIGAMY.)

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 27; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>
