###### Art. 391. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Whoever, being bound by the ties of marriage, shall have contracted another before the dissolution of the previous one, shall be punished by (imprisonment for five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 64, 041; En vigueur

: 13-03-2003>

#### CHAPTER IX. - ABANDONMENT OF FAMILY

Art. 391bis.<L 05-07-1963, art. 1> Any person who, having been sentenced by a judicial decision that can no longer be subject to opposition or appeal, to provide maintenance to his or her spouse, descendants or ascendants, has voluntarily remained more than two months without paying the instalments thereof, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of fifty to five hundred [euros] or by one of these penalties only, without prejudice, where applicable, to the application of more severe criminal sanctions. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
(Any person who fails to perform, under the conditions provided for in paragraph 1, the obligations determined by articles 203bis, 206, 207, 301, 303, (...) 336 and (and 353-14 of the Civil Code) and articles 1288, 3° and 4°, (...) of the Judicial Code, shall be punished by the same penalties.) <L 31-03-1987, art. 93> <L 2003-04-24/32, art. 6; 045; In force: 01-09-2005> <L 2007-04-27/00, art. 40, 1°, 065; In force: 01-09-2007>
The same penalties shall apply to the spouse who has voluntarily evaded, in whole or in part, the effects of the authorisation given by the judge pursuant to (articles 203ter, 221 and (301, § 11) of the Civil Code, and [3 1253ter/5 and 6]3, (...) of the Judicial Code) when it can no longer be subject to opposition or appeal. <L 31-03-1987, art. 93> <L 2007-04-27/00, art. 40, 2° and 3°, 065; In force: 01-09-2007>
The same shall apply to the spouse who, having been sentenced either to one of the obligations the non-performance of which is sanctioned by the first two paragraphs of this article, or by application of (articles 203ter, 221 and (301, § 11) of the Civil Code, and [3 1253ter/5 and 6]3, (...) of the Judicial Code) voluntarily refrains from completing the formalities provided for by social legislation and thereby deprives his or her spouse or children of the benefits to which they could claim. <L 31-03-1987, art. 93> <L 2007-04-27/00, art. 40, 2° and 3°, 065; In force: 01-09-2007>
[1 The same penalties shall apply to any descendant in direct line who, having been sentenced to a maintenance obligation, voluntarily refrains from completing the formalities provided for by social legislation and thereby deprives an ascendant of the benefits to which he or she could claim.]1
(The same penalties shall apply to any person who has voluntarily obstructed guardianship over family benefits or other social allowances, by neglecting to provide the necessary documents to the bodies responsible for the payment of these allowances, by making false or incomplete declarations, or by modifying the allocation given to them by the person or authority designated in accordance with article 29 of the law of 8 April 1965 on the protection of youth (, on the care of minors who have committed an act classified as an offence and on the reparation of damage caused by that act)). <L 2005-08-10/62, art. 2, 054; In force: 02-09-2005> <L 2006-05-15/35, art. 21; 056; In force: 16-10-2006>
In the event of a second conviction for one of the offences provided for in this article, committed within five years from the first, the penalties may be doubled.
[2 In the event of conviction for one of the offences provided for in this article, the judge may also pronounce disqualification from driving a motor vehicle, in accordance with articles 38 to 41 of the law of 16 March 1968 on road traffic policing.]2

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 9, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>
(2)	<L 2014-05-12/07, art. 12, 107; In force: 01-08-2014>
(3)	<L 2013-07-30/23, art. 239, 108; In force: 01-09-2014>

Art. 391ter. <L 05-07-1963, art. 2> When a person has remained more than two months without satisfying one of the obligations the non-performance of which is sanctioned by article 391bis, he or she may be summoned before the justice of the peace at the request of any interested person or the public prosecutor. The summons is made by means of a registered letter signed and sent by the clerk with acknowledgement of receipt.
The justice of the peace hears the explanations of the parties and draws up a report thereof which he transmits to the public prosecutor.

#### CHAPTER X

Crimes and offences in matters of adoption. <inserted by L 2003-04-24/32, art. 7; In force: indeterminate>

Art. 391quater. <inserted by L 2003-04-24/32, art. 7; In force: 01-09-2005> Any person who, with fraudulent intent, has obtained or attempted to obtain for himself or herself an adoption in contravention of the provisions of the law shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of twenty-six euros to five hundred euros or by one of these penalties only.
In the event of recidivism within three years following a judgment of conviction having acquired the force of res judicata for an offence under the first paragraph, these penalties may be doubled.

Art. 391quinquies. <inserted by L 2003-04-24/32, art. 7; In force: 01-09-2005> Any person who has acted as an intermediary by obtaining or attempting to obtain an adoption for another person without being a member of a body previously approved for that purpose by the competent community or who, being a member of an approved body, has obtained or attempted to obtain for another person an adoption in contravention of the provisions of the law, shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred euros to twenty-five thousand euros or by one of these penalties only.

#### CHAPTER XI

Forced marriage [1 and forced legal cohabitation]1. <L 2007-04-25/76, art. 2; In force: 25-06-2007>

(1)	<L 2013-06-02/08, art. 13, 098; In force: 03-10-2013>

Art. 391sexies.<L 2007-04-25/76, art. 2, 005; In force: 25-06-2007> Any person who, by violence or threats, has forced someone to contract a marriage shall be punished by imprisonment [1 of three months to five years and a fine of two hundred and fifty euros to five thousand euros]1.
The attempt is punished by imprisonment [1 of two months to three years and a fine of one hundred and twenty-five euros to two thousand five hundred euros]1.

(1)	<L 2013-06-02/08, art. 14, 098; In force: 03-10-2013>

Art. 391septies. [1 Any person who, by violence or threats, has forced someone to contract a legal cohabitation shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of two hundred and fifty euros to five thousand euros.
The attempt is punished by imprisonment of two months to three years and a fine of one hundred and twenty-five euros to two thousand five hundred euros.]1

(1)	<Inserted by L 2013-06-02/08, art. 15, 098; In force: 03-10-2013>

Art. 391octies.[

###### §

1. The judge who pronounces a conviction on the basis of articles 391sexies or 391septies or who establishes guilt for an offence under these provisions, may also pronounce the nullity of the marriage or legal cohabitation, at the request of the public prosecutor or any party having an interest in the case.

###### § 2

A judgment is only enforceable against the spouses or legal cohabitants if they have been parties or summoned to the case.
The public prosecutor may call in forced intervention the spouse or spouses or the legal cohabitant or legal cohabitants who are not parties to the case.
The intervention confers upon them the status of party to the case. These parties may exercise remedies.
The intervention is made from the beginning of the proceedings so that the parties may assert their rights regarding the annulment of the marriage or legal cohabitation.

###### § 3

Any writ of service of a judgment or decision pronouncing the annulment of a marriage or legal cohabitation is immediately communicated in copy by the acting bailiff to the clerk of the court that pronounced the decision.

###### § 4

[2 When the nullity of the marriage has been pronounced by a judgment or decision that has become final, the court clerk shall immediately transmit the data of the judgment or decision to the BAEC, with mention of the date on which the decision became final.
The BAEC shall establish a notation on this basis and associate it with the marriage certificate.
The BAEC shall immediately notify the judicial decision to the Aliens Office with mention of the date on which it became final.
The court clerk shall immediately inform the parties thereof.]

###### §

5. When the nullity of legal cohabitation has been pronounced by a judgment or decision that has become final, an extract containing the operative part of the judgment or decision and the mention of the day on which it became final shall be sent, without delay, by the court clerk to the civil registrar of the place where the declaration of legal cohabitation was made and to the Aliens Office.
The court clerk shall notify the parties thereof.
The civil registrar shall without delay record the annulment of the legal cohabitation in the population register.]1

(1)	<Inserted by L 2013-06-02/08, art. 16, 098; In force: 03-10-2013>
(2)	<L 2018-06-18/03, art. 103, 130; In force: 31-03-2019>

### TITLE VIII. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PERSONS

#### CHAPTER I

(HOMICIDE AND VOLUNTARY BODILY HARM, TORTURE, INHUMAN TREATMENT AND DEGRADING TREATMENT.) <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 4, 036; In force: 24-08-2002>

###### Art. 392

Homicide committed and injuries caused with the intent to attack the person of a determined individual, or of whoever will be found or encountered, are qualified as voluntary, even if this intent were dependent on some circumstance or some condition, and even if the perpetrator were mistaken as to the person of the victim of the attack.

Art. 392bis. <L 31-03-1987, art. 94> For the application of this chapter, the words "father", "mother" and "ascendant" also designate adoptive parents and, in case of adoption and full adoption, the ascendants of the adoptive parents.

##### SECTION I. - MURDER AND ITS VARIOUS TYPES

###### Art. 393. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Homicide committed with intent to cause death is qualified as murder. It shall be punished (by imprisonment for twenty to thirty years).
<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 65, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 394

Murder committed with premeditation is qualified as assassination. It shall be punished (by life imprisonment). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; In force: 11-08-1996>

###### Art. 395

<L 31-03-1987, art. 95> The murder of father, mother or other ascendants is qualified as parricide and shall be punished (by life imprisonment). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; In force: 11-08-1996>

###### Art. 396

The murder committed on a child at the moment of its birth or immediately thereafter is qualified as infanticide.
Infanticide shall be punished, according to the circumstances, as murder or as

assassination.
(Paragraphs 3 and 4 repealed) <L 12-07-1984, sole article>

###### Art. 397

Murder committed by means of substances that can cause death more or less promptly, in whatever manner these substances have been used or administered, is qualified as poisoning. It shall be punished (by life imprisonment). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; In force: 11-08-1996>

##### SECTION II

VOLUNTARY HOMICIDE NOT QUALIFIED AS MURDER AND VOLUNTARY BODILY HARM.

###### Art. 398

Whoever has voluntarily inflicted injuries or struck blows shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
In case of premeditation, the offender shall be sentenced to imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of fifty [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 399

If the blows or injuries have caused an illness or incapacity for personal work, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The offender shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], if he acted with premeditation. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 400.(See NOTE under TITLE)

The penalties shall be imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], if the blows or injuries have resulted in either an apparently incurable illness, or [1 incapacity for personal work of more than four months]1, or the loss of absolute use of an organ, or serious mutilation. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The penalty shall be (imprisonment of five to ten years), if there has been premeditation. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 66, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

(1)	<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 20, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 401. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 67, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> When blows struck or injuries inflicted voluntarily, but without intent to cause death, have nevertheless caused it, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of five to ten years.
He shall be punished by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years, if he committed these acts of violence with premeditation

Art. 401bis. (repealed) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 52, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 402

Whoever has caused another person an illness or incapacity for personal work, by voluntarily administering to him, but without intent to kill, substances that can cause death, or substances that, without being of a nature to cause death, can nevertheless seriously impair health, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 403.(See NOTE under TITLE)

The penalty shall be (imprisonment of five to ten years), when these substances have caused either an apparently incurable illness, or [1 incapacity for personal work of more than four months]1, or the loss of absolute use of an organ.

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 20, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 404. (See NOTE under TITLE)

If substances administered voluntarily, but without intent to cause death, have nevertheless caused it, the offender shall be punished (by imprisonment) of fifteen to twenty years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 69, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 405

The attempt to administer to another person, without intent to cause death, substances of the nature of those mentioned in article 402, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 405bis.<Inserted by L 2000-11-28/35, art. 28, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> In the cases referred to below, if the crime or offence has been committed against a minor or against a person [1 whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator]1, the penalties shall be as follows:
1° in the cases referred to in article 398, paragraph 1, the penalties shall be imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros]; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
2° in the cases referred to in article 398, paragraph 2, the penalties shall be imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to two hundred [euros]; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
3° in the cases referred to in article 399, paragraph 1, the penalties shall be imprisonment of four months to four years and a fine of fifty [euros] to two hundred [euros]; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> 4° in the cases referred to in article 399, paragraph 2, the penalties shall be imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros]; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
5° in the cases referred to in article 400, paragraph 1, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years;
6° in the cases referred to in article 400, paragraph 2, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;
7° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 1, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;
8° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 2, the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years;
9° in the cases referred to in article 402, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years;
10° in the cases referred to in article 403, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;
11° in the cases referred to in article 404, the penalty shall be imprisonment of seventeen years to twenty years.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 10, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

Art. 405ter.<Inserted by L 2000-11-28/35, art. 28, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> In the cases provided for in articles 398 to 405bis, if the crime or offence has been committed against a minor or against a person [1 vulnerable by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency and who]1, was not able to provide for his or her maintenance, by his or her father, mother or other ascendants [1 or collaterals up to the fourth degree]1, any other person having authority over the minor or [1 the vulnerable person]1 or having custody thereof, or any person who cohabits occasionally or habitually with the victim, the minimum of the penalties provided by these articles shall be doubled if it is imprisonment, and increased by two years if it is imprisonment.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 11, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

Art. 405quater.[1 Where one of the motives for the crime or offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person by reason of his or her alleged race, skin colour, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, change of sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, trade union conviction, a physical or genetic characteristic or social origin, the penalties shall be as follows:
1° in the cases referred to in article 393, the penalty shall be life imprisonment;
2° in the cases referred to in articles 398, 399, 405 and 405bis, 1° to 3°, the maximum of the penalty of imprisonment provided by these articles shall be doubled with a maximum of five years and the maximum of the fine shall be doubled with a maximum of five hundred euros;
3° in the cases referred to in articles 400, paragraph 1, 402 and 405bis, 4°, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years;
4° in the cases referred to in articles 400, paragraph 2, 401, paragraph 1, 403, 405bis, 5° and 9°, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;
5° in the cases referred to in articles 401, paragraph 2, 405bis, 6°, 7° and 10°, the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years;
6° in the cases referred to in articles 404, 405bis, 8° and 11°, the penalty shall be imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years.]1

(1)<L 2013-01-14/06, art. 2, 089; In force: 10-02-2013>

###### Art. 406.(See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 07-06-1963, art. 2> Shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years) whoever shall have maliciously obstructed railway, road, river or maritime traffic by any action affecting the communication routes, engineering structures or equipment, or by any other action likely to render dangerous the traffic or use of means of transport or to cause accidents on the occasion of their use or traffic. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 70, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> Independently of the cases referred to in the preceding paragraph, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros], whoever shall have maliciously obstructed railway, road, river or maritime traffic, by any object constituting an obstacle likely to prevent the traffic or use of means of transport. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
Shall be punished by a penalty of eight days to two months and a fine of twenty-six to five hundred [euros] whoever, by any other action, shall have maliciously prevented traffic in progress on the railway or road. [1 In addition, the judge may pronounce disqualification from driving a motor vehicle for a period of at least eight days and at most five years or for life in accordance with articles 38 to 49/1 of the coordinated laws of 16 March 1968 on road traffic regulations.]1 <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

(1)<L 2018-03-06/04, art. 3, 127; In force: 15-02-2018>

###### Art. 407. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 71, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> If the act has caused injuries of the nature of those provided for by article 399, the offender shall be sentenced to imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years. He shall be sentenced to imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years, if the injuries are of the nature of those provided for by article 400.

###### Art. 408. (See NOTE under TITLE)

If the act has caused the death of a person, the offender shall be punished (by imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 72, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 409

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 29, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> § 1.
Whoever shall have practised, facilitated or favoured any form of mutilation of the genital organs of a female person, with or without the consent of the latter, shall be punished by imprisonment of three years to five years.
The attempt shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year. [1 Shall be punished by the same penalty whoever shall have incited the practice of any form of mutilation of the genital organs of a female person or shall have, directly or indirectly, in writing or verbally made, had made, published, distributed or disseminated advertising in favour of such a practice.]

###### §

2. If the mutilation is practised on a minor person or for profit, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to seven years.

###### § 3

Where the mutilation has caused an apparently incurable illness or [2 incapacity for personal work of more than four months]2, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years.

###### § 4

Where mutilation committed without intent to cause death has nevertheless caused it, the penalty shall be imprisonment for ten to fifteen years.

###### § 5

If the mutilation referred to in § 1 was committed on a minor or a person who, by reason of their physical or mental condition, was unable to provide for their own maintenance, by their father, mother or other ascendants, any other person having authority over the minor or incapable person or having their care, or any person who cohabits occasionally or habitually with the victim, the minimum of the penalties provided for in §§ 1 to 4 shall be doubled if it is imprisonment, and increased by two years if it is detention.

(1)<L 2014-05-05/07, art. 2, 105; En vigueur : 12-07-2014>
(2)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 24, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 410

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 30, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> In the cases mentioned in articles 398 to 405, if the offender has committed the crime or offence against their father and mother or other ascendants [1 in the direct or collateral line up to the fourth degree]1, the minimum of the penalty provided for by these articles shall be doubled if it is imprisonment, and increased by two years if it is detention.
The same shall apply if the offender has committed the crime or offence against their spouse or the person with whom they cohabit or have cohabited and maintain or have maintained a lasting emotional and sexual relationship.

(Furthermore, in the case referred to in article 398, paragraph 1, the maximum penalty is increased to one year's imprisonment.) <L 2003-01-28/33, art. 2, 038; En vigueur : 22-02-2003>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 12, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

Art. 410bis.<inséré par L 2006-12-20/41, art. 6; En vigueur : 22-02-2007> In the cases mentioned in articles 398 to 405, if the offender has committed the crime or offence against a driver, an attendant, a ticket inspector or a ticket clerk of a public transport network operator, [1 a member of staff employed by the FPS Justice in a penitentiary establishment or within the security corps,]1 a postman, a firefighter, a member of civil protection, a paramedic, a doctor, a pharmacist, a physiotherapist, a nurse, a member of staff assigned to reception in the emergency departments of care institutions, a social worker, or a psychologist of a public service, in the exercise of their functions, [1 the penalties shall be those provided for in paragraph 3]1. [1 If ]1 the offender, being a pupil or student who is enrolled in an educational establishment or who was enrolled there during the six months preceding the facts, or the father or mother or a family member of that pupil or student, or any other person having authority over that pupil or student or having their care, has committed the crime or offence against a member of staff or management of that educational establishment, against persons responsible for the care of pupils in a medico-pedagogical institute organised or subsidised by the community, or against an external intervener instructed by the community authorities to prevent and resolve problems of school violence, in the exercise of their functions [1 , the penalties shall be those provided for in paragraph 3]1.
[1 The penalties are as follows:
1° in the cases referred to in articles 398, 399 and 405, the maximum imprisonment penalty provided for by these articles shall be doubled with a maximum of five years;
2° in the cases referred to in articles 400, paragraph 1 and 402, the penalty shall be imprisonment for five to ten years;
3° in the cases referred to in articles 400, paragraph 2, 401, paragraph 1 and 403, the penalty shall be imprisonment for ten to fifteen years;
4° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 2, the penalty shall be imprisonment for fifteen

years to twenty years;
5° in the cases referred to in article 404, the penalty shall be imprisonment for twenty to thirty years.]1

(1)<L 2012-12-27/29, art. 20, 090; En vigueur : 10-02-2013>

Art. 410ter.[1 In the cases referred to in articles 398 to 405, if the offender has committed the crime or offence against a referee at a sporting event, the minimum of the penalty provided for by these articles shall be increased by half of that penalty if it is imprisonment and increased by one year if it is detention.]1

(1)<Inséré par L 2012-07-05/07, art. 2, 086; En vigueur : 29-07-2012>

##### SECTION III. - EXCUSABLE HOMICIDE, WOUNDING AND ASSAULT

###### Art. 411

Homicide, wounding and assault are excusable if they were immediately provoked by serious violence against persons.

###### Art. 412

The crimes and offences mentioned in the preceding article are also excusable if they were committed in repelling, during the day, the scaling or breaking of enclosures, walls or entrances of an inhabited house or apartment or their outbuildings, unless it is established that the perpetrator could not have believed in an attack on persons, either as the direct aim of the person attempting the scaling or breaking, or as a consequence of the resistance that the designs of that person would encounter.

###### Art. 413. (Repealed)

<L 1997-11-24/51, art. 3, 020; En vigueur : 16-02-1998>

###### Art. 414.[1 Where the fact of excuse is proved, the penalty shall be reduced

-	to imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one hundred euros to five hundred euros, if it concerns a crime carrying a maximum penalty exceeding twenty years' imprisonment, whether or not it has been correctionalised,
-	to imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of fifty euros to two hundred euros, if it concerns any other crime, correctionalised or not;
-	to imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six

euros to one hundred euros, if it concerns another offence.]1

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 25, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 415. (repealed)

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 52, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>-03-
1987, art. 97>

##### SECTION IV. - JUSTIFIED HOMICIDE, WOUNDING AND ASSAULT

###### Art. 416

There is neither crime nor offence when homicide, wounding and assault were commanded by the actual necessity of legitimate defence of oneself or others.

###### Art. 417

Included in the cases of actual necessity of defence are the following two cases:
If homicide has been committed, if wounding has been inflicted, if assault has been carried out in repelling, during the night, the scaling or breaking of enclosures, walls or entrances of an inhabited house or apartment or their outbuildings, unless it is established that the perpetrator could not have believed in an attack on persons, either as the direct aim of the person attempting the scaling or breaking, or as a consequence of the resistance that the designs of that person would encounter. If the act took place in defending oneself against the perpetrators of theft or pillage, carried out with violence against persons.

##### Section V

<inserted by L 2002-06-14/42, art. 5; In force: 24-08-2002> Torture, inhuman treatment and degrading treatment

Art. 417bis. <inserted by L 2002-06-14/42, art. 5; In force: 24-08-2002> For the purposes of this section, the following definitions apply:
1° torture: any deliberate inhuman treatment which causes acute pain or very serious and cruel suffering, whether physical or mental;
2° inhuman treatment: any treatment by which serious mental or physical suffering is intentionally inflicted on a person, in particular for the purpose of obtaining information or confessions from that person, punishing that person, putting pressure on that person or intimidating that person or third parties;
3° degrading treatment: any treatment which causes the person subjected to it serious humiliation or debasement in the eyes of others or in his own eyes.

Art. 417ter.<inserted by L 2002-06-14/42, art. 5; In force: 24-08-2002> Whoever subjects a person to torture shall be punished by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years.
The offence referred to in the first paragraph shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years in the following cases:
1° where it has been committed:
a)	either by a public officer or official, a depositary or an agent of the public force acting in the exercise of his functions;
b)	either against a person [1 whose vulnerable situation by reason of age, pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental disability or by reason of precarious circumstances was apparent or known to the perpetrator]1;
c)	either against a minor;
2° or where the act has caused an apparently incurable disease, [2 incapacity for personal work of more than four months]2, the complete loss of an organ or of the use of an organ, or serious mutilation.
The offence referred to in the first paragraph shall be punished by twenty to thirty years' imprisonment:
1° where it has been committed against a minor or against a person who, by reason of his physical or mental condition, was unable to provide for his own maintenance, by his father, mother or other ascendants, any other person having authority over him or having him in their care, or any adult who cohabits occasionally or habitually with the victim;
2° or where it has caused death and was committed without intention to cause it.
The order of a superior or an authority cannot justify the offence provided for in the first paragraph.
(A state of necessity cannot justify the offence provided for in the first paragraph.) <L 2006-05-18/53, art. 2, 060; In force: 11-12-2006>

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 13, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>
(2)	<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

Art. 417quater.<inserted by L 2002-06-14/42, art. 5; In force: 24-08-2002>

Whoever subjects a person to inhuman treatment shall be punished by imprisonment of five to ten years.
The offence referred to in the first paragraph shall be punished by ten to fifteen years' imprisonment in the following cases:
1° where it has been committed:
a)	either by a public officer or official, a depositary or an agent of the public force acting in the exercise of his functions;
b)	either against a person [1 whose vulnerable situation by reason of age, pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental disability or by reason of precarious circumstances was apparent or known to the perpetrator]1;
c)	either against a minor;
2° or where the act has caused an apparently incurable disease, [2 incapacity for personal work of more than four months]2, the complete loss of an organ or of the use of an organ, or serious mutilation.
The offence referred to in the first paragraph shall be punished by fifteen to twenty years' imprisonment:
1° where it has been committed against a minor or against a person who, by reason of his physical or mental condition, was unable to provide for his own maintenance, by his father, mother or other ascendants, any other person having authority over him or having him in their care, or any adult who cohabits occasionally or habitually with the victim;
2° or where it has caused death and was committed without intention to cause it.
The order of a superior or an authority cannot justify the offence provided for in the first paragraph.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 14, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>
(2)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

Art. 417quinquies.<inserted by L 2002-06-14/42, art. 5; In force: 24-08-2002>Whoever subjects a person to degrading treatment shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to two years and a fine of 50 EUR to 300 EUR or by one of these penalties only.
[1 If the degrading treatment is committed against a person whose vulnerable situation by reason of age, pregnancy, illness,

infirmity or physical or mental disability was apparent or known to the perpetrator, the minimum penalty provided for in paragraph 1 shall be doubled.]1

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 15, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

#### CHAPITRE II. - INVOLUNTARY HOMICIDE AND BODILY HARM

###### Art. 418

A person is guilty of involuntary homicide or harm who has caused the harm through lack of foresight or precaution, but without intention to harm the person of another.

###### Art. 419

Whoever has involuntarily caused the death of a person shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
(Where the death is the consequence of a traffic accident, the imprisonment shall be three months to five years and the fine 50 euros to 2000 euros.) <L 2005-07-20/52, art. 28, 052; In force: 31-03-2006>

Art. 419bis. (Repealed) <L 2005-07-20/52, art. 30, 052; In force: 31-03-2006>

###### Art. 420

If the lack of foresight or precaution has resulted only in blows or injuries, the offender (shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months) and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 31-03-1936, art. 1> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
(Where the blows or injuries are the consequence of a traffic accident, the imprisonment shall be eight days to one year and the fine 50 euros to 1,000 euros.) <L 2005-07-20/52, art. 29, 052; In force: 31-03-2006>

Art. 420bis. (Repealed) <L 2005-07-20/52, art. 30, 052; In force: 31-03-2006>

###### Art. 421

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only, whoever has involuntarily caused another person an illness or incapacity for personal work, by administering to that person substances which are of a nature to cause death

or to seriously impair health. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 422

Where a railway convoy has suffered an accident of a nature to endanger the persons therein, the person who has involuntarily caused it shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
If bodily injuries have resulted from the accident, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to three years and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
If the accident has caused the death of a person, the imprisonment shall be six months to five years and the fine one hundred [euros] to six hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

OF CERTAIN CULPABLE ABSTENTIONS.

Art. 422bis.<L 06-01-1961, art. 1> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to (one year) and a fine of fifty to five hundred [euros] or by one of these penalties only, any person who abstains from coming to the aid of or procuring aid for a person exposed to serious danger, either where he has himself observed the situation of that person, or where that situation is described to him by those who request his intervention. <L 1995-04-13/33, art. 4, 1°, 015; In force: 05-05-1995> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The offence requires that the abstainer could intervene without serious danger to himself or to others. Where he has not personally observed the danger to which the person to be assisted was exposed, the abstainer may not be punished where the circumstances in which he was invited to intervene could lead him to believe in the lack of seriousness of the call or in the existence of risks.
(The penalty provided for in paragraph 1 is increased to two years where the person exposed to serious danger is a minor [1 or is a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts]1.) <L 1995-04-13/33, art. 4, 2°, 015; In force: 05-05-1995>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 16, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

Art. 422ter. <L 06-01-1964, art. 1> Shall be punished by the penalties provided for in the preceding article any person who, being able to do so without serious danger to himself or to others, refuses or neglects to provide to a person in danger the assistance which is legally required of him; any person who, being able to do so, refuses or neglects to carry out the works, the service, or to provide the assistance which he has been required to provide in the circumstances of accidents, tumults, shipwreck, flooding, fire or other calamities, as well as in cases of brigandage, pillage, flagrant offence, public outcry or judicial execution.

Art. 422quater.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 35, 064; In force: 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by articles 422bis and 422ter, the minimum of the correctional penalties imposed by those articles may be doubled, where one of the motives for the crime or offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person by reason of his or her alleged race, skin colour, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, [1 trade union conviction,]1 a physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 111, 074; In force: 31-12-2009>

#### CHAPITRE III. - (Offences against minors, [1 vulnerable persons]1 and the family.)

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 17, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

##### Section I

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31; In force: 27-03-2001> Desertion and abandonment of children or [1 vulnerable persons]1 in need.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 17, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 423

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> § 1er. Shall be

punished by imprisonment of one month to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only, those who have deserted or caused to be deserted, in any place whatsoever, a minor or [1 a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts]1. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 2

If as a result of the desertion, the person referred to in § 1er has remained seriously mutilated, suffers from an apparently incurable disease or has lost the absolute use of an organ, the offenders shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 3

If the desertion has caused the death of the person referred to in § 1er, the offenders shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 18, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 424

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only, without prejudice, where applicable, to the application of more severe criminal provisions: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The father or mother or the adoptive parents who abandon their child in need, even though he or she has not been left alone, who refuse to take him or her back or who refuse to pay for his or her maintenance when they have entrusted him or her to a third party or when he or she has been entrusted to a third party by judicial decision.
[1 The descendants in direct line who abandon their father, mother, adoptive parent or other ascendant in need, even though he or she has not been left alone, who refuse to take him or her back or who refuse to pay for his or her maintenance when they have entrusted him or her to a third party or when he or she has been entrusted to a third party by judicial decision.]1
In case of a second conviction for one of the offences provided for in this article, committed within a period of five years from the first, the penalties may be doubled.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 19, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

##### Section II

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> Deprivation

of food or care inflicted on minors or [1 vulnerable persons]1.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 20, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 425

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> § 1er. Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only, any person who has voluntarily deprived of food or care, to the point of compromising his or her health, a minor or [1 a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts and who]1 was not able to provide for his or her own maintenance. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 2

If the deprivation of food or care has caused either an apparently incurable disease, or the complete loss of the use of an organ, or a serious mutilation, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years.

###### § 3

If the deprivation of food or care done voluntarily, without intention to cause death, has nevertheless caused it, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 21, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 426

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1er. Whoever, having the care of a minor or of a person [1 vulnerable by reason of age, pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental deficiency and who is unable to provide for his or her own maintenance]1, shall have neglected the maintenance of that minor or that person to the point of compromising his or her health, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two months and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros] or by one of these penalties only, without prejudice, where applicable, to the application of more severe criminal provisions. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### § 2

If the neglect has caused the death of the minor or [1 of a person referred to in § 1er and who was unable to provide for his or her own maintenance]1, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

2002>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 22, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

##### Section III

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Provision common to sections Ier and II.

###### Art. 427

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> In the cases provided for in articles 423, 425 and 426, if the offender has committed the acts against his father, mother, adoptive parents or other ascendants, the minimum of the penalty prescribed by those articles shall be doubled if it is imprisonment, and increased by two years if it is criminal detention.
The same shall apply where the offender is the father, mother or adoptive parent of the victim, or any other person having authority over him or her or having his or her care.
[1 The penalty provided for in article 33 may, in addition, be applied.]1

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 23, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

##### Section IV

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Abduction and concealment of minors [1 and vulnerable persons]1.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 24, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 428

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1er.
Whoever shall have abducted or caused to be abducted a minor under twelve years of age shall be punished by criminal detention of five years to ten years, even if the minor voluntarily followed his or her abductor.

###### § 2

Whoever shall have, by violence, cunning or threat, abducted or caused to be abducted a minor over twelve years of age [1 or any person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts,]1 shall be punished by criminal detention of five years to ten years.

###### § 3

(...) <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 6, 036; En vigueur : 24-08-2002>

###### § 4

The penalty shall be criminal detention of fifteen years to twenty years if the abduction or detention of the abducted minor [1 or of the person referred to in § 2]1 has caused either an apparently incurable illness, or [2 incapacity for personal work of more than

four months]2, or the complete loss of the use of an organ, or serious mutilation.

###### § 5

If the abduction or detention has caused death, the penalty shall be criminal detention of twenty years to thirty years.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 25, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>
(2)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 429

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Whoever shall keep a minor [1 or a vulnerable person, referred to in article 428, § 2, whom he or she knows to have been abducted]1 shall be punished by the same penalties as the perpetrator of the abduction.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 26, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 430

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> In the cases referred to in articles 428 and 429, with the exception of the cases referred to in [2 article 428, §§ 4 and 5]2, the penalty shall be imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], if within five days of the abduction, the abductor or the person referred to in article 429 has voluntarily returned the abducted minor [1 or the abducted vulnerable person]1. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 27, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>
(2)<L 2014-05-05/09, art. 15, 106; En vigueur : 18-07-2014>

##### Section V

Failure to produce children. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 431

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Those who, having charge of a minor under twelve years of age, shall not produce him or her to the persons who have the right to claim him or her, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
If the offender conceals that minor for more than five days from those who have the right to claim him or her or if he or she unlawfully detains that minor outside the territory of the Kingdom, he or she shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of twenty-six

[euros] to two hundred [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 432

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1er. The father or mother who removes or attempts to remove his or her minor child from proceedings brought against him or her under legislation relating to the protection of youth or assistance to youth, who removes or attempts to remove him or her from the custody of the persons to whom the competent authority has entrusted him or her, who does not produce him or her to those who have the right to claim him or her, abducts him or her or causes him or her to be abducted, even with his or her consent, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
If the offender has been deprived of parental authority in whole or in part, the imprisonment may be increased to three years.

###### § 2

If the offender conceals the minor child for more than five days from those who have the right to claim him or her or if he or she unlawfully detains the minor child outside the territory of the Kingdom, he or she shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
If the offender has been deprived of parental authority in whole or in part, the imprisonment shall be at least three years.

###### § 3

In cases where a decision has been made concerning the custody of the minor child either during or following divorce or legal separation proceedings, or in other circumstances provided for by law, the penalties provided for in §§ 1er and 2 shall be applied to the father or mother who removes or attempts to remove his or her minor child from the custody of those to whom he or she has been entrusted by virtue of the decision, who does not produce him or her to those who have the right to claim him or her, abducts him or her or causes him or her to be abducted, even with his or her consent.

###### § 4

Where the custody of the minor child has been the subject of a settlement agreement prior to proceedings by mutual consent, the penalties provided for in §§ 1er and 2 shall be applied to the father or mother who, from the date of registration of the divorce by mutual consent, removes or attempts to remove his or her minor child from the custody of those to whom he or she has been entrusted by virtue of the decision or the settlement agreement, who does not produce him or her to those who have the right to claim him or her, abducts him or her or causes him or her to be abducted, even with his or her

consent.

##### Section VI

Use of minors [1 and vulnerable persons]1 for criminal or unlawful purposes. <insérée par L 2005-08-10/62, art. 3 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 28, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 433

<L 2005-08-10/62, art. 4, 054 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005> Subject to the application of Article 433quinquies, any person who has, directly or through an intermediary, attracted or used a minor [1 or a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator,]1 with a view to committing, in one of the manners provided for by Article 66, a crime or an offence, shall be punished with the penalties provided for that crime or offence, the minimum custodial sentence of which shall be increased by one month where the maximum imprisonment penalty provided for is one year, by two months where it is two years, by three months where it is three years, by five months where it is five years, and by two years in the case of imprisonment for a fixed term, and the minimum amount of the fine, where applicable, shall be doubled.
The minimum penalty provided for in paragraph 1 shall be increased again and in the same proportion where:
1° the minor is under sixteen years of age, or
2° the person referred to in paragraph 1 abuses the particularly vulnerable position in which the minor finds himself or herself, or
3° the person referred to in paragraph 1 is the father, the mother, another ascendant, the adopter, or any other person having authority over the minor or having custody of him or her, or
4° the action of attracting minors or using them with a view to committing a crime or an offence constitutes a habitual activity.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 29, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

##### Section VII

Invasion of the privacy of the minor. <inserted by L 2005-08-10/62, art. 5 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005>

Art. 433bis. <inserted by L 2005-08-10/62, art. 6 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005> The publication and dissemination by means of books, through the press, by cinematography, by radio, by television or by any other means, of the report of proceedings before the youth court, before the investigating judge and before the chambers of the court of appeal competent to rule on appeals brought against their decisions, are prohibited.
Only the grounds and the operative part of the judicial decision pronounced in public hearing are excepted, subject to the application of paragraph 3.
The publication and dissemination, by any means, of texts, drawings, photographs or images likely to reveal the identity of a person prosecuted or who is the subject of a measure provided for (in Articles 37, 39, 43, 49, 52, 52quater and 57bis) of the Act of 8 April 1965 on the protection of youth or in the Act of 1 March 2002 on the provisional placement of minors who have committed an act classified as an offence, are also prohibited. The same applies to the person who is the subject of a measure taken within the framework of the procedure referred to in Article 63bis of the Act of 8 April 1965 on the protection of youth (on the care of minors who have committed an act classified as an offence and on the reparation of the damage caused by that act). <L 2006-05-15/35, art. 22, 056; En vigueur : 01-10-2007>
Offences against this Article shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of three hundred euros to three thousand euros or by one of these penalties only.

##### Section VIII

[1 Luring of minors through information and communication technologies for criminal or offence purposes.]1

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 26, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 433bis/1. [1 An adult person who communicates through information and communication technologies with a proven or supposed minor, with a view to facilitating the perpetration against him or her of a crime or an offence, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to five years:
1° if he or she has concealed or lied about his or her identity or age or capacity; 2° if he or she has insisted on the discretion to be observed regarding their exchanges;
3° if he or she has offered or held out the prospect of a gift or any advantage; 4° if he or she has used any other manoeuvre.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2014-04-10/25, art. 3, 103; En vigueur : 10-05-2014>

CHAPITRE IIIbis. - Exploitation of begging <Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 6; En vigueur : 12-09-2005>

Art. 433ter.<Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 7; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> The following shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of five hundred euros to twenty-five thousand euros:
1° whoever has recruited, led away, diverted or detained a person with a view to delivering him or her to begging, has incited him or her to beg or to continue doing so, or has placed him or her at the disposal of a beggar so that he or she may use him or her to arouse public commiseration;
2° whoever has, in any manner whatsoever, exploited the begging of another.
The attempt to commit the offences referred to in paragraph 1 shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of one hundred euros to two thousand euros.
[1 The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]1

(1)<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 3, 097; En vigueur : 02-08-2013>

Art. 433quater.<Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 8; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> The offence referred to in Article 433ter, paragraph 1, shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred euros to fifty thousand euros where it has been committed:
1° against a minor;
2° by abusing the [1 situation of vulnerability in which a person finds himself or herself by reason of his or her illegal or precarious administrative situation, his or her precarious social situation, his or her age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency]1, in such a way that the person has in fact no other real and acceptable choice but to submit to that abuse;
3° by making use, directly or indirectly, of fraudulent manoeuvres, violence, threats or any form of coercion whatsoever.
[2 The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]2

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 30, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>
(2)<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 4, 097; En vigueur : 02-08-2013>

CHAPITRE IIIter. - Trafficking in human beings <Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 9; En vigueur : 12-09-2005>

Art. 433quinquies.<Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 10; En vigueur : 12-09-2005>

###### § 1

er. [1 The offence of trafficking in human beings is constituted by the act of recruiting, transporting, transferring, harbouring, receiving a person, taking or transferring control exercised over him or her:
1° for the purposes of exploitation of prostitution or other forms of sexual exploitation;
2° for the purposes of exploitation of begging;
3° for the purposes of work or services, in conditions contrary to human dignity;
4° for the purposes of removal of organs in violation of the Act of 13 June 1986 on the removal and transplantation of organs, or of human bodily material in violation of the Act of 19 December 2008 on the obtaining and use of human bodily material intended for human medical applications or for scientific research purposes;
5° or in order to have that person commit a crime or an offence, against his or her will.]1
Except in the case referred to in 5, the consent of the person referred to in paragraph 1 to the envisaged or actual exploitation is irrelevant.

###### § 2

The offence provided for in § 1 shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred euros to fifty thousand euros.

###### § 3

The attempt to commit the offence referred to in § 1 shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of one hundred euros to ten thousand euros.
[

###### §

4. The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]2

(1)<L 2013-04-29/15, art. 2, 096; En vigueur : 02-08-2013>
(2)<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 5, 097; En vigueur : 02-08-2013>

Art. 433sexies.<Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 11; En vigueur : 12-09-2005>

The offence provided for in article 433quinquies, § 1er, shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from seven hundred and fifty euros to seventy-five thousand euros when the offence has been committed:
1° by a person who has authority over the victim, or by a person who has abused the authority or the facilities conferred by his or her functions;
2° by a public officer or official, a depositary or an agent of the public force acting in the course of the exercise of his or her functions.
[1 The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]1

(1)	<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 6, 097; En vigueur : 02-08-2013>

Art. 433septies.<Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 12; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> The offence provided for in article 433quinquies, § 1er, shall be punished by imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years and a fine from one thousand euros to one hundred thousand euros in the following cases:
1° when the offence has been committed against a minor;
2° when it has been committed by abusing the [1 situation of vulnerability in which a person finds himself or herself by reason of his or her illegal or precarious administrative situation, his or her precarious social situation, his or her age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency]1, in such a way that the person has in fact no other real and acceptable choice but to submit to this abuse;
3° when it has been committed by using, directly or indirectly, fraudulent manoeuvres, violence, threats or any form of coercion [4 , or by resorting to abduction, abuse of authority or deception]4;
[4 3bis° when it has been committed by means of the offer or acceptance of payments or any advantages whatsoever to obtain the consent of a person having authority over the victim;]4
4° when the life of the victim has been endangered deliberately or by serious negligence;
5° when the offence has caused an apparently incurable disease, a [3 personal incapacity for work of more than four months]3, the complete loss of an organ or of the use of an organ, or a serious mutilation;
6° when the activity concerned constitutes a habitual activity;
7° when it constitutes an act of participation in the principal or accessory activity of an association, whether or not the offender has the capacity of leader.
[2 The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]2

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 31, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

Art. 433octies.<Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 13; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> The offence provided for in article 433quinquies, § 1er, shall be punished by imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years and a fine from one thousand euros to one hundred and fifty thousand euros in the following cases:
1° when the offence has caused the death of the victim without intention to cause it; 2° when it constitutes an act of participation in the principal or accessory activity of a criminal organisation, whether or not the offender has the capacity of leader.
[1 The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]1
