CHAPITRE IIIquater. - De l'abus de la vulnérabilité d'autrui en vendant, louant ou mettant à disposition des biens en vue de réaliser un profit anormal <Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 15; En vigueur : 12-09-2005>

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 32, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

Art. 433decies.<Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 15; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> Shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of five hundred euros to twenty-five thousand euros, anyone who has abused, either directly or

through an intermediary, the [1situation of vulnerability in which a person finds themselves by reason of their illegal or precarious administrative situation, their precarious social situation, their age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency]1, by selling, renting or making available, with the intention of realising an abnormal profit, movable property, a part thereof, immovable property, a room or other space referred to in Article 479 of the Penal Code in conditions incompatible with human dignity [2 ...]2. The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 33, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>
(2)	<L 2013-04-29/14, art. 2, 095; En vigueur : 02-08-2013>

Art. 433undecies. <Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 17; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> The offence referred to in Article 433decies shall be punished by one to five years' imprisonment and a fine of one thousand euros to one hundred thousand euros in the following cases:
1° where the activity concerned constitutes a habitual activity;
2° where it constitutes an act of participation in the principal or accessory activity of an association, whether or not the offender has the status of a director.
The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.

Art. 433duodecies. <Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 18; En vigueur : 12-09-2005>The offence referred to in Article 433decies shall be punished by imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of one thousand euros to one hundred and fifty thousand euros if it constitutes an act of participation in the principal or accessory activity of a criminal organisation, whether or not the offender has the status of a director. The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.

Art. 433terdecies.<Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 19; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> In the cases referred to in Articles 433undecies and 433duodecies, the offenders shall also be sentenced to deprivation of the rights specified in [1 Article 31, paragraph 1]1.
The special confiscation provided for in Article 42, 1°, shall be applied to offenders of the offence referred to in Article 433decies, even where the ownership of the things to which it relates does not belong to the convicted person, without however such confiscation being able to prejudice the rights of third parties over the property liable to confiscation. It must also be applied, in the same circumstances, to the movable property, the part thereof, the immovable property, the room or any other space referred to in that article.
(It may also be applied to the counter-value of such movables or immovables alienated between the commission of the offence and the final judicial decision.) <L 2006-02-09/33, art. 2, 055 ; En vigueur : 10-03-2006>

(1)	<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 20, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

Art. 433quaterdecies.<Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 20; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> As the case may be, the public prosecutor or the examining magistrate may seize the movable property, the part thereof, the immovable property, the room or any other space referred to in Article 433decies. If a decision is made to carry out the seizure, the movable property, the part thereof, the immovable property, the room or any other space referred to in Article 433decies must be sealed or, with the written consent of the owner or lessor, be made available to the C.P.A.S. in order to be restored and temporarily rented.
The decision of the public prosecutor or the examining magistrate, as the case may be, to proceed with the seizure shall be served on the owner or lessor. In the case of seizure of immovable property, the decision must also be served within twenty-four hours at the latest and be presented for transcription to the [1 competent office of the General Administration of Patrimonial Documentation]1 of the place where the property is located. The day of transcription taken into account is that of the service of the seizure decision. The seizure remains valid until the time of the final judicial decision by which either confiscation has been pronounced or the lifting of the seizure is pronounced. A lifting of the seizure may previously be granted at any time, as the case may be, by the public prosecutor or by the examining magistrate after the latter has notified the public prosecutor. The person whose property has been seized may only bring the remedies provided for in Articles 28sexies and 61quater of the Code of Criminal Instruction after a period of one year from the date of seizure.

(1)	<L 2018-07-11/07, art. 69, 133; En vigueur : 30-07-2018>

Art. 433quinquiesdecies. <Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 21; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> In the cases referred to in Article 433decies, the victims may be, where appropriate, accommodated or rehoused by decision, as the case may be, of the competent minister, the competent authority or the officials designated by them, in consultation with the competent services in this matter. The accommodation costs shall be borne by the accused. Where the accused is acquitted, the costs shall be borne, as the case may be, by the State or the competent C.P.A.S.

#### CHAPITRE IV

DES ATTENTATS A LA LIBERTE INDIVIDUELLE ET A L'INVIOLABILITE DU DOMICILE, COMMIS PAR DES PARTICULIERS.

###### Art. 434

Shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], those who, without order from the constituted authorities and except in cases where the law permits or orders the arrest or detention of individuals, have arrested or caused to be arrested, detained or caused to be detained any person. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 435

The imprisonment shall be six months to three years and the fine fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros], if the illegal and arbitrary detention has lasted more than ten days. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 436

If the illegal and arbitrary detention has lasted more than one month, the offender shall be sentenced to imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 437. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

The penalty of (imprisonment of five to ten years) shall be pronounced, if the arrest was carried out either on a false order of the public authority, or with the costume or under the name of one of its agents, or if the person arrested or detained was threatened with death. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 74, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 438. (Repealed)

<L 2002-06-14/42, art. 7, 036; En vigueur : 24-08-2002>

Art. 438bis.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 36, 064; En vigueur : 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for in this chapter, the minimum penalties prescribed by these articles may be doubled if they are correctional penalties and increased by two years if they are imprisonment, when one of the motives for the crime or offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person on the grounds of their alleged race, their

skin colour, their ancestry, their national or ethnic origin, their nationality, their sex, their sexual orientation, their marital status, their birth, their age, their fortune, their religious or philosophical conviction, their current or future state of health, a disability, their language, their political conviction, [1 their trade union conviction,]1 a physical or genetic characteristic or their social origin.

(1)	<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 112, 074; En vigueur : 31-12-2009>

###### Art. 439

Shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], whoever, without order from the authority and outside the cases where the law permits entry into the home of private individuals against their will, [1 either shall have entered a house, an apartment, a room or a dwelling inhabited by another, or their outbuildings, by means of threats or violence against persons, by means of breaking and entering, climbing or false keys, or shall occupy this property, or shall stay there without authorisation from the occupants.]1 <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(1)	<L 2017-10-18/08, art. 2, 126; En vigueur : 16-11-2017>

###### Art. 440

The imprisonment shall be six months to five years and the fine one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], if the act was committed, either on a false order from the public authority, or with the uniform, or under the name of one of its agents, or with the combination of the following three circumstances: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
If the act was carried out at night;
If it was carried out by two or more persons;
If the offenders or one of them were carrying weapons.
The offenders may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33 (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 441

The attempt of the offence provided for in the preceding article shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 442. Shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to two years and a

fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], whoever shall have entered, without the consent of the owner or tenant, the places designated in article 439, and shall have been found there at night. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 442

/1. [

###### §

1er. Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one month and a fine of twenty-six euros to one hundred euros or one of these penalties only, whoever, either without order from the authority, or without authorisation from a person possessing a title or a right that gives access to the property concerned or that permits its use or staying in the property and outside the cases where the law authorises it, shall have entered the house, apartment, room or uninhabited dwelling of another, or their outbuildings or any other premises or the uninhabited movable property of another that may or may not serve as a dwelling, or shall occupy it, or shall stay there in any manner whatsoever, without being themselves the holder of the aforementioned right or title.

###### § 2

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six euros to two hundred euros or one of these penalties only, whoever, within the fixed period, does not comply with the eviction order referred to in article 12, § 1er, of the law of 18 October 2017 on unlawful penetration, occupation or stay in another's property or with the expulsion referred to in article 1344decies of the Judicial Code.

###### § 3

The offence referred to in paragraph 1er may only be prosecuted upon complaint by a person possessing a title or a right to the property concerned.]1

(1)	<Inséré par L 2017-10-18/08, art. 3, 126; En vigueur : 16-11-2017>

CHAPITRE IVbis. - (inséré par <L 1998-10-30/34, art. 2, En vigueur : 27-12-1998>) HARASSMENT.

Art. 442bis.(inséré par <L 1998-10-30/34, art. 2, En vigueur : 27-12-1998>) Whoever shall have harassed a person when they knew or should have known that they would seriously affect by this behaviour the tranquillity of the person concerned, shall be punished by a penalty of imprisonment of fifteen days to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

[1 If the acts referred to in paragraph 1 are committed to the detriment of a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts, the minimum penalty provided for in paragraph 1 shall be doubled.]1
[2 ...]2

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 34, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>
(2)	<L 2016-03-25/07, art. 2, 116; En vigueur : 15-04-2016>

Art. 442ter.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 37, 064; En vigueur : 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for in article 442bis, the minimum correctional penalties prescribed by this article may be doubled, when one of the motives for the offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person on the grounds of their alleged race, their skin colour, their ancestry, their national or ethnic origin, their nationality, their sex, their sexual orientation, their marital status, their birth, their age, their fortune, their religious or philosophical conviction, their current or future state of health, a disability, their language, their political conviction, [1 their trade union conviction,]1 a physical or genetic characteristic or their social origin.

(1)	<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 113, 074; En vigueur : 31-12-2009>

CHAPITRE IVter. [1 - Abuse of the situation of weakness of persons]1

(1)	<Inséré par L 2011-11-26/19, art. 35, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

Art. 442quater.[

###### §

1er. Whoever shall have, while knowing the situation of physical or psychological weakness of a person, seriously impairing the capacity for discernment of this person, fraudulently abused this weakness to lead this person to an act or an abstention seriously harming their physical or mental integrity or their patrimony, shall be punished by a penalty of one month to two years' imprisonment and a fine of one hundred euros to one thousand euros or one of these penalties only.

###### § 2

The penalties shall be imprisonment of one month to four years and a fine of two hundred euros to two thousand euros or one of these penalties only

in the following cases:
1° if the act or abstention referred to in § 1er results from a placing in a state of physical or psychological subjection by the exercise of serious or repeated pressure or techniques capable of impairing the capacity for discernment;
2° if the abuse referred to in § 1er was committed against a minor;
3° if the act or abstention referred to in § 1er resulted in either an apparently incurable illness, or a [2 personal incapacity for work of more than four months]2, or the complete loss of use of an organ, or a serious mutilation;
4° if the abuse referred to in § 1er constitutes an act of participation in the main or accessory activity of an association.

###### § 3

The penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years if the act or abstention of the person caused their death.

###### § 4

The court may, in application of §§ 1er and 2, disqualify the convicted person from all or part of the rights listed in article 31, paragraph 1, for a term of five years to ten years.

###### § 5

The court may order that the judgment or a summary thereof be published, at the expense of the convicted person, in one or more daily newspapers, or in any other manner.]1

(1)	<Inséré par L 2011-11-26/19, art. 36, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>
(2)	<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 20, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

#### CHAPTER V. - OFFENCES AGAINST THE HONOUR OR REPUTATION OF PERSONS

###### Art. 443

Any person who, in the cases indicated below, has maliciously imputed to a person a specific fact which is of a nature to harm the honour of that person or to expose them to public contempt, and of which legal proof is not provided, is guilty of slander when the law admits proof of the imputed fact, and of defamation when the law does not admit such proof.
(When the imputed fact is that of having, during hostilities, made a pact with the enemy, either by providing them with assistance in soldiers, men, money, food, arms, ammunition or any materials whatsoever, or by procuring or facilitating by any means whatsoever entry, maintenance or stay on the territory, without having been compelled or required to do so, proof thereof shall always be admissible and may be made by all means of law.

If such proof is provided sufficiently, the imputation shall not give rise to any criminal prosecution.) <L 11-10-1919, art. unique>

###### Art. 444

The offender shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], when the imputations have been made: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
Either in public meetings or places;
Or in the presence of several individuals, in a place not public, but open to a certain number of persons having the right to assemble or frequent it; Or in any place whatsoever, in the presence of the offended person and before witnesses;
Or by printed or unprinted writings, images or emblems posted, distributed or sold, offered for sale or exposed to public view;
Or finally by writings not made public, but addressed or communicated to several persons.

###### Art. 445

Shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to six months and a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros]:<L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur
: 01-01-2002>
Any person who has made in writing to the authority a slanderous denunciation;
Any person who has addressed in writing to a person slanderous imputations against their subordinate.

###### Art. 446

Slander and defamation against any constituted body shall be punished in the same manner as slander or defamation directed against individuals.

###### Art. 447

The accused of an offence of slander for imputations directed, in respect of facts relating to their functions, either against holders or agents of authority or against any person having a public character, or against any constituted body, shall be admitted to make, by all ordinary means, proof of the imputed facts, subject to contrary proof by the same means.
If it concerns a fact which falls within private life, the author of the imputation may not invoke, for their defence, any proof other than that which results from a judgment or any other authentic act.
If the imputed fact is the subject of a criminal prosecution or of a denunciation on

which no decision has been made, the action for slander shall be suspended until the final judgment, or until the final decision of the competent authority. (In the case where the public action or the disciplinary action relating to the imputed fact is extinguished, the file concerning that action is joined to the file of the action for slander and the action for slander is resumed.
In the case of a decision to file without further action or of no case to answer as regards the action relating to the imputed fact, the action for slander is resumed, without prejudice to a suspension of that action if the investigation relating to the imputed fact experiences new judicial developments.) <L 2001-07-04/55, art. 2, 032; En vigueur
: 20-08-2001>

###### Art. 448

Any person who has insulted a person either by acts, or by writings, images or emblems, in one of the circumstances indicated in article 444, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
(Shall be punished by the same penalties any person who, in one of the circumstances indicated in article 444, has insulted by words, in their capacity or by reason of their functions, a person holding authority or public force, or having a public character.) <L 27-07-1934, art. 3>

###### Art. 449

When there exists at the time of the offence legal proof of the imputed facts, if it is established that the accused made the imputation without any motive of public or private interest and with the sole purpose of causing harm, they shall be punished, as guilty of malicious disclosure, by imprisonment of eight days to two months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to four hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 450

The offences provided for by this chapter, committed against private individuals, with the exception of slanderous denunciation, may only be prosecuted upon complaint of the person who claims to be offended.
If the person has died without having lodged a complaint or without having renounced it, or if the slander or defamation was directed against a person after their death, prosecution may only take place upon complaint of their spouse, descendants or (legal) heirs up to and including the third degree. <L 31-03-1987, art. 98>

###### Art. 451

No one may allege as a cause of justification or excuse, that the writings, printed matter, images or emblems which are the subject of the prosecution are only the reproduction of publications made in Belgium or in foreign countries.

###### Art. 452

<L 10-10-1967, art. 141> Speeches delivered or writings produced before the courts shall not give rise to any criminal prosecution, when such speeches or writings relate to the case or to the parties.
Slanderous, insulting or defamatory imputations unrelated to the case or to the parties may give rise either to public action, or to civil action by the parties or third parties.

SPECIAL PROVISION.

###### Art. 453

Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], any person who has been guilty of violation of tombs or sepulchres. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Art. 453bis.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 38, 064; En vigueur : 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by this chapter, the minimum of the correctional penalties imposed by these articles may be doubled, when one of the motives for the offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person by reason of their supposed race, skin colour, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, [1 trade union conviction,]1 physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)	<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 114, 074; En vigueur : 31-12-2009>

#### CHAPTER VI. - ON CERTAIN OTHER OFFENCES AGAINST PERSONS

###### Art. 454

Whoever shall have mixed or caused to be mixed, either with foodstuffs or beverages, or with any food substances or commodities, intended to

be sold or distributed, materials which are of a nature to cause death or to seriously impair health, shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to two thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 455. Shall be punished by the penalties provided for in the preceding article

Whoever shall sell, distribute or expose for sale foodstuffs, beverages, substances or any food commodities, knowing that they contain materials of a nature to cause death or to seriously impair health;
Whoever shall have sold or procured such materials, knowing that they were to be used to adulterate food substances or commodities.

###### Art. 456

Shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros], whoever shall have in his store, his shop or in any other place, foodstuffs, beverages, commodities or food substances, intended to be sold or distributed, knowing that they contain materials of a nature to cause death or to seriously impair health. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 457

The mixed foodstuffs, beverages, commodities or food substances shall be seized, confiscated and rendered unusable.
(Paragraph 2 repealed) <L 29-10-1919, art. 90>
He may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.
The court shall order that the judgment be posted in the places it shall designate and inserted in whole or in extract in the newspapers it shall indicate; all at the expense of the convicted person.

###### Art. 458

Physicians, surgeons, health officers, pharmacists, midwives and all other persons who are depositaries, by status or by profession, of secrets confided to them, who, except in the case where they are called to give evidence in court (or before a parliamentary commission of inquiry) and [1 the case where the law, decree or ordinance obliges or authorizes them]1 to disclose these secrets, shall have revealed them, shall be punished by imprisonment [1 of one year to three years and a fine of one hundred euros to one thousand euros or by one of these penalties only]1. <L 1996-06-30/34, art. 10, 017; In force: 26-07-1996> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

(1)	<L 2017-07-06/24, art. 312, 124; In force: 03-08-2017>

Art. 458bis.[1 Any person who, by status or by profession, is a depositary of secrets and has thereby knowledge of an offence provided for [4 in articles
371/1]4 [5 to 377, 377quater, 379, 380, 383bis, §§ 1 and 2, 392 to 394, 396 to 405ter, 409, 423, 425, 426 and 433quinquies]5, which has been committed against a minor or against a person who is vulnerable by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, [2 violence between partners,]2 [6 acts of violence perpetrated in the name of culture, custom, religion, tradition or so-called
"honour",]6 an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency may, without prejudice to the obligations imposed upon him by article 422bis, inform the King's Prosecutor thereof, either when there exists a serious and imminent danger to the physical or mental integrity of the minor or the vulnerable person referred to, and he is not in a position, alone or with the assistance of third parties, to protect that integrity, or when there are indications of a serious and real danger that other minors or vulnerable persons referred to may be victims of the offences provided for in the aforementioned articles and he is not in a position, alone or with the assistance of third parties, to protect that integrity.]1

(NOTE: by its judgment no. 127/2013 of 26-09-2013, the Constitutional Court annulled article 6 of the law of 30 November 2011, insofar as it applies to the lawyer who is the depositary of confidences of his client, the perpetrator of the offence which has been committed within the meaning of that article, when such information is likely to incriminate that client.)

Art. 458ter. [

###### §

1. There is no offence when a person who, by status or by profession, is a depositary of secrets, communicates them in the context of a consultation organized either by or pursuant to a law, a decree or an

ordinance, or with a reasoned authorization from the King's Prosecutor.
This consultation may exclusively be organized either with a view to protecting the physical and psychological integrity of the person or of third parties, or with a view to preventing the offences referred to in Title Iter of Book II or the offences committed in the context of a criminal organization, as defined in article 324bis.
The law, decree or ordinance, or the reasoned authorization from the King's Prosecutor, referred to in paragraph 1, shall determine at least who may participate in the consultation, with what purpose and according to what procedures the consultation shall take place.

###### § 2

The participants are bound to secrecy with regard to the secrets communicated during the consultation. Any person violating this secrecy shall be punished by the penalties provided for in article 458.
The secrets which are communicated during this consultation may give rise to criminal prosecution only for those offences for which the consultation was organized.]1

(1)	<Inserted by L 2017-07-06/24, art. 313, 124; In force: 03-08-2017>

Art. 458quater. [1 Articles 458bis and 458ter are not applicable to the lawyer with regard to the communication of confidential information from his client when such information is likely to expose his client to criminal prosecution.]1

(1)	<Inserted by L 2017-07-06/24, art. 314, 124; In force: 03-08-2017>

###### Art. 459

Shall be punished by the same penalties the employees or agents of the pawnshop, who shall have revealed to persons other than police officers or the judicial authority the names of persons who have deposited or caused to be deposited objects at the establishment.

###### Art. 460

Whoever shall be convicted of having suppressed a letter entrusted (to a postal operator), or of having opened it in order to violate its secrecy, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one month and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only, without prejudice to more severe penalties, if the offender is a public official or an agent of the government or

(a member of the staff of a postal operator or any person acting on its behalf). <AR 1999-06-09/57, art. 27, 026; In force: 18-08-1999> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 460bis.<L 14-01-1928, art. 4> (Shall be punished by the same penalties, whoever shall have suppressed a copy of a writ of which he was the holder by application of article 68bis of the Code of Civil Procedure or who shall have opened, in order to violate its secrecy, the envelope containing that copy, unless, in the latter case, it concerns the father or mother of a minor child, or the spouse, the guardian, the administrator, [1 or the curator]1 of the person concerned.)

(1)	<L 2013-03-17/14, art. 151, 101; In force: 01-09-2014 (L 2014-05-12/02,

###### art. 22)>

Art. 460ter.<inserted by L 1998-03-12/39, art. 44; In force: 1998-10-02> Any use [1 ...]1 of information obtained by [1 consulting or obtaining a copy
of the]1 file, which has had the purpose and effect of obstructing the course of the investigation, of infringing the privacy, physical or moral integrity or property of a person mentioned in the file is punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year or a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

(1)	<L 2012-12-27/29, art. 27, 090; In force: 10-02-2013>

### TITLE IX. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PROPERTY

#### CHAPTER I. - THEFTS AND EXTORTIONS

###### Art. 461

Whoever has fraudulently removed a thing which does not belong to him, is guilty of theft.
(The act of fraudulently removing another person's thing with a view to momentary use is assimilated to theft.) <L 25-06-1964, art. 1>

###### Art. 462

Thefts committed by spouses to the prejudice of their spouses; by a widower or a widow, as regards things which had belonged to the deceased spouse; by descendants to the prejudice

of their ascendants, by ascendants to the prejudice of their descendants, or by relatives in the same degrees, shall only give rise to civil reparations.
[1 Paragraph 1 is not applicable if these thefts have been committed to the prejudice of a person vulnerable by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency.]1
Any other person who has participated in these thefts or received all or part of the stolen objects shall be punished as if [1 paragraph 1]1 did not exist.

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 37, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

##### SECTION I. - THEFTS COMMITTED WITHOUT VIOLENCE OR THREATS

###### Art. 463

Thefts not specified in this chapter shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.
(However, in the case provided for by article 461, paragraph 2, the term of imprisonment shall not exceed three years.) <L 25-06-1964, art. 2>
[1 The minimum penalty shall be three months' imprisonment and a fine of fifty euros if the theft has been committed to the prejudice of a person whose particularly vulnerable situation by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness or a physical or mental deficiency or infirmity was apparent or known to the perpetrator.]1

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 38, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 464

The imprisonment shall be at least three months, if the thief is a domestic servant or a hired servant, even when he has committed the theft against persons whom he did not serve, but who were either in the master's house, or in the house to which he accompanied him, or if he is a worker, journeyman or apprentice, in the house, workshop or shop of his master, or an individual habitually working in the dwelling where he has stolen.

###### Art. 465

In the cases of the preceding articles, the guilty parties may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33 (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 466

Attempts at the thefts mentioned in the preceding articles shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 467. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Theft shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years): <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 75, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>
If it has been committed by means of breaking and entering, scaling or false keys; If it has been committed by a public official by means of his functions;
If the guilty parties or one of them have assumed the title or insignia of a public official, or have alleged a false order of the public authority.

##### SECTION II. - THEFTS COMMITTED BY MEANS OF VIOLENCE OR THREATS AND EXTORTIONS

###### Art. 468. (See NOTE under TITLE)

Whoever has committed a theft by means of violence or threats shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 75, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 469

The case where the thief, caught in the act, has used violence or made threats, either to maintain possession of the objects removed, or to ensure his flight, is assimilated to theft committed by means of violence or threats.

###### Art. 470

Whoever has extorted, by means of violence or threats, either funds, valuables, movable objects, obligations, notes, promises, receipts, or the signature or delivery of any document whatsoever containing or effecting an obligation, disposition or discharge, shall be punished by the penalties provided for in article 468, as if he had committed a theft with violence or threats.

###### Art. 471.(See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2001-12-11/50, art. 3, 033; In force: 17-02-2002> In the cases provided for in articles 468, 469 and 470 the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years:
if the offence has been committed with breaking and entering, scaling or false keys;
if the offence has been committed by a public official by means of his functions; if the guilty parties, or one of them, have assumed the title or insignia of a public official or have alleged a false order of the public authority;

if the offence has been committed at night;
if the offence has been committed by two or more persons;
if the guilty party has used a vehicle or any other motorised or non-motorised device to facilitate the offence or to ensure his flight;
[1 if the offence has been committed to the prejudice of a person whose particularly vulnerable situation by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness or a physical or mental deficiency or infirmity was apparent or known to the perpetrator.]1

(1)	<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 39, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 472. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2001-12-11/50, art. 4, 033; In force: 17-02-2002> In the cases provided for in articles 468, 469 and 470 the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years:
if the offence has been committed with two of the circumstances mentioned in article 471;
if weapons or objects resembling them have been used or shown, or if the guilty party has made it believed that he was armed;
if the guilty party has used inhibiting or toxic substances to commit the offence or ensure his flight;
if, to facilitate the offence or to ensure his flight, the guilty party has used a vehicle or any other motorised or non-motorised device, obtained by means of a crime or an offence;
if, to facilitate the offence or to ensure his flight, the guilty party has used a motor vehicle or any other motorised device fitted with insignia or devices capable of creating confusion with a motor vehicle or any other motorised device belonging to the law enforcement forces.

###### Art. 473.(See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 02-07-1975, art. 4> In the cases provided for in articles 468, 469, 470 and 471, the penalty shall be (imprisonment) of fifteen years to twenty years, if the violence or threats have caused, either an apparently incurable illness, or [1 an incapacity for personal work of more than four months]1, or the complete loss of the use of an organ, or a serious mutilation. <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 8, 036; In force: 24-08-2002>
The same penalty shall be applied if the offenders have subjected the persons to (acts referred to in article 417ter, paragraph one); <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 8, 036; In

force: 24-08-2002>
In the cases provided for in article 472, the penalty shall be increased (to imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years). <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 8, 036; In force: 24-08-2002>

(1)	<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>


This excerpt sits under: PENAL CODE > BOOK 2. - OFFENCES AND THEIR PUNISHMENT IN PARTICULAR > TITLE VIII. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PERSONS > CHAPTER VI. - CERTAIN OTHER OFFENCES AGAINST PERSONS

###### Art. 474. (See NOTE under TITLE)

If violence or threats exercised without intent to cause death have nevertheless caused it, the guilty parties shall be sentenced (to imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 76, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>
(Paragraph 2 repealed) <L 02-07-1975, art. 6>

###### Art. 475

Murder committed to facilitate theft or extortion, or to ensure impunity therefor, shall be punished (by life imprisonment). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; In force: 11-08-1996>

###### Art. 476

The penalties provided for by [1 articles 473 to 475]1 shall be applied, even if the completion of the theft or extortion has been prevented by circumstances independent of the will of the guilty parties.

(1)	<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 28, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

SECTION IIbis. - (THEFTS AND EXTORTIONS RELATING TO NUCLEAR MATERIALS). <L 17-04-1986, art. 2>

###### Art. 477. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 17-04-1986, art. 2> Theft of nuclear materials is punished by (imprisonment from five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 77, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

Art. 477bis. (See NOTE under TITLE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 2> Theft of nuclear materials is punished (by imprisonment) from ten years to fifteen years: <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 78, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>
1° if it has been committed with the aid of violence or threats; 2° if it has been committed with breaking and entering, scaling or false keys;
3° if it has been committed by a public official by means of his functions; 4° if the guilty parties or one of them, have assumed the title or insignia of a

public official or have alleged a false order of the public authority.

Art. 477ter. (See NOTE under TITLE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 2> Extortion of nuclear materials by means of violence or threats is punished (by imprisonment) from ten years to fifteen years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 78, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

Art. 477quater. <L 17-04-1986, art. 2> Assimilated to theft or extortion of nuclear materials committed by means of violence or threats, is the case where the thief or extortioner, caught in the act, has exercised violence or made threats, either to maintain possession of the nuclear materials taken, or to ensure his escape.

Art. 477quinquies. (See NOTE under TITLE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 2> Theft or extortion of nuclear materials by means of violence or threats, as well as the act referred to in article 477quater, are punished (by imprisonment) from fifteen to twenty years:
<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 78, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>
1° if they have been committed with breaking and entering, scaling or false keys;
2° if they have been committed by a public official by means of his functions; 3° if the guilty parties, or one of them, have assumed the title or insignia of a public official or have alleged a false order of the public authority;
4° if they have been committed at night;
5° if they have been committed by two or more persons;
6° if the guilty party has used a vehicle or any other motorised or non-motorised device to facilitate the extortion or to ensure his escape.

Art. 477sexies.(See NOTE under TITLE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 2> § 1. Theft or extortion of nuclear materials by means of violence or threats, as well as the act referred to in article 477quater, are punished (by imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years): <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 79, 041;In force: 13-03-2003>
1° if they have been committed with two of the circumstances mentioned in article 477quinquies;
2° if weapons or objects resembling them have been used or shown or if the guilty party has made it believed that he was armed;
3° if the guilty party has used inhibitive or toxic substances to commit the act or to ensure his escape;
4° if the guilty party has used a stolen vehicle or any other motorised or non-motorised device,

which has been stolen to facilitate the act or to ensure his escape;
5° if, to facilitate the act or to ensure his escape, the guilty party has used a motor vehicle or any other motorised device fitted with insignia or devices capable of creating confusion with a motor vehicle or any other motorised device belonging to law enforcement forces.

###### § 2. The same acts are punished by the same penalty

1° if the violence or threats have caused, either an apparently incurable illness, or [1 incapacity for personal work of more than four months]1, or the complete loss of use of an organ, or serious mutilation;
2° if the offenders (have performed on persons acts referred to in article 417ter, first paragraph;) <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 9, 036; In force: 24-08-2002> 3° if the violence or threats exercised without intent to cause death, have nevertheless caused it.

###### § 3

The penalty provided for by § 2 is applied even if the completion of the theft or extortion has been prevented by circumstances independent of the will of the guilty parties.

(1)	<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

##### SECTION III. - (MEANING OF CERTAIN TERMS USED IN THIS CODE)

<L 02-07-1975, art. 5>

###### Art. 478

Theft committed at night is theft committed more than one hour before sunrise and more than one hour after sunset.

###### Art. 479

Is deemed an inhabited house, any building, any apartment, any dwelling, any lodge, any cabin, even mobile, or other place used for habitation.

###### Art. 480

Are deemed dependencies of an inhabited house, courtyards, farmyards, gardens and all other enclosed land, as well as barns, stables and all other buildings enclosed therein, whatever their use, even if they form a separate enclosure within the general enclosure.

###### Art. 481

Mobile pens intended to contain livestock in the countryside, in whatever manner they are made, are deemed dependencies of an inhabited house

when they are established on the same piece of land, with mobile cabins or other shelters intended for the guards.

###### Art. 482

Included in the word weapons, are the objects designated in article 135 of this code.

###### Art. 483

By violence the law means acts of physical constraint exercised on persons.
By threats the law means all means of moral constraint through fear of imminent harm.

###### Art. 484

Breaking and entering consists in forcing, breaking, damaging, demolishing or removing any kind of exterior or interior enclosure of a house, building, any construction or its dependencies, of a boat, of a railway carriage, of a vehicle; in forcing cupboards or closed furniture, intended to remain in place and to protect the effects they contain.

###### Art. 485. Are assimilated to theft with breaking and entering

The removal of the furniture referred to in the preceding article; Theft committed by means of breaking seals.

###### Art. 486. Is qualified as scaling

Any entry into houses, buildings, courtyards, farmyards, any buildings, gardens, parks, enclosures, executed over walls, doors, roofs or any other kind of enclosure;
Entry through an underground opening other than that which has been established to serve as an entrance.

###### Art. 487. Are qualified as false keys

All hooks, lock picks, skeleton keys, imitated, counterfeited or altered keys; Keys which have not been intended by the owner, tenant, innkeeper or lodger, for the locks, padlocks or any fastenings to which the guilty party has used them;
Lost, mislaid or stolen keys which have been used to commit the theft. However, the use of false keys shall constitute an aggravating circumstance only if it took place to open objects whose breaking and entering would have resulted in an

aggravation of penalty.

Art. 487bis.[

###### §

1er. By nuclear materials, is meant the nuclear materials referred to in Article 1, 8th indent, of the Act of 15 April 1994 on the protection of the population and the environment against the dangers resulting from ionising radiation and on the Federal Agency for Nuclear Control.

###### § 2

By radioactive material, is meant any nuclear material or other radioactive substance containing nuclides which disintegrate spontaneously, a process accompanied by the emission of one or more types of ionising radiation such as alpha, beta, gamma and neutron radiation, and which could, by reason of its radiological or fissile properties, cause death, serious bodily injury or substantial damage to property or to the environment.

###### § 3. By nuclear facility is meant

a)	any nuclear reactor, including a reactor installed on board a vessel, vehicle, aircraft or spacecraft as a source of energy for propelling such vessel, vehicle, aircraft or spacecraft, or for any other purpose;
b)	any device or means of transport for the purpose of producing, storing, reprocessing or transporting radioactive materials.

###### § 4. By device is meant

a)	any nuclear explosive device; or
b)	any radioactive material dispersal device or any radiation-emitting device which, by reason of its radiological properties, causes death, serious bodily injury or substantial damage to property or to the environment.

###### § 5

By operator of a facility where nuclear materials are produced, processed, used, handled, stored or definitively disposed of, is meant any natural or legal person who assumes responsibility for such a facility.

###### § 6

By person external to a facility where nuclear materials are produced, processed, used, handled, stored or definitively disposed of, is meant the natural person who is not bound, directly or otherwise, by an employment contract, a traineeship or training agreement or a contract for the provision of works or services, to a facility where nuclear materials are produced, processed, used, handled, stored or definitively disposed of.]1

(1)<L 2013-05-23/09, art. 3, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

DISPOSITION PARTICULIERE.

###### Art. 488

Whoever fraudulently counterfeits or alters keys shall be sentenced to imprisonment from three months to two years and to a fine from twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
If the offender is a locksmith by profession, he shall be punished by imprisonment from two years to five years and by a fine from two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

CHAPITRE Ibis. - [1 On the physical protection of nuclear materials and other radioactive materials]1

(1)<L 2013-05-23/09, art. 4, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

Art. 488bis.(Voir NOTE sous TITRE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 4> § 1. Whoever, intentionally and without being authorised by the competent authority or outside the conditions provided for upon authorisation, has delivered to him, acquires, possesses, uses, alters, transfers, abandons, transports or disperses nuclear materials is punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 80, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### § 2

The penalty is (imprisonment) of ten years to fifteen years if the act has caused to another: <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 80, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>
1° either an apparently incurable illness, [2 incapacity for personal work of more than four months]2, the loss of the absolute use of an organ or a serious mutilation;
2° The destruction in whole or in part of buildings, bridges, dykes, roads, railways, locks, warehouses, yards, sheds, ships, boats, aircraft or other works of art, or constructions belonging to another.

###### § 3

If the act committed without intention to cause death has nevertheless caused it, the offender is punished by (imprisonment) of fifteen to twenty years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 80, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>
[

###### §

4. Is punished by imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years, whoever,

intentionally and without being authorised by the competent authority or outside the conditions provided for upon authorisation, commits an act directed against nuclear materials or against a facility in which nuclear materials are produced, processed, used, handled, stored or definitively disposed of, or an act disrupting the operation of such a facility, if, by these acts and as a result of exposure to radiation or the release of radioactive substances:
1° he intentionally causes or knows that he may cause death or serious injury to another or substantial damage to property or to the environment; or
2° he intentionally compels a natural or legal person, an international organisation or a government to perform an act or to refrain from doing so.]1

(1)<L 2013-05-23/09, art. 5, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>
(2)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 20, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 488ter. [1 Is punished by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years, whoever, intentionally and without being authorised by the competent authority or outside the conditions provided for upon authorisation, possesses, manufactures, uses in any manner radioactive materials other than nuclear or radioactive devices or commits an act directed against radioactive materials other than nuclear or radioactive devices, if, by these acts:
1° he intentionally causes or knows that he may cause death or serious injury to another or substantial damage to property or to the environment; or
2° he intentionally compels a natural or legal person, an international organisation or a government to perform an act or to refrain from doing so.]1

(1)<Inséré par L 2013-05-23/09, art. 6, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

Art. 488quater. [1 Is punished by imprisonment of five to ten years, whoever demands, intentionally and without being authorised by the competent authority or outside the conditions provided for upon authorisation, the delivery of radioactive materials or devices or of nuclear facilities by resorting to threat, in

circumstances which make it credible, or to the use of force.]1

(1)<Inséré par L 2013-05-23/09, art. 7, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

Art. 488quinquies. [1 Is punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and by a fine of one hundred euros to fifty thousand euros or by one of these penalties only, any person external to a facility where nuclear materials are produced, processed, used, handled, stored or definitively disposed of who, without order of the authority and outside the cases where the law permits it, enters or attempts to enter the parts of such a facility for which access is limited to the persons referred to in Article 8bis, §§ 1 to 4, of the Act of 11 December 1998 on classification and security clearances, certificates and opinions, either without having been authorised by the operator or his agent, or by resorting to fraudulent manoeuvres likely to mislead the operator or his agent as to his legitimacy to enter those parts of the facility.]1

(1)<Inséré par L 2013-05-23/09, art. 8, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

#### CHAPITRE II. - DES FRAUDES

##### SECTION I. [1 - Offences related to insolvency.]1

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 4, 128; En vigueur : 01-05-2018>

###### Art. 489.(See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 1997-08-08/80, art. 118, 019; In force: 01-01-1998> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of one hundred [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only,
[1 enterprises referred to in [2 Article I.1, paragraph 1, 1°]2, of the Code of Economic Law or the directors, de jure or de facto, of companies or legal persons]1 in a state of bankruptcy, who have: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
1° contracted, for the benefit of third parties, without sufficient consideration, commitments too considerable having regard to the financial situation of the enterprise;
2° without legitimate impediment, failed to perform the obligations prescribed by Article [1 XX.146 of the Code of Economic Law]1.

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 5, 128; In force: 01-05-2018>
(2)<L 2018-04-15/14, art. 4, 135; In force: 01-11-2018>

Art. 489bis.<inserted by L 1997-08-08/80, art. 119, 019; In force: 01-01-1998> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only, the persons referred to in Article 489, who have: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
1° with the intention of delaying the declaration of bankruptcy, made purchases for resale below the market price or who have resorted to loans, circulation of bills and other ruinous means of procuring funds;
2° simulated expenses or losses or have not been able to justify the existence or use of all or part of the assets, as they appear from the accounting documents and books at the date of cessation of payment and of all property of any nature whatsoever obtained subsequently;
3° with the intention of delaying the declaration of bankruptcy, paid or favoured a creditor to the prejudice of the estate;
4° with the same intention, failed to make the declaration of bankruptcy within the time limit prescribed by Article [1 XX.102 of the Code of Economic Law]1; knowingly failed to provide, on the occasion of the declaration of bankruptcy, the information required by Article [1 XX.103 of the same Code]1; knowingly provided inaccurate information on the occasion of the declaration of bankruptcy or subsequently to requests addressed by the examining judge or by the trustees.

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 6, 128; In force: 01-05-2018>

Art. 489ter.<inserted by L 1997-08-08/80, art. 120, 019; In force: 01-01-1998> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to five years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros], the persons referred to in Article 489 who, with fraudulent intent or with intent to harm, have:
1° misappropriated or concealed part of the assets; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
2° removed, in whole or in part, accounting books or documents referred to in
[1 Book III, Chapter 2, of the Code of Economic Law]1; the attempt of these offences is punished by imprisonment of one month to three years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-

2002>
Those guilty of these offences or of their attempt may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with Article 33.

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 7, 128; In force: 01-05-2018>

Art. 489quater.<inserted by L 1997-08-08/80, art. 121, 019; In force: 01-01-1998> The public prosecution relating to the offences referred to in Articles 489, 489bis and 489ter is pursued independently of any action that could be pursued before the commercial court. The state of bankruptcy may not, however, be contested before the criminal judge, if that state has been the subject of a decision of the commercial court or of the Court of Appeal, having acquired the force of res judicata, at the end of proceedings in which the accused was a party, either personally or as representative of [1 the enterprise]1 in bankruptcy.

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 8, 128; In force: 01-05-2018>

Art. 489quinquies.<inserted by L 1997-08-08/80, art. 122, 019; In force: 01-01-1998> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only, those who, fraudulently, have: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
1° in the interest [1 of the enterprise in bankruptcy]1 even in the absence of intervention by [1 the latter or the directors, de jure or de facto, of that company or legal person]1, removed, concealed or received all or part of the assets;
2° presented in the bankruptcy and affirmed, either in their own name or through the interposition of persons, fictitious or exaggerated claims.

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 9, 128; In force: 01-05-2018>

Art. 489sexies. <inserted by L 1997-08-08/80, art. 123, 019; In force: 01-01-1998> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to five years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros], the trustee who has been guilty of malfeasance in his management. He is, in addition, sentenced to restitution and damages due to the estate of creditors. The guilty party may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with Article 33. <L 2000-06-26/42, art.
2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 490

<L 1997-08-08/80, art. 124, 019; En vigueur : 01-01-1998> Courts pronouncing a conviction to a sentence of imprisonment pursuant to articles 489, 489bis, and 489ter, shall order that their decisions be published, by extract, at the expense of the convicted person, in the Moniteur belge.
This extract contains:
1° the surname, first names, place and date of birth as well as the address and [1 enterprise]1 number of the convicted persons and, where applicable, the trade name or denomination and registered office of the [2 enterprises]2 declared bankrupt of which they are the de jure or de facto directors;
2° the date of the judgment or decision of conviction and the court which pronounced it;
3° the offences which gave rise to the convictions and the sentences pronounced; where, by reason of unity of intention, a single sentence has been pronounced for one of the aforementioned offences and other offences, all the offences punished by that single sentence shall be mentioned.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/14, art. 2, 077; En vigueur : 25-01-2010>
(2)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 10, 128; En vigueur : 01-05-2018>

Art. 490bis. <L 1997-08-08/50, art. 141, 019; En vigueur : 01-01-1998> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only, whoever has fraudulently organised his insolvency and has not performed the obligations by which he is bound. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> The organisation of his insolvency by the debtor may be inferred from any circumstance of a nature to reveal his intention to render himself insolvent.
With regard to the third party co-author or accomplice of the offence, the public prosecution is extinguished if he restores the property which had been handed over to him.

Art. 490ter. [1 The debtor shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of five euros to one hundred twenty-five thousand euros or by one of these penalties only:
1° if, in order to obtain or facilitate the judicial reorganisation procedure, he has, in any manner whatsoever, voluntarily concealed part of his assets or liabilities, or exaggerated those assets or minimised those liabilities;

2° if he has knowingly and voluntarily caused or allowed one or more supposed creditors or creditors whose claims have been exaggerated to participate in the deliberations;
3° if he has knowingly and voluntarily omitted one or more creditors from the list of creditors;
4° if he has knowingly and voluntarily made or caused to be made to the court or to an officer of justice inaccurate or incomplete statements concerning the state of his affairs or concerning the prospects for reorganisation.]1

(1)<Inséré par L 2017-08-11/14, art. 11, 128; En vigueur : 01-05-2018>

Art. 490quater. [1 Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of 5 euros to 125,000 euros, (i) those who have fraudulently, without being creditors, taken part in the vote provided for in article XX.78 of the Code of Economic Law, (ii) those who, being creditors, have exaggerated their claims and (iii) those who have stipulated, either with the debtor or with any other persons, particular advantages to influence the direction of their vote on the reorganisation plan or who have concluded a particular agreement under which would result in their favour an advantage chargeable to the debtor's assets.]1

(1)<Inséré par L 2017-08-11/14, art. 12, 128; En vigueur : 01-05-2018>

##### SECTION II. - DES ABUS DE CONFIANCE

###### Art. 491

Whoever shall have fraudulently either misappropriated or dissipated to the prejudice of another, effects, monies, goods, bills, receipts, writings of any nature containing or creating an obligation or discharge and which had been handed over to him on condition of returning them or making a determined use or employment thereof, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> The guilty party may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 492

The provision of article 462 shall be applicable to the offence provided for by the preceding article.

Art. 492bis. <inséré par L 1997-08-08/80, art. 142, En vigueur : 01-01-1998> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to five years and a fine of one hundred [euros] (to five hundred thousand [euros]), the de jure or de facto directors of commercial and civil companies as well as non-profit associations who, with fraudulent intent and for personal purposes, directly or indirectly, have made use of the property or credit of the legal person which they knew to be significantly prejudicial to the patrimonial interests thereof and to those of its creditors or members. <Erratum, voir M.B. 07.02.2001, p. 3294 > <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
The guilty parties may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 493

(Shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], whoever shall have abused the needs, weaknesses, (passions or ignorance) of a minor [1 or of a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts,]1 in order to make him subscribe, to his prejudice, obligations, receipts, discharges, commercial instruments or any other obligatory instruments, in whatever form that negotiation may have been made or disguised.)
<AR148 18-03-1935, art. 1> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
The guilty party may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 40, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 494

<AR148 18-03-1935, art. 2> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of one thousand [euros] to ten thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only, whoever, habitually abusing the weaknesses or passions of the borrower, has, by reason of a loan of a sum of money, contracted in any form whatsoever, caused to be promised, for himself or for another, an interest or other advantages exceeding the legal interest. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
Shall be punished by the same penalties, whoever, habitually abusing the needs or ignorance of the borrower, has, by reason of a loan of a sum of money, contracted in any form whatsoever, caused to be promised, for himself or for another, an interest or other advantages manifestly exceeding the normal interest and the coverage of the risks of that loan.
In the cases provided for in this article, the judge, at the request of any injured party, shall reduce his obligations in accordance with article 1907ter of the Civil Code.

###### Art. 495

Whoever, after having produced, in a judicial dispute, any title, document or memorandum, shall have maliciously or fraudulently misappropriated it, in any manner whatsoever, shall be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
This penalty shall be pronounced by the court seised of the dispute.

Art. 495bis. <L 10-10-1967, art. 144> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only, whoever, being the holder of a document the production of which in court has been ordered by a judgment, shall have fraudulently destroyed, altered or concealed that document. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

##### SECTION III. - FRAUD AND DECEPTION

###### Art. 496

Whoever, with the intent to appropriate a thing belonging to another, shall have obtained or had delivered to them funds, movables, obligations, receipts, discharges, either by using false names or false qualities, or by employing fraudulent manoeuvres to persuade the existence of false enterprises, of an imaginary power or credit, to create the hope or fear of a success, an accident or any other chimerical event, or to otherwise abuse confidence or credulity, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
[1 If the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph have been committed against a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts, the latter shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of twenty-six euros to three

thousand euros.]1
(The attempt of the offence provided for in paragraph 1 shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two thousand [euros].) <L 1993-06-16/35, art. 1, 1°, 009; In force: 03-08-1993> <L 2000-06-26/42, art.
2, In force: 01-01-2002>
(In the cases provided for in the preceding paragraphs, the offender may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.) <L 1993-06-16/35, art. 1, 2°, 009; In force: 03-08-1993>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 41, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 497

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three years and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
(Those who shall have, with fraudulent intent, given or attempted to give to a coin having legal tender in Belgium or abroad the appearance of a coin of superior value;
Those who shall have issued or attempted to issue coins to which the appearance of coins of a superior value has been given or who, with the aim of putting them into circulation, shall have introduced them into the country or attempted to introduce them.) <L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 12°>
Those who shall have issued or attempted to issue as coins pieces of metal bearing no monetary imprint.

Art. 497bis. <L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 13°> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], those who, with the aim of putting them into circulation, shall have received or procured coins to which the appearance of coins of a superior value has been given. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The attempt shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force
: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 498

Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only, whoever shall have deceived the purchaser:

As to the identity of the thing sold, by fraudulently delivering a thing other than the specific object on which the transaction related; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
As to the nature or origin of the thing sold by selling or delivering a thing similar in appearance to that which he purchased or believed he was purchasing.

###### Art. 499

<L 17-06-1896, art. unique> Shall be sentenced to imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros] or to one of these penalties only, those who, by fraudulent manoeuvres, shall have deceived: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
1° The purchaser or the seller as to the quantity of things sold;
2° The parties engaged in a contract for the hire of work or one of them, either as to the quantity or as to the quality of work supplied, when, in this second case, the determination of the quality of work must serve to fix the amount of the wage.

###### Art. 500

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only:
<L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
Those who shall have adulterated or caused to be adulterated (foodstuffs) intended to be sold or retailed;
Those who shall have sold, retailed or exposed for sale these articles, knowing that they were adulterated; <L 24-01-1977, art. 24, 1°>
Those who, by posters or by notices, printed or not, shall have maliciously or fraudulently propagated or revealed processes for adulterating these same articles.

###### Art. 501

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only, whoever in whose possession are found (foodstuffs) intended to be sold or retailed, and who knows that they are adulterated. <L 24-01-1977, art. 24, 2°> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 501bis. <L 20-06-1964, art. 15> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six to three hundred [euros] or by one of these penalties only whoever, without the fraudulent intent required by article 500, shall have sold, retailed, or exposed for sale adulterated (foodstuffs).

<L 24-01-1977, art. 24, 3°> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 502

In the cases provided for (by articles 500 and 501), the court may order that the judgment be posted in the places it designates and inserted, in whole or in extract, in the newspapers it indicates; all at the expense of the convicted person. <L 24-01-1977, art. 24, 4°>
(Paragraph 2 repealed) <L 29-10-1919, art. 90>

###### Art. 503

<L 24-01-1977, art. 25> The adulterated foodstuffs found in the possession of the offender shall be seized and confiscated.
However, when the adulteration has the effect of rendering these foodstuffs unfit for consumption and by reason of their nature or condition, they are not capable of preservation, they shall be destroyed or denatured after a sample is taken by the reporting officer, assisted by an official provided for by article 11 of the law relating to the protection of consumer health with regard to foodstuffs and other products, who shall jointly sign the reports of seizure and destruction or denaturing of these foodstuffs. Confiscation shall be pronounced in any event.
Foodstuffs which, notwithstanding the adulteration, remain fit for consumption may be handed over to a social welfare institution dependent on a subordinate administration, either immediately after a sample is taken if they are foodstuffs not capable of preservation, or, if they are capable of preservation, after a judicial decision pronouncing confiscation.

###### Art. 504

The provision of article 462 shall be applicable to the offences provided for by articles 496, 498 and 499.

SECTION IIIBIS. - <Inserted by L 1999-02-10/39, art. 5; In force: 02-04-1999> Private corruption.

Art. 504bis.<Inserted by L 1999-02-10/39, art. 5; In force: 02-04-1999> § 1er. Constitutes passive private corruption the act by a person who has the capacity of director or manager of a legal person, agent or employee of a legal or natural person, [1 to solicit, accept or receive]1, directly or through the interposition of persons, an offer, a

promise or an advantage of any nature, for himself or for a third party, to do or refrain from doing an act of his function or facilitated by his function, without the knowledge and without the authorisation, as the case may be, of the Board of Directors or the General Meeting, the principal or the employer.

###### § 2

Active private corruption consists of the act of proposing, directly or through intermediaries, to a person who has the capacity of director or manager of a legal person, agent or employee of a legal or natural person, an offer, a promise or an advantage of any nature, for themselves or for a third party, to do or refrain from doing an act of their function or facilitated by their function, without the knowledge and without the authorization, as the case may be, of the Board of Directors or the General Meeting, the principal or the employer.

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 29, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 504ter. <Inséré par L 1999-02-10/39, art. 5; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> § 1er. In the case of private corruption, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from 100 [euros] to 10,000 [euros] or one of these penalties. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur :01-01-2002>

###### § 2

In the case where the solicitation referred to in Article 504bis, § 1er, is followed by a proposal referred to in Article 504bis, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in Article 504bis, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros] or one of these penalties. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

SECTION IIIbis. - Computer fraud. <inséré par L 2000-11-28/34, art. 5; En vigueur : 13-02-2001>

Art. 504quater. <inséré par L 2000-11-28/34, art. 5; En vigueur : 13-02-2001>§ 1er. (Whoever seeks to obtain, for themselves or for another, with fraudulent intent, an illegal economic advantage) by introducing into a computer system, by modifying or deleting data that is stored, processed or transmitted by a computer system, or by modifying by any technological means (the normal use) of data in a computer system, is punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties

only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 4, 059; En vigueur : 22-09-2006>

###### § 2

The attempt to commit the offence referred to in § 1er is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to fifty thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### § 3

The penalties provided for in §§ 1er and 2 are doubled if an offence against one of these provisions is committed within five years following the pronouncement of a conviction for one of these offences or for one of the offences referred to in Articles 210bis, 259bis, 314bis or in Title IXbis.

##### SECTION IV. - (Receiving and other operations relating to things derived from an offence.)

<L 1990-07-17/30, art. 4, 004; En vigueur : 25-08-1990>

###### Art. 505

<L 1995-04-07/57, art. 7, 004; En vigueur : 20-05-1995> Shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
1° those who have received, in whole or in part, things removed, misappropriated or obtained by means of a crime or an offence;
2° (those who have purchased, received in exchange or free of charge, possessed, kept or managed things referred to in Article 42, 3°, when they knew or should have known the origin of these things at the beginning of these operations;) <L 2007-05-10/63, art. 2, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>
3° those who have converted or transferred things referred to in Article 42, 3°, for the purpose of concealing or disguising their illicit origin or of helping any person who is involved in the commission of the offence from which these things originate, to escape the legal consequences of his acts;
4° (those who have concealed or disguised the nature, origin, location, disposition, movement or ownership of things referred to in Article 42, 3°, when they knew or should have known the origin of these things at the beginning of these operations.) <L 2007-05-10/63, art. 2, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>
(The offences referred to in paragraph 1, 3° and 4°, exist even if their perpetrator is also the perpetrator, co-perpetrator or accomplice of the offence from which the things referred to in Article 42, 3° originate. The offences referred to in paragraph 1, 1° and 2°, exist even if their perpetrator is also the perpetrator, co-perpetrator or accomplice of

the offence from which the things referred to in Article 42, 3° originate, when this offence was committed abroad and cannot be prosecuted in Belgium.)
<L 2007-05-10/63, art. 2, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>
(Except with regard to the perpetrator, co-perpetrator or accomplice of the offence from which the things referred to in Article 42, 3° originate, the offences referred to in paragraph 1, 2° and 4°, relate exclusively, in tax matters, to acts committed in the context [1 of serious tax fraud, whether organised or not]1.
The bodies and persons referred to in Articles 2, 2bis and 2ter of the Act of 11 January 1993 on the prevention of the use of the financial system for the purposes of money laundering and terrorist financing, may rely on the preceding paragraph insofar as, with regard to the acts referred to therein, they have complied with the obligation provided for in [1 Article 28]1 of the Act of 11 January 1993 which regulates the procedures for communicating information to the Financial Information Processing Unit.) <L 2007-05-10/63, art. 2, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>
The things referred to (in paragraph 1, 1°) of this article constitute the object (of the offence covered by this provision), within the meaning of Article 42, 1°, and shall be confiscated, even if the ownership thereof does not belong to the convicted person, without (this penalty) being able however to prejudice the rights of third parties over the property liable to confiscation. <L 2007-05-10/63, art. 2, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>
