(The things referred to in paragraph 1, 3° and 4°, constitute the object of the offences covered by these provisions, within the meaning of Article 42, 1°, and shall be confiscated, in respect of each of the perpetrators, co-perpetrators or accomplices of these offences, even if the ownership thereof does not belong to the convicted person, without this penalty being able however to prejudice the rights of third parties over the property liable to confiscation. If these things cannot be found in the patrimony of the convicted person, the judge shall proceed to their monetary valuation and the confiscation shall relate to a sum of money equivalent thereto. In this case, the judge may however reduce this sum in order not to subject the convicted person to an unreasonably heavy penalty.
The things referred to in paragraph 1, 2°, of this article constitute the object of the offence covered by this provision, within the meaning of Article 42, 1°, and shall be confiscated, in respect of each of the perpetrators, co-perpetrators or accomplices of these offences, even if the ownership thereof does not belong to the convicted person, without this penalty being able however to prejudice the rights of third parties over the property

liable to confiscation. If these things cannot be found in the patrimony of the convicted person, the judge shall proceed to their monetary valuation and the confiscation shall relate to a sum of money proportional to the participation of the convicted person in the offence.) <L 2007-05-10/63, art. 2, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>
The attempt of the offences referred to in 2°, 3° and 4° of this article shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to fifty thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
Persons punished pursuant to these provisions may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with Article 33.

(1)<L 2013-07-15/02, art. 15, 094; En vigueur : 29-07-2013>

Art. 505bis. <inserted by L 2005-08-10/62, art. 7 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005> Those who have received, in whole or in part, things removed, misappropriated or obtained by means of the offence or crime referred to in Article 433, shall be punished by the penalties provided for in Article 505, paragraph 1, of which the minimum penalty is increased in the case of imprisonment to three months and in the case of a fine to one thousand euros.<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 81, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 506

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 81, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003> In the case where the penalty applicable to the perpetrators of the crime is that of imprisonment for life or imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years, (the receivers referred to in Articles 505 and 505bis) shall be sentenced to imprisonment of five years to ten years, if they are convicted of having had, at the time of the receiving, knowledge of the circumstances to which the law attaches either imprisonment for life or imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years. <LW 2005-08-10/62, art. 8, 054 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005>

##### SECTION V. - CERTAIN OTHER FRAUDS

###### Art. 507

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], the person whose property has been seized and all those who have fraudulently destroyed or misappropriated, in his interest, objects seized from him.
<L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
(The same provision is applicable to the spouse or to those who in his interest

destroy, damage or misappropriate movables which have been the subject of a measure provided for (in Article 223 of the Civil Code) (and in Articles 1253septies, second paragraph, and 1280 of the Judicial Code.)) <L 1990-04-09/35, art. 1, 003; En vigueur : 19-06-1990>

Art. 507bis. <inserted by L 1998-03-12/39, art. 45 ; En vigueur : 1998-10-02> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], the person who does not comply with the conditions set for the lifting of an act of information or instruction, in accordance with Articles 28sexies and 61quater of the Code of Criminal Procedure. <L 2000-06-26/42, art.
2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>


This excerpt sits under: PENAL CODE > BOOK 2. - OFFENCES AND THEIR PUNISHMENT IN PARTICULAR > TITLE IX. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PROPERTY > CHAPTER II. - FRAUDS > SECTION IV. - (Receiving and other operations relating to things derived from an offence.)

###### Art. 508

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
Those who, having found a movable thing belonging to another or having obtained possession of it by chance, shall have fraudulently concealed it or delivered it to third parties;
Those who, having discovered a treasure, shall have appropriated it to the prejudice of the persons to whom the law attributes a part of it.

Art. 508bis. <L 23-03-1936, art. unique> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of two hundred to fifteen hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only, whoever, knowing that he is in absolute impossibility of paying, shall have had served to him, in an establishment intended for that purpose, beverages or food which he shall have consumed in whole or in part, shall have been given lodging in a travellers' hotel or an inn, or shall have hired a rental vehicle. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
In case of recidivism, the penalties may be doubled. (Paragraph 3 repealed) <L 17-12-1963, art. 2>

Art. 508ter. <L 17-12-1963, art. 1> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of two hundred to one thousand five hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only, whoever, after having had a vehicle supplied with fuel or lubricant, shall have fraudulently absconded from immediate payment. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

In case of recidivism, the penalties may be doubled.

###### Art. 509

Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three thousand [euros], whoever shall have fraudulently procured funds, securities or discharges by means of a bill drawn on a person who does not exist or whom he knew not to be his debtor or not to be so at maturity, and who had not authorized him to draw on him. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
However, prosecution may not take place, or shall cease, if the bill has been paid, or if the funds have been provided at the time the fraud was discovered, unless the drawee has filed a complaint.
In that case, the offender shall be sentenced to imprisonment of fifteen days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], or to one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 509bis. <L 02-05-1956, art. 28> Is punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of twenty-six to three thousand [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
1° Whoever knowingly issues without sufficient and available provision, a postal cheque or a postal transfer;
2° Whoever transfers one of these instruments, knowing that the provision is not sufficient and available;
3° Whoever, having issued one of these instruments, knowingly withdraws, within six months of their issue, all or part of their provision;
4° Whoever, having issued one of these instruments, renders, with fraudulent intent or with intent to harm, all or part of the provision unavailable.

Art. 509ter. <L 31-03-1958, art. 3> Is punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
1° Whoever, after having endorsed an invoice, knowingly collects it for his own benefit;
2° Whoever, after having endorsed the original or a duplicate of an invoice, knowingly has funds remitted to him or has any advantage attributed to him, by means of the endorsement of another original copy or duplicate of the same invoice; 3° Whoever has funds remitted to him or has any advantage attributed to him

by knowingly endorsing an invoice relating to a legally extinguished obligation.

Art. 509quater. <L 2007-05-15/62, art. 33, 072; In force: 01-09-2007> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of two hundred euros to fifteen hundred euros, or by one of these penalties only, the expert who, knowing that a direct payment is not authorized, accepts it nevertheless from a party to the case.

#### CHAPITRE III. - DESTRUCTIONS, DEGRADATIONS, DOMMAGES

##### SECTION I. - DE L'INCENDIE

###### Art. 510. (See NOTE under TITRE)

<L 07-06-1963, art. 3> Shall be punished by (imprisonment for a term) of fifteen years to twenty years those who shall have set fire to buildings, bridges, dikes, roads, railways, locks, warehouses, yards, sheds, ships, boats, carriages, wagons, aircraft or other works of art, constructions or motor vehicles, if the perpetrator should have presumed that one or more persons were present therein at the time of the fire. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 82, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 511

<L 07-06-1963, art. 4> Shall be punished by (imprisonment for a term) of ten years to fifteen years, those who shall have set fire to the immovable properties designated in article 510, as well as to ships, boats and aircraft, but outside the cases provided for by that article, or to forests, woods, coppices or standing crops. <L 2003-01-23/42, art.
82, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>
However, if these properties belong exclusively to those who set fire to them, and if the fire was set with malicious or fraudulent intent, the offenders shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 512

<L 07-06-1963, art. 5> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros], those who shall have voluntarily set fire to the movable properties of another, other than ships, boats and aircraft, and on condition that the act was of a nature to

cause serious prejudice to another. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force
: 01-01-2002>
If the movable properties belong exclusively to those who set fire to them and if the fire was set with malicious or fraudulent intent, the penalties shall be imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 513. (See NOTE under TITRE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 83, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> When the fire shall have been set during the night, the penalties provided in articles 510 to 512 shall be replaced:
imprisonment for a term of fifteen years to twenty years, by imprisonment for a term of twenty years to thirty years; imprisonment for a term of ten years to fifteen years, by imprisonment for a term of fifteen years to twenty years; the imprisonment and fine, provided in article 511, second paragraph, and in article 512, first paragraph, by imprisonment for a term of five years to ten years; the imprisonment and fine, provided in article 512, second paragraph, by imprisonment of one year to four years and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros] in article 512, second paragraph, by imprisonment of one year to four years and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 514

Where arson carries a penalty of imprisonment, attempted arson shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 514bis.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 39, 064; In force: 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by articles 510 to 514, the minimum penalties provided for by these articles may be doubled if they are correctional penalties and increased by two years if they are imprisonment, where one of the motives for the crime or offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person on the grounds of their alleged race, skin colour, descent, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, [1 trade union conviction,]1 a physical or

genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 115, 074; In force: 31-12-2009>

###### Art. 515

In the cases provided for by the preceding articles, the offender sentenced to imprisonment may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33 (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 516

Anyone who, with the intention of committing one of the acts provided for in articles 510, 511 and 512, has set fire to any objects whatsoever, placed so as to communicate it to the thing he wished to destroy, shall be punished as if he had directly set fire or attempted to set fire to that latter thing.

###### Art. 517

Where the fire is communicated from the object which the offender wished to burn to another object the destruction of which carries a heavier penalty, that latter penalty shall be pronounced, if the two things were placed in such a way that the fire necessarily had to be communicated from one to the other.

###### Art. 518

<L 07-06-1963, art. 7> Where the arson has caused injuries to one or more persons, and the perpetrator of the act should have presumed that they were in the premises set on fire at the time of the crime or offence, the offender shall be sentenced as if those injuries had been inflicted with premeditation, and the penalty which the law attaches thereto shall be applied to the offender if that penalty is heavier than that which he has incurred by reason of the arson.
In the contrary case, that latter penalty shall be increased by two years above the maximum, if it consists of imprisonment (of fifteen years to twenty years or a lesser term). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 84, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>
If the act has caused death, the penalty shall be (imprisonment for life). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; In force: 11-08-1996>

###### Art. 519

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only, arson of the movable or immovable property of another which has been caused either by the age or lack of repair or cleaning of ovens, chimneys, forges, nearby houses or factories, or by fires lit in the fields, less than one hundred metres from houses, buildings, forests, heaths, woods,

orchards, plantations, hedges, stacks, piles of grain, straw, hay, fodder or any other deposit of combustible materials, or by fires or lights carried or left, or by fireworks lit or set off without sufficient precaution. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 520

<L 07-06-1963, art. 8> Shall be punished by the penalties provided for by the preceding articles and according to the distinctions established therein, those who have destroyed or attempted to destroy, by the effect of an explosion, buildings, bridges, dykes, roads, railways, locks, warehouses, yards, sheds, ships, boats, carriages, wagons, aircraft or other works of art, constructions or motor vehicles.

##### SECTION II

DESTRUCTION OF CONSTRUCTIONS, STEAM ENGINES AND TELEGRAPHIC APPARATUS.

###### Art. 521. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 07-06-1963, art. 9> Whoever has, outside the cases referred to in articles 510 to 520, destroyed, by any means whatsoever, in whole or in part, buildings, bridges, dykes, roads, railways, locks, warehouses, yards, sheds, ships, boats, aircraft or other works of art, or constructions belonging to another, shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 85, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>
In case of putting out of use with intent to harm, the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen days to three years and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The penalty provided for in the second paragraph is applicable in case of destruction, in whole or in part, or putting out of use with intent to harm, of carriages, wagons and motor vehicles.

###### Art. 522

The provision of article 518 shall be applicable to the case provided for by the preceding article.

###### Art. 523

<L 07-06-1963, art. 10> Whoever has destroyed a machine belonging to another, intended to produce, transform or distribute motive power or to consume it for purposes other than purely domestic, shall be sentenced to imprisonment of fifteen days to three years and to a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-

01-2002>
There is destruction as soon as the effects of the machine are prevented in whole or in part, whether the act relates to the motor apparatus, or whether it relates to the apparatus set in motion.

###### Art. 524. (Repealed)

<L 13-10-1930, art. 31>

###### Art. 525. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 86, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> Where the acts provided for by the two preceding articles have been committed in assembly or in a gang and by means of violence, assault or threats, the offenders shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years.
The leaders and instigators shall be sentenced to imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years and to a fine of five hundred [euros] to five thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 525bis.<inserted by L 2007-05-10/35, art. 40; In force: 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by articles 521 to 525, the minimum penalties provided for by these articles may be doubled if they are correctional penalties, and increased by two years if they are imprisonment, where one of the motives for the offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person on the grounds of their alleged race, skin colour, descent, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, [1 trade union conviction,]1 a physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 116, 074; In force: 31-12-2009>

##### SECTION III

DESTRUCTION OR DEGRADATION OF TOMBS, MONUMENTS, OBJECTS OF ART, TITLES, DOCUMENTS OR OTHER PAPERS.

###### Art. 526

Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], whoever has destroyed, demolished, mutilated or degraded: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
Tombs, commemorative signs or sepulchral stones;

Monuments, statues or other objects intended for public utility or decoration and erected by the competent authority or with its authorization;
Monuments, statues, paintings or objects of art whatsoever, placed in churches, temples or other public buildings.

###### Art. 527

Whoever shall maliciously or fraudulently have destroyed in any manner whatsoever registers, minutes or original acts of the public authority, titles, promissory notes, bills of exchange, commercial or banking instruments containing or creating an obligation, disposition or discharge, shall be punished as if he had misappropriated the same documents and according to the distinctions established in the first chapter of this title.

##### SECTION IV

DESTRUCTION OR DETERIORATION OF FOODSTUFFS, GOODS OR OTHER MOVABLE PROPERTY.

###### Art. 528

Any destruction, any damage to the movable property of another carried out by means of violence or threats, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 529. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 87, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> If the act was committed in a gathering or in a gang, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years.
The leaders and instigators shall be punished by imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years.

###### Art. 530. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 88, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> The destruction or damage to the movable property of another, carried out by means of violence or threats, in an inhabited house or its outbuildings, and with one of the circumstances provided for in article 471, shall be punished by imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years.
The penalty shall not be less than twelve years if the crime was committed in a gathering or in a gang.
The leaders and instigators shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years.

###### Art. 531. If the violence or threats by means of which the destruction or

damage was committed have caused an illness or bodily injury of the nature of those provided for by article 400, the guilty parties shall be punished by the penalty immediately above that which they shall have incurred under the terms of the two preceding articles.

###### Art. 532

Murder committed, either to facilitate the destruction or damage, or to ensure impunity therefor, shall be punished (by life imprisonment). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; In force: 11-08-1996>

Art. 532bis.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 41, 064; In force: 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by articles 528 to 532, the minimum of the penalties imposed by these articles may be doubled if they are correctional penalties, and increased by two years if they are imprisonment, when one of the motives for the offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person by reason of his or her alleged race, skin colour, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, [1 trade union conviction,]1 physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 117, 074; In force: 31-12-2009>

###### Art. 533

Whoever shall maliciously or fraudulently have altered or deteriorated goods or materials used for manufacturing, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The imprisonment shall be six months to three years and the fine fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], if the offence was committed by a person employed in the factory, workshop or commercial establishment. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force
: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 534

Whoever shall maliciously have removed, cut or destroyed the ties or obstacles which hold a boat, a wagon or a carriage, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two years.

Section IVbis. - Graffiti and degradation of immovable property. <inserted by L 2007-01-25/39, art. 3, In force: 02-03-2007>

Art. 534bis. <inserted by L 2007-01-25/39, art. 3, In force: 02-03-2007> § 1er. Whoever carries out graffiti without authorisation on movable or immovable property shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to six months and a fine of twenty-six euros to two hundred euros or by one of these penalties only.

###### § 2

The maximum imprisonment is increased to one year's imprisonment in case of recidivism for an offence referred to in paragraph one within five years from the date of a previous judgment imposing a conviction and having become final.

Art. 534ter. <inserted by L 2007-01-25/39, art. 3, In force: 02-03-2007> Whoever shall wilfully have degraded the immovable property of another shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to six months and a fine of twenty-six euros to two hundred euros or by one of these penalties only.

Art. 534quater.<inserted by L 2007-05-10/35, art. 42, In force: 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by articles 534bis and 534ter, the minimum of the penalties imposed by these articles may be doubled if they are correctional penalties, and increased by two years if they are imprisonment, when one of the motives for the offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person by reason of his or her alleged race, skin colour, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, [1 trade union conviction,]1 physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 118, 074; In force: 31-12-2009>

##### SECTION V

DESTRUCTION AND DEVASTATION OF CROPS, PLANTS, TREES, GRAFTS, GRAIN AND FODDER, DESTRUCTION OF AGRICULTURAL INSTRUMENTS.

###### Art. 535. Whoever shall maliciously have cut or devastated standing crops or plants

grown naturally or planted by hand shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 536

Whoever shall maliciously have ravaged a sown field, spread in a field seed of darnel or any other harmful herb or plant, broken or put out of service agricultural instruments, livestock pens or keepers' huts shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 537

Whoever shall maliciously have felled one or more trees, cut, mutilated or barked these trees so as to cause them to perish, or destroyed one or more grafts, shall be punished:
In respect of each tree, by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros]; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
In respect of each graft, by imprisonment of eight days to fifteen days and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to fifty [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
In no case shall the total penalty exceed three years for imprisonment, nor five hundred [euros] for the fine. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

##### SECTION VI. - DESTRUCTION OF ANIMALS

###### Art. 538

Whoever shall have poisoned horses or other draught or burden animals, horned cattle, sheep, goats or pigs, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 539

Whoever throws into a river, canal, stream, pond, fish pond or reservoir substances likely to destroy fish and with the intention of achieving that result, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 540

Those who, without necessity, have killed one of the animals mentioned in article 538, or have caused it serious injury, shall be punished as follows:
If the offence was committed in the buildings, enclosures and outbuildings, or on the lands of which the owner of the animal killed or injured was the owner, tenant, sharecropper or lessee, the penalty shall be imprisonment of one month to six months and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
If it was committed in places of which the offender was the owner, tenant, sharecropper or lessee, the penalty shall be imprisonment of eight days to two months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
If it was committed in any other place, the imprisonment shall be fifteen days to three months and the fine fifty [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 541

Whoever has, without necessity, killed a domestic animal other than those mentioned in article 538, or has caused it serious injury, in a place of which the person to whom that animal belongs is the owner, usufructuary, user, tenant, sharecropper or lessee, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
The same penalties shall be imposed if these acts were committed maliciously on a tamed animal or on an animal kept in captivity, in the places where they are kept, or on a domestic animal at the time it was being used for the service for which it was intended and in a place where its owner had the right to be.

###### Art. 542

In the cases provided for in the preceding articles, if there has been a breach of enclosure, the minimum penalty shall be increased in accordance with article 266.

##### SECTION VII. - PROVISIONS COMMON TO THE PRECEDING SECTIONS

###### Art. 543

If the acts provided for in sections V and VI of this chapter were committed either in hatred of a public official and by reason of his functions, or during the night, the minimum penalty shall be increased in accordance with article 266.

###### Art. 544. (Repealed)

<L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

##### SECTION VIII

DESTRUCTION OF ENCLOSURES, DISPLACEMENT OR REMOVAL OF BOUNDARY MARKERS AND CORNER POSTS.

###### Art. 545

Whoever has, in whole or in part, filled in ditches, cut or torn up live or dry hedges, destroyed rural or urban enclosures, of whatever materials they are made; displaced or removed boundary markers, corner posts or other trees planted or recognised to establish the boundaries between different properties, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 546

When the acts provided for by the preceding article were carried out with the intention of committing a usurpation of land, the penalty shall be imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of fifty [euros] to two thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

SECTION VIIIbis. [1 - Intrusion into port areas.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2016-05-20/04, art. 2, 117; In force: 12-06-2016>

###### Art. 546

/1. [1 Whoever has entered or intruded, without being authorised or permitted, into a port facility referred to in article 5, 6° and 7°, of the law of 5 February 2007 on maritime security, or into immovable or movable property located within the perimeter of the port within the meaning of the same law, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six euros to five hundred euros or by one of these penalties only.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2016-05-20/04, art. 3, 117; In force: 12-06-2016>

###### Art. 546

/2. [

###### §

1. The offence referred to in article 546/1 is punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six euros to one thousand

euros or by one of these penalties only:
1° when the activity concerned constitutes a habitual activity; 2° if it was committed during the night;
3° if it was committed by two or more persons;
4° if it was committed with fraudulent intent or with intent to cause harm; 5° if it was committed using violence or threats;
6° if the person entered or intruded into a critical infrastructure within the meaning of the law of 1 July 2011 on the security and protection of critical infrastructures.

###### § 2

The attempt to commit the offence referred to in paragraph 1 of this article is punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six euros to five hundred euros or by one of these penalties only.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2016-05-20/04, art. 4, 117; In force: 12-06-2016>

###### Art. 546

##### SECTION IX. - DESTRUCTION AND DAMAGE CAUSED BY FLOODING

###### Art. 547. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 89, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> Those who have maliciously or fraudulently flooded all or part of the workings of a mine shall be punished by imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years. If, according to the circumstances, the offender must have presumed that one or more persons were in the mine at the time of the flooding, he shall be sentenced to imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years.

###### Art. 548

The provision of article 518 shall be applicable to the act provided for by the preceding article.

###### Art. 549. Any person who has maliciously or fraudulently flooded

another person's property, or has transmitted water to it in a damaging manner, shall be sentenced to a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 550

Owners, tenants or any other persons operating mills, factories or ponds, who, by raising the overflow of their waters above the height determined by the competent authority, have flooded the roads or properties of others, shall be punished by a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
If any damage has resulted from these acts, the penalty shall be, in addition to the fine, imprisonment of eight days to one month.

TITLE IXbis. - Offences against the confidentiality, integrity and availability of computer systems and data stored, processed or transmitted by such systems. <inserted by L 2000-11-28/34, art. 6; In force: 13-02-2001>

Art. 550bis.<inserted by L 2000-11-28/34, art. 6; In force: 13-02-2001> § 1. Whoever, knowing that he is not authorised to do so, accesses a computer system or remains in it, is punished [1 by imprisonment of six months to two years]1 and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to twenty-five thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
If the offence referred to in paragraph 1 is committed with fraudulent intent, [1 the penalty of imprisonment is six months to three years]

###### 1. §

2. Anyone who, with fraudulent intent or with the aim to cause harm, exceeds their access authority to a computer system, is punished [1 by imprisonment from six months to three years]1 and by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to twenty-five thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force
: 01-01-2002>

###### § 3

Anyone who is in one of the situations referred to in §§ 1 and 2 and who: 1° either retrieves, in any manner whatsoever, the data stored, processed or transmitted by the computer system;
2° either makes any use of a computer system belonging to a third party or uses the computer system to access the computer system of a third party;
3° either causes any damage, even unintentionally, to the computer system or to the data that are stored processed or transmitted

by that system or to the computer system of a third party or to the data that are stored, processed or transmitted by that system;
is punished [1 by imprisonment from one to five years]1 and by a fine from twenty-six [euros] Belgian to fifty thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force : 01-01-2002>

###### § 4

The attempt to commit one of the offences referred to in §§ 1 and 2 is punished by the same penalties.

###### § 5

(Anyone who, unlawfully, possesses, produces, sells, obtains for use, imports, distributes or makes available in any other form, any device, including computer data, primarily designed or adapted to enable the commission of the offences provided for in §§ 1 to 4, is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and by a fine from twenty-six euros to one hundred thousand euros or by one of these penalties only.) <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 5, 059; In force : 22-09-2006>

###### § 6

Anyone who orders the commission of one of the offences referred to in §§ 1 to 5 or who incites it, is punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and by a fine from one hundred [euros] to two hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force : 01-01-2002>

###### § 7

Anyone who, knowing that data have been obtained by the commission of one of the offences referred to in §§ 1 to 3, holds them, discloses them to another person or divulges them, or makes any use of the data thus obtained, is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force : 01-01-2002>

###### § 8

The penalties provided for by §§ 1 to 7 are doubled if an offence against one of these provisions is committed within five years following the pronouncement of a conviction for one of these offences or for one of the offences referred to in articles 210bis, 259bis, 314bis, 504quater or 550ter.

(1)<L 2017-07-06/24, art. 214, 124; In force : 03-08-2017>

Art. 550ter.<inserted by L 2000-11-28/34, art. 6; In force : 13-02-2001> § 1. (Anyone who, knowing that they are not authorised to do so, directly or indirectly, introduces into a computer system, modifies or deletes data, or who modifies by any technological means the normal use of data in a computer system, is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and

by a fine from twenty-six euros to twenty-five thousand euros or by one of these penalties only.
If the offence referred to in paragraph 1 is committed with fraudulent intent or with the aim to cause harm, the imprisonment penalty is from six months to five years.) <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 6, 1°, 059; In force : 22-09-2006>
[1 The same penalty shall be applied when the offence referred to in paragraph 1 is committed against a computer system of a critical infrastructure as referred to in article 3, 4°, of the law of 1 July 2011 on the security and protection of critical infrastructures.]

###### §

2. Anyone who, following the commission of an offence referred to in § 1, causes damage to data in the computer system concerned or in any other computer system, is punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to seventy-five thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force : 01-01-2002>

###### § 3

Anyone who, following the commission of an offence referred to in § 1, prevents, totally or partially, the correct functioning of the computer system concerned or of any other computer system, is punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force : 01-01-2002>

###### § 4

(Anyone who, unlawfully, possesses, produces, sells, obtains for use, imports, distributes or makes available in any other form, a device including computer data, primarily designed or adapted to enable the commission of the offences provided for in §§ 1 to 3, when they know that such data may be used to cause damage to data or to prevent, totally or partially, the correct functioning of a computer system, is punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and by a fine from twenty-six euros to one hundred thousand euros or by one of these penalties only.) <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 6, 2°, 059; In force : 22-09-2006>

###### § 5

The penalties provided for by §§ 1 to 4 are doubled if an offence against one of these provisions is committed within five years following the pronouncement of a conviction for one of these offences or for one of the offences referred to in articles 210bis, 259bis, 314bis, 504quater or 550bis.
(§ 6. The attempt to commit the offence referred to in § 1 is punished by the same penalties.) <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 6, 3°, 059; In force : 22-09-2006>

(1)<L 2017-07-06/24, art. 215, 124; In force : 03-08-2017>
